from django.contrib import admin
from apps.academics.models import (
    AcademicSection,
    ClassRoutine,
    Homework,
    Subject,
    Syllabus,
    StudentAttendance,
)
from apps.academics.models import AcademicHoliday

@admin.register(AcademicSection)
class AcademicSectionAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ('class_level', 'group', 'section_name', 'session', 'in_charge_teacher', 'is_active')
    list_filter = ('class_level', 'group', 'session', 'is_active')
    search_fields = ('section_name', 'session', 'in_charge_teacher__first_name')

@admin.register(Subject)
class SubjectAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ('name', 'code', 'class_level', 'group', 'default_teacher', 'is_active')
    list_filter = ('class_level', 'group', 'is_active')
    search_fields = ('name', 'code')

@admin.register(ClassRoutine)
class ClassRoutineAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ('section', 'subject', 'teacher', 'day_of_week', 'start_time', 'end_time', 'room_number')
    list_filter = ('day_of_week', 'section__class_level', 'section__group')
    search_fields = ('subject__name', 'teacher__first_name', 'room_number')

@admin.register(Syllabus)
class SyllabusAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ('title', 'subject', 'session', 'uploaded_by', 'created_at')
    list_filter = ('session', 'subject__class_level')
    search_fields = ('title', 'subject__name')

@admin.register(Homework)
class HomeworkAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ('title', 'section', 'subject', 'teacher', 'due_date', 'created_at')
    list_filter = ('due_date', 'section__class_level', 'subject')
    search_fields = ('title', 'description', 'teacher__username')

@admin.register(StudentAttendance)
class StudentAttendanceAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ('date', 'student', 'section', 'status', 'recorded_by', 'created_at')
    list_filter = ('date', 'status', 'section__class_level', 'section__group')
    search_fields = ('student__student_unique_id', 'student__user__first_name', 'student__roll_number')
    date_hierarchy = 'date'


@admin.register(AcademicHoliday)
class AcademicHolidayAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ('title', 'holiday_type', 'start_date', 'end_date', 'is_attendance_locked')
    list_filter = ('holiday_type', 'is_attendance_locked')
    search_fields = ('title',)

from datetime import time
from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError
from django.db import models
from django.db.models import Q
from apps.common.models import BaseModel
from apps.users_auth.models import User
from django.utils import timezone

class AcademicSection(BaseModel):
    """
    à¦•à¦²à§‡à¦œà§‡à¦° à¦¶à§à¦°à§‡à¦£à¦¿, à¦¬à¦¿à¦­à¦¾à¦—, à¦¶à¦¾à¦–à¦¾ à¦“ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦¬à¦°à§à¦· à¦à¦¬à¦‚ à¦¶à§à¦°à§‡à¦£à¦¿ à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œà§‡à¦° à¦®à¦¡à§‡à¦²à¥¤
    """
    class ClassLevel(models.TextChoices):
        XI = 'XI', 'à¦à¦•à¦¾à¦¦à¦¶ à¦¶à§à¦°à§‡à¦£à¦¿ (1st Year)'
        XII = 'XII', 'à¦¦à§à¦¬à¦¾à¦¦à¦¶ à¦¶à§à¦°à§‡à¦£à¦¿ (2nd Year)'

    class GroupChoices(models.TextChoices):
        SCIENCE = 'SCI', 'à¦¬à¦¿à¦œà§à¦žà¦¾à¦¨ (Science)'
        HUMANITIES = 'HUM', 'à¦®à¦¾à¦¨à¦¬à¦¿à¦• (Humanities)'
        BUSINESS = 'BUS', 'à¦¬à§à¦¯à¦¬à¦¸à¦¾à§Ÿ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾ (Business Studies)'
        GENERAL = 'GEN', 'à¦¸à¦¾à¦§à¦¾à¦°à¦£ (General)'

    class_level = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=ClassLevel.choices, db_index=True)
    group = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=GroupChoices.choices, db_index=True)
    section_name = models.CharField(max_length=30, default='A', help_text="à¦¯à§‡à¦®à¦¨: A, B à¦¬à¦¾ à¦•, à¦–")
    session = models.CharField(max_length=20, db_index=True, help_text="à¦¯à§‡à¦®à¦¨: 2025-2026")
    
    in_charge_teacher = models.ForeignKey(
        User,
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name='incharge_sections',
        limit_choices_to={'role': User.Role.TEACHER},
    )
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Academic Section'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Academic Sections'
        unique_together = ('class_level', 'group', 'section_name', 'session')
        ordering = ['class_level', 'group', 'section_name']

    def clean(self):
        if self.in_charge_teacher and self.in_charge_teacher.role != User.Role.TEACHER:
            raise ValidationError("à¦¶à§à¦§à§à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦• à¦°à§‹à¦²à§‡à¦° à¦‡à¦‰à¦œà¦¾à¦°à¦•à§‡à¦‡ à¦¶à§à¦°à§‡à¦£à¦¿ à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¯à¦¾à¦¬à§‡à¥¤")

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.get_class_level_display()} | {self.get_group_display()} ({self.section_name}) [{self.session}]"


class Subject(BaseModel):
    """
    à¦•à¦²à§‡à¦œà§‡à¦° à¦ªà¦¾à¦ à§à¦¯ à¦¬à¦¿à¦·à§Ÿ (Subject) à¦®à¦¡à§‡à¦²à¥¤
    à¦‰à¦¦à¦¾à¦¹à¦°à¦£: Physics 1st Paper (Code: 174), Chemistry, Bangla à¦‡à¦¤à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¦à¦¿à¥¤
    """
    name = models.CharField(max_length=150)
    code = models.CharField(max_length=20, db_index=True, help_text="à¦¯à§‡à¦®à¦¨: 174, 175, 101")
    class_level = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=AcademicSection.ClassLevel.choices, db_index=True)
    group = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=AcademicSection.GroupChoices.choices, db_index=True)
    
    default_teacher = models.ForeignKey(
        User,
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name='assigned_subjects',
        limit_choices_to={'role': User.Role.TEACHER},
        help_text="à¦à¦‡ à¦¬à¦¿à¦·à§Ÿà§‡à¦° à¦ªà§à¦°à¦§à¦¾à¦¨ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦•"
    )
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Subject'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Subjects'
        unique_together = ('code', 'class_level')
        ordering = ['class_level', 'group', 'code']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.name} ({self.code}) - {self.get_class_level_display()}"


class ClassRoutine(BaseModel):
    """
    à¦•à§à¦²à¦¾à¦¸ à¦°à§à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦¨ à¦“ à¦ªà¦¿à¦°à¦¿à§Ÿà¦¡ à¦¶à¦¿à¦¡à¦¿à¦‰à¦² à¦®à¦¡à§‡à¦²à¥¤
    à¦…à¦Ÿà§‹à¦®à§‡à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦• à¦•à¦¨à¦«à§à¦²à¦¿à¦•à§à¦Ÿ à¦šà§‡à¦•à¦¿à¦‚ à¦¨à¦¿à¦¶à§à¦šà¦¿à¦¤ à¦•à¦°à¦¬à§‡ à¦¯à¦¾à¦¤à§‡ à¦à¦•à¦‡ à¦¸à¦®à§Ÿ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦•à§‡à¦° à¦¡à¦¾à¦¬à¦² à¦¶à¦¿à¦¡à¦¿à¦‰à¦² à¦¬à¦¾ à¦•à§à¦²à¦¾à¦¸à¦°à§à¦®à§‡à¦° à¦¸à¦‚à¦˜à¦¾à¦¤ à¦¨à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà¥¤
    """
    class DayOfWeek(models.IntegerChoices):
        SATURDAY = 0, 'à¦¶à¦¨à¦¿à¦¬à¦¾à¦° (Saturday)'
        SUNDAY = 1, 'à¦°à¦¬à¦¿à¦¬à¦¾à¦° (Sunday)'
        MONDAY = 2, 'à¦¸à§‹à¦®à¦¬à¦¾à¦° (Monday)'
        TUESDAY = 3, 'à¦®à¦™à§à¦—à¦²à¦¬à¦¾à¦° (Tuesday)'
        WEDNESDAY = 4, 'à¦¬à§à¦§à¦¬à¦¾à¦° (Wednesday)'
        THURSDAY = 5, 'à¦¬à§ƒà¦¹à¦¸à§à¦ªà¦¤à¦¿à¦¬à¦¾à¦° (Thursday)'
        FRIDAY = 6, 'à¦¶à§à¦•à§à¦°à¦¬à¦¾à¦° (Friday)'

    section = models.ForeignKey(AcademicSection, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='routines')
    subject = models.ForeignKey(Subject, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='routines')
    teacher = models.ForeignKey(
        User,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='routine_slots',
        limit_choices_to={'role': User.Role.TEACHER}
    )
    day_of_week = models.IntegerField(choices=DayOfWeek.choices, db_index=True)
    start_time = models.TimeField()
    end_time = models.TimeField()
    room_number = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True, default='')

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Class Routine Slot'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Class Routine Slots'
        ordering = ['day_of_week', 'start_time']

    def clean(self):
        if self.start_time and self.end_time and self.start_time >= self.end_time:
            raise ValidationError("à¦•à§à¦²à¦¾à¦¸ à¦¶à§à¦°à§à¦° à¦¸à¦®à§Ÿ à¦¶à§‡à¦· à¦¸à¦®à§Ÿà§‡à¦° à¦šà§‡à§Ÿà§‡ à¦†à¦—à§‡à¦° à¦¹à¦¤à§‡ à¦¹à¦¬à§‡à¥¤")

        # à§§. à¦à¦•à¦‡ à¦¸à§‡à¦•à¦¶à¦¨à§‡ à¦à¦•à¦‡ à¦¦à¦¿à¦¨à§‡ à¦•à§‹à¦¨à§‹ à¦ªà¦¿à¦°à¦¿à§Ÿà¦¡ à¦“à¦­à¦¾à¦°à¦²à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦ª à¦¹à¦šà§à¦›à§‡ à¦•à¦¿ à¦¨à¦¾ à¦šà§‡à¦•
        section_conflict = ClassRoutine.objects.filter(
            section=self.section,
            day_of_week=self.day_of_week,
            start_time__lt=self.end_time,
            end_time__gt=self.start_time
        ).exclude(pk=self.pk)

        if section_conflict.exists():
            raise ValidationError(f"à¦à¦‡ à¦¶à¦¾à¦–à¦¾à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ {self.get_day_of_week_display()} à¦¬à¦¾à¦°à§‡ à¦‰à¦•à§à¦¤ à¦¸à¦®à§Ÿà§‡ à¦‡à¦¤à¦¿à¦®à¦§à§à¦¯à§‡ à¦à¦•à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦ªà¦¿à¦°à¦¿à§Ÿà¦¡ à¦¨à¦¿à¦°à§à¦§à¦¾à¦°à¦¿à¦¤ à¦†à¦›à§‡à¥¤")

        # à§¨. à¦à¦•à¦‡ à¦¸à¦®à§Ÿà§‡ à¦à¦‡ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦•à§‡à¦° à¦…à¦¨à§à¦¯ à¦•à§‹à¦¨à§‹ à¦•à§à¦²à¦¾à¦¸à§‡ à¦ªà¦¿à¦°à¦¿à§Ÿà¦¡ à¦†à¦›à§‡ à¦•à¦¿ à¦¨à¦¾ à¦šà§‡à¦•
        teacher_conflict = ClassRoutine.objects.filter(
            teacher=self.teacher,
            day_of_week=self.day_of_week,
            start_time__lt=self.end_time,
            end_time__gt=self.start_time
        ).exclude(pk=self.pk)

        if teacher_conflict.exists():
            raise ValidationError(f"à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦• {self.teacher.get_full_name()} à¦à¦° {self.get_day_of_week_display()} à¦¬à¦¾à¦°à§‡ à¦‰à¦•à§à¦¤ à¦¸à¦®à§Ÿà§‡ à¦…à¦¨à§à¦¯ à¦•à§à¦²à¦¾à¦¸à§‡ à¦•à§à¦²à¦¾à¦¸ à¦°à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤")

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.section} | {self.subject.name} | {self.get_day_of_week_display()} ({self.start_time.strftime('%I:%M %p')}-{self.end_time.strftime('%I:%M %p')})"


class Syllabus(BaseModel):
    """
    à¦¸à¦¿à¦²à§‡à¦¬à¦¾à¦¸ à¦¬à¦¾ à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦°à§à¦® à¦ªà§à¦²à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¨à¦¾à¦° à¦«à¦¾à¦‡à¦² à¦†à¦ªà¦²à§‹à¦¡ à¦®à¦¡à§‡à¦²à¥¤
    """
    subject = models.ForeignKey(Subject, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='syllabi')
    session = models.CharField(max_length=20, db_index=True, help_text="à¦¯à§‡à¦®à¦¨: 2025-2026")
    title = models.CharField(max_length=200, help_text="à¦¯à§‡à¦®à¦¨: à¦à¦•à¦¾à¦¦à¦¶ à¦¬à¦¿à¦œà§à¦žà¦¾à¦¨ - à¦¬à¦¾à¦°à§à¦·à¦¿à¦• à¦ªà¦°à§€à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦° à¦ªà§‚à¦°à§à¦£à¦¾à¦™à§à¦— à¦¸à¦¿à¦²à§‡à¦¬à¦¾à¦¸")
    file = models.FileField(upload_to='syllabi/%Y/')
    uploaded_by = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Syllabus'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Syllabi'
        ordering = ['-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.title} - {self.subject.name} [{self.session}]"


class Homework(BaseModel):
    """
    à¦¦à§ˆà¦¨à¦¿à¦• à¦•à§à¦²à¦¾à¦¸à§‡à¦° à¦¹à§‹à¦®à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾à¦°à§à¦• à¦¬à¦¾ à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¸à¦¾à¦‡à¦¨à¦®à§‡à¦¨à§à¦Ÿ à¦®à¦¡à§‡à¦²à¥¤
    """
    section = models.ForeignKey(AcademicSection, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='homeworks')
    subject = models.ForeignKey(Subject, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='homeworks')
    teacher = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='assigned_homeworks')
    title = models.CharField(max_length=255)
    description = models.TextField()
    due_date = models.DateField(db_index=True)
    attachment = models.FileField(upload_to='homeworks/%Y/%m/', null=True, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Homework'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Homeworks'
        ordering = ['-due_date', '-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.title} - {self.subject.name} ({self.section})"


class StudentAttendance(BaseModel):
    """
    à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦¦à§‡à¦° à¦¦à§ˆà¦¨à¦¿à¦• à¦¹à¦¾à¦œà¦¿à¦°à¦¾ à¦®à¦¡à§‡à¦²à¥¤
    à¦¶à§à¦°à§‡à¦£à¦¿ à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¤à¦¿à¦¦à¦¿à¦¨ à¦ªà§à¦°à§‹ à¦¸à§‡à¦•à¦¶à¦¨à§‡à¦° à¦¬à¦¾à¦²à§à¦• à¦¹à¦¾à¦œà¦¿à¦°à¦¾ à¦¸à¦¾à¦¬à¦®à¦¿à¦Ÿ à¦•à¦°à¦¬à§‡à¦¨à¥¤
    """
    class AttendanceStatus(models.TextChoices):
        PRESENT = 'PRESENT', 'à¦‰à¦ªà¦¸à§à¦¥à¦¿à¦¤ (Present)'
        ABSENT = 'ABSENT', 'à¦…à¦¨à§à¦ªà¦¸à§à¦¥à¦¿à¦¤ (Absent)'
        LATE = 'LATE', 'à¦¦à§‡à¦°à¦¿à¦¤à§‡ à¦‰à¦ªà¦¸à§à¦¥à¦¿à¦¤à¦¿ (Late)'
        LEAVE = 'LEAVE', 'à¦›à§à¦Ÿà¦¿ (On Leave)'

    student = models.ForeignKey(
        'users_auth.StudentProfile',
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='attendances'
    )
    section = models.ForeignKey(
        AcademicSection,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='student_attendances'
    )
    date = models.DateField(default=timezone.localdate, db_index=True)
    status = models.CharField(
        max_length=10,
        choices=AttendanceStatus.choices,
        default=AttendanceStatus.PRESENT,
        db_index=True
    )
    remarks = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True)
    recorded_by = models.ForeignKey(
        User,
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name='recorded_attendances'
    )

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Student Attendance'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Student Attendances'
        unique_together = ('student', 'date')
        ordering = ['-date', 'student__roll_number']
        indexes = [
            # à¦¸à§‡à¦•à¦¶à¦¨ à¦“ à¦¤à¦¾à¦°à¦¿à¦– à¦¦à¦¿à§Ÿà§‡ à¦¦à§à¦°à§à¦¤ à¦¹à¦¾à¦œà¦¿à¦°à¦¾ à¦«à¦¿à¦²à§à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦°à¦¿à¦‚
            models.Index(fields=['section', 'date']),
            models.Index(fields=['student', 'status']),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"[{self.date}] {self.student.roll_number} - {self.student.user.get_full_name()} ({self.status})"


class AcademicHoliday(BaseModel):
    """
    à¦à¦•à¦¾à¦¡à§‡à¦®à¦¿à¦• à¦•à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦²à§‡à¦¨à§à¦¡à¦¾à¦°à§‡à¦° à¦›à§à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦° à¦¦à¦¿à¦¨ à¦“ à¦¶à§à¦•à§à¦°à¦¬à¦¾à¦° à¦¸à§‡à¦Ÿà¦†à¦ªà¥¤
    """
    class HolidayType(models.TextChoices):
        GOVERNMENT = 'GOVERNMENT', 'à¦¸à¦°à¦•à¦¾à¦°à¦¿ à¦›à§à¦Ÿà¦¿ (Government Holiday)'
        WEEKEND = 'WEEKEND', 'à¦¸à¦¾à¦ªà§à¦¤à¦¾à¦¹à¦¿à¦• à¦›à§à¦Ÿà¦¿/à¦¶à§à¦•à§à¦°à¦¬à¦¾à¦° (Weekend)'
        RELIGIOUS = 'RELIGIOUS', 'à¦§à¦°à§à¦®à§€à§Ÿ à¦‰à§Žà¦¸à¦¬ (Religious Festival)'
        COLLEGE = 'COLLEGE', 'à¦•à¦²à§‡à¦œ à¦¨à¦¿à¦œà¦¸à§à¦¬ à¦›à§à¦Ÿà¦¿ (College Authority)'

    title = models.CharField(max_length=150, help_text="à¦¯à§‡à¦®à¦¨: à¦®à¦¹à¦¾à¦¨ à¦¬à¦¿à¦œà§Ÿ à¦¦à¦¿à¦¬à¦¸, à¦ªà¦¬à¦¿à¦¤à§à¦° à¦ˆà¦¦à§à¦² à¦«à¦¿à¦¤à¦°")
    holiday_type = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=HolidayType.choices, default=HolidayType.GOVERNMENT)
    start_date = models.DateField(db_index=True)
    end_date = models.DateField(db_index=True)
    is_attendance_locked = models.BooleanField(default=True, help_text="à¦à¦‡ à¦¦à¦¿à¦¨à§‡ à¦•à§‹à¦¨à§‹ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦• à¦¬à¦¾ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€ à¦¹à¦¾à¦œà¦¿à¦°à¦¾ à¦¦à¦¿à¦¤à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à¦¬à§‡ à¦¨à¦¾")

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Academic Holiday'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Academic Holidays'
        ordering = ['start_date']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.title} ({self.start_date} to {self.end_date})"

from django.db import transaction
from django.utils import timezone
from rest_framework import serializers

from apps.academics.models import (
    AcademicHoliday,
    AcademicSection,
    ClassRoutine,
    Homework,
    StudentAttendance,
    Subject,
    Syllabus,
)
from apps.finance.models import TransportVehicle
from apps.users_auth.models import StudentProfile, User
from apps.common.validators import validate_upload


class AcademicSectionSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    class_level_display = serializers.CharField(source='get_class_level_display', read_only=True)
    group_display = serializers.CharField(source='get_group_display', read_only=True)
    in_charge_teacher_name = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    total_students = serializers.SerializerMethodField()

    class Meta:
        model = AcademicSection
        fields = [
            'id',
            'class_level',
            'class_level_display',
            'group',
            'group_display',
            'section_name',
            'session',
            'in_charge_teacher',
            'in_charge_teacher_name',
            'total_students',
            'is_active',
            'created_at',
        ]
        read_only_fields = ['id', 'created_at']

    def get_in_charge_teacher_name(self, obj) -> str | None:
        if obj.in_charge_teacher:
            full_name = obj.in_charge_teacher.get_full_name()
            return full_name if full_name else obj.in_charge_teacher.username
        return None

    def get_total_students(self, obj) -> int:
        if hasattr(obj, 'student_count'):
            return obj.student_count
        return obj.students.count()

    def validate_in_charge_teacher(self, value):
        if value and value.role != User.Role.TEACHER:
            raise serializers.ValidationError("à¦¶à§à¦§à§à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° à¦à¦•à¦œà¦¨ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦•à¦•à§‡à¦‡ (TEACHER) à¦¶à§à¦°à§‡à¦£à¦¿ à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œ à¦¹à¦¿à¦¸à§‡à¦¬à§‡ à¦¨à¦¿à¦¯à§à¦•à§à¦¤ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¯à¦¾à¦¬à§‡à¥¤")
        return value


class StudentDetailSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    """
    à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§à¦¡à§‡à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà§‡à¦° à¦¸à¦®à§à¦ªà§‚à¦°à§à¦£ à¦¤à¦¥à§à¦¯ à¦¦à§‡à¦–à¦¾à¦¨à§‹à¦° à¦à¦¬à¦‚ à¦†à¦ªà¦¡à§‡à¦Ÿ à¦•à¦°à¦¾à¦° à¦¸à¦¿à¦°à¦¿à§Ÿà¦¾à¦²à¦¾à¦‡à¦œà¦¾à¦°à¥¤
    """
    user_id = serializers.UUIDField(source='user.id', read_only=True)
    username = serializers.CharField(source='user.username', read_only=True)
    first_name = serializers.CharField(source='user.first_name')
    last_name = serializers.CharField(source='user.last_name', required=False, allow_blank=True)
    full_name = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    phone = serializers.CharField(source='user.phone', required=False, allow_null=True, allow_blank=True)
    email = serializers.EmailField(source='user.email', required=False, allow_blank=True)
    is_active = serializers.BooleanField(source='user.is_active', required=False)
    section_name_display = serializers.CharField(source='section.__str__', read_only=True)
    class_level = serializers.CharField(source='section.class_level', read_only=True, default='')
    class_level_display = serializers.CharField(source='section.get_class_level_display', read_only=True, default='')
    group = serializers.CharField(source='section.group', read_only=True, default='')
    group_display = serializers.CharField(source='section.get_group_display', read_only=True, default='')
    gender_display = serializers.CharField(source='get_gender_display', read_only=True)
    assigned_vehicle_number = serializers.CharField(source='assigned_vehicle.vehicle_number', read_only=True, default=None)
    created_by_name = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    temporary_pin = serializers.SerializerMethodField()

    class Meta:
        model = StudentProfile
        fields = [
            'id',
            'user_id',
            'student_unique_id',
            'username',
            'first_name',
            'last_name',
            'full_name',
            'gender',
            'gender_display',
            'phone',
            'email',
            'section',
            'section_name_display',
            'class_level',
            'class_level_display',
            'group',
            'group_display',
            'roll_number',
            'session',
            'ssc_roll',
            'passing_year',
            'hsc_registration_no',
            'father_name',
            'mother_name',
            'guardian_phone',
            'guardian_whatsapp_number',
            'student_whatsapp_number',
            'present_address',
            'monthly_tuition_fee',
            'uses_transport',
            'assigned_vehicle',
            'assigned_vehicle_number',
            'monthly_transport_fee',
            'is_active',
            'created_by',
            'created_by_name',
            'created_at',
            'temporary_pin',
        ]
        read_only_fields = ['id', 'user_id', 'student_unique_id', 'username', 'session', 'created_by', 'created_at']

    def get_full_name(self, obj) -> str:
        full_name = f"{obj.user.first_name} {obj.user.last_name}".strip()
        return full_name if full_name else obj.user.username

    def get_created_by_name(self, obj) -> str | None:
        if obj.created_by:
            return obj.created_by.get_full_name() or obj.created_by.username
        return None

    def get_temporary_pin(self, obj) -> str:
        request = self.context.get('request')
        if request and request.user.is_authenticated and request.user.role == User.Role.PRINCIPAL:
            return obj.get_temporary_pin()
        return ''

    @transaction.atomic
    def update(self, instance, validated_data):
        user_data = validated_data.pop('user', {})
        user = instance.user
        for attr, value in user_data.items():
            setattr(user, attr, value)
        user.save()

        return super().update(instance, validated_data)


class StudentEnrollmentSerializer(serializers.Serializer):
    """
    à¦¶à§à¦°à§‡à¦£à¦¿ à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œ à¦¬à¦¾ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦¸à¦¿à¦ªà¦¾à¦² à¦•à¦°à§à¦¤à§ƒà¦• à¦¨à¦¤à§à¦¨ à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§à¦¡à§‡à¦¨à§à¦Ÿ à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦•à¦¾à¦‰à¦¨à§à¦Ÿ à¦¤à§ˆà¦°à¦¿à¦° à¦¸à¦¿à¦°à¦¿à§Ÿà¦¾à¦²à¦¾à¦‡à¦œà¦¾à¦°à¥¤
    """
    username = serializers.CharField(
        max_length=150, 
        required=False, 
        allow_blank=True,
        help_text="à¦–à¦¾à¦²à¦¿ à¦°à¦¾à¦–à¦²à§‡ à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§à¦¡à§‡à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà§‡à¦° Unique ID à¦¸à§à¦¬à§Ÿà¦‚à¦•à§à¦°à¦¿à§Ÿà¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ Username à¦¹à¦¿à¦¸à§‡à¦¬à§‡ à¦¸à§‡à¦Ÿ à¦¹à¦¬à§‡à¥¤"
    )
    password = serializers.CharField(write_only=True, min_length=6)
    first_name = serializers.CharField(max_length=150)
    last_name = serializers.CharField(max_length=150, required=False, allow_blank=True)
    gender = serializers.ChoiceField(choices=StudentProfile.GenderChoices.choices, default=StudentProfile.GenderChoices.MALE)
    phone = serializers.CharField(max_length=20, required=False, allow_null=True, allow_blank=True)
    email = serializers.EmailField(required=False, allow_blank=True)

    section = serializers.PrimaryKeyRelatedField(queryset=AcademicSection.objects.filter(is_active=True))
    roll_number = serializers.IntegerField(min_value=1)
    ssc_roll = serializers.CharField(max_length=30, required=False, allow_blank=True)
    passing_year = serializers.CharField(max_length=4, required=False, allow_blank=True)
    hsc_registration_no = serializers.CharField(max_length=50, required=False, allow_blank=True)

    father_name = serializers.CharField(max_length=150)
    mother_name = serializers.CharField(max_length=150)
    guardian_phone = serializers.CharField(max_length=20)
    guardian_whatsapp_number = serializers.CharField(max_length=20, required=False, allow_blank=True)
    student_whatsapp_number = serializers.CharField(max_length=20, required=False, allow_blank=True)
    present_address = serializers.CharField(required=False, allow_blank=True)

    monthly_tuition_fee = serializers.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    uses_transport = serializers.BooleanField(default=False)
    assigned_vehicle = serializers.PrimaryKeyRelatedField(queryset=TransportVehicle.objects.filter(is_active=True), required=False, allow_null=True)
    monthly_transport_fee = serializers.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)

    def validate(self, attrs):
        section = attrs['section']
        roll_number = attrs['roll_number']
        phone = attrs.get('phone') or None
        attrs['phone'] = phone

        if StudentProfile.objects.filter(section=section, roll_number=roll_number).exists():
            raise serializers.ValidationError({
                "roll_number": f"'{section}' à¦¶à¦¾à¦–à¦¾à§Ÿ à¦°à§‹à¦² à¦¨à¦®à§à¦¬à¦° {roll_number} à¦¦à¦¿à§Ÿà§‡ à¦‡à¦¤à¦¿à¦®à¦§à§à¦¯à§‡ à¦à¦•à¦œà¦¨ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€ à¦­à¦°à§à¦¤à¦¿ à¦°à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤"
            })

        if attrs.get('ssc_roll') and attrs.get('passing_year') and StudentProfile.objects.filter(
            ssc_roll=attrs['ssc_roll'],
            passing_year=attrs['passing_year'],
        ).exists():
            raise serializers.ValidationError({
                "ssc_roll": "à¦à¦‡ SSC Roll à¦à¦¬à¦‚ Passing Year à¦‡à¦¤à¦¿à¦®à¦§à§à¦¯à§‡ à¦¬à§à¦¯à¦¬à¦¹à§ƒà¦¤ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤"
            })

        year_prefix = section.session.split('-')[0].strip()
        generated_unique_id = (
            f"{year_prefix}-{section.class_level}-"
            f"{section.group}-{section.section_name}-"
            f"{int(roll_number):04d}"
        ).upper()

        username = attrs.get('username', '').strip() or generated_unique_id
        if User.objects.filter(username=username).exists():
            raise serializers.ValidationError({
                "username": f"'{username}' à¦‡à¦‰à¦œà¦¾à¦°à¦¨à§‡à¦®à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦‡à¦¤à¦¿à¦®à¦§à§à¦¯à§‡ à¦¬à§à¦¯à¦¬à¦¹à§ƒà¦¤ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤"
            })

        if phone and User.objects.filter(phone=phone).exists():
            raise serializers.ValidationError({
                "phone": "à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦° à¦à¦‡ à¦¬à§à¦¯à¦•à§à¦¤à¦¿à¦—à¦¤ à¦«à§‹à¦¨ à¦¨à¦®à§à¦¬à¦°à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦‡à¦¤à¦¿à¦®à¦§à§à¦¯à§‡ à¦…à¦¨à§à¦¯ à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦•à¦¾à¦‰à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà§‡ à¦¬à§à¦¯à¦¬à¦¹à§ƒà¦¤ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤"
            })

        attrs['username'] = username
        attrs['generated_unique_id'] = generated_unique_id
        return attrs

    @transaction.atomic
    def create(self, validated_data):
        request = self.context.get('request')
        creator = request.user if request else None
        section = validated_data['section']

        user = User.objects.create_user(
            username=validated_data['username'],
            password=validated_data['password'],
            first_name=validated_data['first_name'],
            last_name=validated_data.get('last_name', ''),
            email=validated_data.get('email', ''),
            phone=validated_data.get('phone'),
            role=User.Role.STUDENT,
        )

        student_profile = StudentProfile.objects.create(
            user=user,
            student_unique_id=validated_data['generated_unique_id'],
            gender=validated_data.get('gender', StudentProfile.GenderChoices.MALE),
            section=section,
            roll_number=validated_data['roll_number'],
            session=section.session,
            ssc_roll=validated_data.get('ssc_roll', ''),
            passing_year=validated_data.get('passing_year', ''),
            hsc_registration_no=validated_data.get('hsc_registration_no', ''),
            father_name=validated_data['father_name'],
            mother_name=validated_data['mother_name'],
            guardian_phone=validated_data['guardian_phone'],
            guardian_whatsapp_number=validated_data.get('guardian_whatsapp_number', ''),
            student_whatsapp_number=validated_data.get('student_whatsapp_number', ''),
            present_address=validated_data.get('present_address', ''),
            monthly_tuition_fee=validated_data.get('monthly_tuition_fee', 0.00),
            uses_transport=validated_data.get('uses_transport', False),
            assigned_vehicle=validated_data.get('assigned_vehicle', None),
            monthly_transport_fee=validated_data.get('monthly_transport_fee', 0.00),
            created_by=creator,
        )
        return student_profile

class BulkAttendanceCreateSerializer(serializers.Serializer):
    section_id = serializers.UUIDField()
    date = serializers.DateField(default=timezone.localdate)
    attendance_records = SingleAttendanceItemSerializer(many=True)

    def validate_section_id(self, value):
        if not AcademicSection.objects.filter(id=value, is_active=True).exists():
            raise serializers.ValidationError("à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¦à¦¤à§à¦¤ à¦¶à§à¦°à§‡à¦£à¦¿ à¦¬à¦¾ à¦¶à¦¾à¦–à¦¾à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦–à§à¦à¦œà§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾ à¦¯à¦¾à§Ÿà¦¨à¦¿à¥¤")
        return value

    def validate(self, attrs):
        section_id = attrs['section_id']
        records = attrs.get('attendance_records', [])
        
        if not records:
            raise serializers.ValidationError({"attendance_records": "à¦•à¦®à¦ªà¦•à§à¦·à§‡ à¦à¦•à¦œà¦¨ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦° à¦¹à¦¾à¦œà¦¿à¦°à¦¾ à¦¡à§‡à¦Ÿà¦¾ à¦ªà¦¾à¦ à¦¾à¦¨à§‹ à¦†à¦¬à¦¶à§à¦¯à¦•à¥¤"})

        student_ids = [item['student_id'] for item in records]
        if len(student_ids) != len(set(student_ids)):
            raise serializers.ValidationError({"attendance_records": "à¦à¦•à¦‡ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦° à¦à¦•à¦¾à¦§à¦¿à¦• à¦à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà§à¦°à¦¿ à¦¤à¦¾à¦²à¦¿à¦•à¦¾à§Ÿ à¦°à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤"})

        valid_student_count = StudentProfile.objects.filter(
            id__in=student_ids,
            section_id=section_id
        ).count()

        if valid_student_count != len(student_ids):
            raise serializers.ValidationError("à¦¤à¦¾à¦²à¦¿à¦•à¦¾à§Ÿ à¦à¦®à¦¨ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€ à¦°à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡ à¦¯à¦¾à¦°à¦¾ à¦à¦‡ à¦¶à¦¾à¦–à¦¾à¦° à¦…à¦¨à§à¦¤à¦°à§à¦­à§à¦•à§à¦¤ à¦¨à§Ÿà¥¤")

        return attrs

    @transaction.atomic
    def save_bulk(self, recorder_user):
        section_id = self.validated_data['section_id']
        attendance_date = self.validated_data['date']
        records = self.validated_data['attendance_records']

        section = AcademicSection.objects.get(id=section_id)
        saved_instances = []

        for item in records:
            attendance, _ = StudentAttendance.objects.update_or_create(
                student_id=item['student_id'],
                date=attendance_date,
                defaults={
                    'section': section,
                    'status': item['status'],
                    'remarks': item.get('remarks', ''),
                    'recorded_by': recorder_user,
                }
            )
            saved_instances.append(attendance)

        return saved_instances

from django.test import TestCase
from rest_framework.test import APIRequestFactory, force_authenticate

from apps.academics.models import AcademicSection
from apps.academics.views import StudentViewSet
from apps.users_auth.models import StudentProfile, User


class StudentProfileSecurityTests(TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.teacher = User.objects.create_user(
            username='teacher',
            password='password',
            role=User.Role.TEACHER,
        )
        self.other_teacher = User.objects.create_user(
            username='other-teacher',
            password='password',
            role=User.Role.TEACHER,
        )
        self.section = AcademicSection.objects.create(
            class_level=AcademicSection.ClassLevel.XI,
            group=AcademicSection.GroupChoices.SCIENCE,
            section_name='A',
            session='2026-2027',
            in_charge_teacher=self.teacher,
        )
        self.other_section = AcademicSection.objects.create(
            class_level=AcademicSection.ClassLevel.XI,
            group=AcademicSection.GroupChoices.SCIENCE,
            section_name='B',
            session='2026-2027',
            in_charge_teacher=self.other_teacher,
        )
        self.student_user = User.objects.create_user(
            username='student',
            password='password',
            role=User.Role.STUDENT,
        )
        self.other_student_user = User.objects.create_user(
            username='other-student',
            password='password',
            role=User.Role.STUDENT,
        )
        self.student = StudentProfile.objects.create(
            user=self.student_user,
            section=self.section,
            roll_number=1,
            session='2026-2027',
            father_name='Father',
            mother_name='Mother',
            guardian_phone='01700000000',
        )
        self.other_student = StudentProfile.objects.create(
            user=self.other_student_user,
            section=self.other_section,
            roll_number=1,
            session='2026-2027',
            father_name='Other Father',
            mother_name='Other Mother',
            guardian_phone='01700000001',
        )
        self.factory = APIRequestFactory()

    def test_teacher_list_is_limited_to_incharge_section(self):
        request = self.factory.get('/api/v1/academics/students/')
        force_authenticate(request, user=self.teacher)
        response = StudentViewSet.as_view({'get': 'list'})(request)

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        returned_ids = {item['id'] for item in response.data['results']}
        self.assertIn(str(self.student.id), returned_ids)
        self.assertNotIn(str(self.other_student.id), returned_ids)

router.register(r'holidays', AcademicHolidayViewSet, basename='holiday')

    path('', include(router.urls)),
]

class AcademicSectionViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    """
    à¦¶à§à¦°à§‡à¦£à¦¿/à¦¶à¦¾à¦–à¦¾ à¦ªà¦°à¦¿à¦šà¦¾à¦²à¦¨à¦¾ à¦à¦¬à¦‚ à¦¶à§à¦°à§‡à¦£à¦¿ à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œ à¦¨à¦¿à¦¯à§à¦•à§à¦¤ à¦•à¦°à¦¾à¦° ViewSetà¥¤
    """
    queryset = AcademicSection.objects.select_related('in_charge_teacher').annotate(
        student_count=Count('students')
    ).all()
    serializer_class = AcademicSectionSerializer

    def get_permissions(self):
        if self.action in ['list', 'retrieve']:
            return [IsAuthenticated()]
        return [IsPrincipal()]

    @action(detail=True, methods=['patch'], url_path='assign-incharge')
    def assign_incharge(self, request, pk=None):
        section = self.get_object()
        teacher_id = request.data.get('teacher_id')

        if not teacher_id:
            section.in_charge_teacher = None
            section.save()
            return Response({
                "success": True,
                "message": "à¦à¦‡ à¦¶à§à¦°à§‡à¦£à¦¿ à¦¥à§‡à¦•à§‡ à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œ à¦…à¦¬à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¹à¦¤à¦¿ à¦¦à§‡à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤",
                "data": AcademicSectionSerializer(section).data
            })

        try:
            teacher = User.objects.get(id=teacher_id, role=User.Role.TEACHER, is_active=True)
        except User.DoesNotExist:
            return Response(
                {"success": False, "message": "à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¦à¦¤à§à¦¤ à¦†à¦‡à¦¡à¦¿à¦¤à§‡ à¦•à§‹à¦¨à§‹ à¦¸à¦•à§à¦°à¦¿à§Ÿ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦• à¦ªà¦¾à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾ à¦¯à¦¾à§Ÿà¦¨à¦¿à¥¤"},
                status=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND
            )

        section.in_charge_teacher = teacher
        section.save()

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": f"{teacher.get_full_name()} à¦•à§‡ à¦¸à¦«à¦²à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ '{section}' à¦à¦° à¦¶à§à¦°à§‡à¦£à¦¿ à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œ à¦¨à¦¿à¦¯à§à¦•à§à¦¤ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤",
            "data": AcademicSectionSerializer(section).data
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class StudentViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    """
    à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§à¦¡à§‡à¦¨à§à¦Ÿ à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦•à¦¾à¦‰à¦¨à§à¦Ÿ à¦¤à§ˆà¦°à¦¿, à¦¤à¦¾à¦²à¦¿à¦•à¦¾ à¦¦à§‡à¦–à¦¾ à¦à¦¬à¦‚ à¦ªà§à¦°à§‹à¦«à¦¾à¦‡à¦²/à¦«à¦¿ à¦¸à§‡à¦Ÿà¦†à¦ª à¦†à¦ªà¦¡à§‡à¦Ÿ à¦•à¦°à¦¾à¦° ViewSetà¥¤
    - à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦¸à¦¿à¦ªà¦¾à¦²: à¦¸à¦¬ à¦¶à¦¾à¦–à¦¾à¦° à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§à¦¡à§‡à¦¨à§à¦Ÿ à¦¤à§ˆà¦°à¦¿, à¦¦à§‡à¦–à¦¾, à¦à¦¡à¦¿à¦Ÿ à¦“ à¦¡à¦¿à¦²à¦¿à¦Ÿ à¦•à¦°à¦¤à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à¦¬à§‡à¦¨à¥¤
    - à¦¶à§à¦°à§‡à¦£à¦¿ à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œ: à¦¶à§à¦§à§à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° à¦¨à¦¿à¦œà§‡à¦° à¦¦à¦¾à§Ÿà¦¿à¦¤à§à¦¬à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¾à¦ªà§à¦¤ à¦¶à¦¾à¦–à¦¾à§Ÿ à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§à¦¡à§‡à¦¨à§à¦Ÿ à¦¤à§ˆà¦°à¦¿ à¦“ à¦à¦¡à¦¿à¦Ÿ à¦•à¦°à¦¤à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à¦¬à§‡à¦¨à¥¤
    """
    queryset = StudentProfile.objects.select_related('user', 'section', 'created_by').all()

    def get_permissions(self):
        if self.action == 'destroy':
            return [IsPrincipal()]
        if self.action in ['create', 'update', 'partial_update']:
            return [IsTeacherOrPrincipal()]
        role = getattr(self.request.user, 'role', None)
        if role == User.Role.STUDENT:
            return [IsAuthenticated()]
        if role == User.Role.TEACHER:
            return [IsTeacherOrPrincipal()]
        return [IsPrincipal()]

    def get_serializer_class(self):
        if self.action == 'create':
            return StudentEnrollmentSerializer
        return StudentDetailSerializer

    def get_queryset(self):
        user = self.request.user
        queryset = super().get_queryset()

        # à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§à¦¡à§‡à¦¨à§à¦Ÿ à¦²à¦—à¦‡à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦²à§‡ à¦¸à§‡ à¦¶à§à¦§à§ à¦¨à¦¿à¦œà§‡à¦° à¦ªà§à¦°à§‹à¦«à¦¾à¦‡à¦²à¦‡ à¦¦à§‡à¦–à¦¤à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦¬à§‡
        if user.role == User.Role.STUDENT:
            return queryset.filter(user=user)

        if user.role == User.Role.TEACHER:
            queryset = queryset.filter(section__in_charge_teacher=user)

        # à¦•à§à§Ÿà§‡à¦°à¦¿ à¦ªà§à¦¯à¦¾à¦°à¦¾à¦®à¦¿à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦° à¦¦à¦¿à§Ÿà§‡ à¦«à¦¿à¦²à§à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦° (?section=<uuid>&uses_transport=true&my_incharge_only=true)
        section_id = self.request.query_params.get('section')
        if section_id:
            queryset = queryset.filter(section_id=section_id)

        uses_transport = self.request.query_params.get('uses_transport')
        if uses_transport is not None:
            is_transport = uses_transport.lower() in ['true', '1']
            queryset = queryset.filter(uses_transport=is_transport)

        my_incharge_only = self.request.query_params.get('my_incharge_only')
        if my_incharge_only and my_incharge_only.lower() in ['true', '1'] and user.role == User.Role.TEACHER:
            queryset = queryset.filter(section__in_charge_teacher=user)

        return queryset

    def _verify_incharge_permission(self, user, section: AcademicSection):
        """
        à¦šà§‡à¦• à¦•à¦°à¦¬à§‡ à¦‡à¦‰à¦œà¦¾à¦° à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦¸à¦¿à¦ªà¦¾à¦² à¦•à¦¿ à¦¨à¦¾ à¦…à¦¥à¦¬à¦¾ à¦“à¦‡ à¦¨à¦¿à¦°à§à¦¦à¦¿à¦·à§à¦Ÿ à¦¶à¦¾à¦–à¦¾à¦° à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œ à¦•à¦¿ à¦¨à¦¾à¥¤
        """
        if user.role == User.Role.PRINCIPAL or user.is_superuser:
            return
        if section.in_charge_teacher_id != user.id:
            raise PermissionDenied(
                f"à¦†à¦ªà¦¨à¦¿ '{section}' à¦¶à¦¾à¦–à¦¾à¦° à¦¶à§à¦°à§‡à¦£à¦¿ à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œ à¦¨à¦¨à¥¤ à¦¶à§à¦§à§à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° à¦“à¦‡ à¦¶à¦¾à¦–à¦¾à¦° à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œ à¦¬à¦¾ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦¸à¦¿à¦ªà¦¾à¦² à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§à¦¡à§‡à¦¨à§à¦Ÿ à¦ªà¦°à¦¿à¦šà¦¾à¦²à¦¨à¦¾ à¦•à¦°à¦¤à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à¦¬à§‡à¦¨à¥¤"
            )

    def create(self, request, *args, **kwargs):
        serializer = self.get_serializer(data=request.data, context={'request': request})
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        section = serializer.validated_data['section']
        self._verify_incharge_permission(request.user, section)

        student_profile = serializer.save()
        return Response(
            {
                "success": True,
                "message": "à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦° à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦•à¦¾à¦‰à¦¨à§à¦Ÿ à¦“ à¦ªà§à¦°à§‹à¦«à¦¾à¦‡à¦² à¦¸à¦«à¦²à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ à¦¤à§ˆà¦°à¦¿ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤",
                "data": StudentDetailSerializer(student_profile, context={'request': request}).data,
            },
            status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
        )

    @action(detail=False, methods=['post'], permission_classes=[IsPrincipal], url_path='bulk-import')
    def bulk_import(self, request):
        upload = request.FILES.get('file')
        if not upload or not upload.name.lower().endswith(('.xlsx', '.xlsm')):
            return Response(
                {"success": False, "message": "à¦à¦•à¦Ÿà¦¿ .xlsx à¦…à¦¥à¦¬à¦¾ .xlsm Excel file upload à¦•à¦°à§à¦¨à¥¤"},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )

        try:
            workbook = load_workbook(upload, read_only=True, data_only=True)
            worksheet = workbook.active
            rows = list(worksheet.values)
        except Exception as exc:
            return Response(
                {"success": False, "message": f"Excel file à¦ªà§œà¦¾ à¦¯à¦¾à§Ÿà¦¨à¦¿: {exc}"},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )

        if not rows or not rows[0]:
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "Excel sheet à¦–à¦¾à¦²à¦¿à¥¤"}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        def normalize(value):
            return re.sub(r'[^a-z0-9]', '_', str(value or '').strip().lower()).strip('_')

        aliases = {
            'name': ('name', 'full_name', 'student_name', 'first_name'),
            'first_name': ('first_name',),
            'last_name': ('last_name',),
            'gender': ('gender',),
            'section': ('section', 'section_id'),
            'class_level': ('class_level', 'class'),
            'group': ('group',),
            'section_name': ('section_name', 'branch'),
            'session': ('session',),
            'roll_number': ('roll_number', 'roll', 'class_roll'),
            'ssc_roll': ('ssc_roll', 'ssc_roll_no'),
            'passing_year': ('passing_year', 'pass_year'),
            'hsc_registration_no': ('hsc_registration_no', 'hsc_registration'),
            'father_name': ('father_name', 'father'),
            'mother_name': ('mother_name', 'mother'),
            'guardian_phone': ('guardian_phone', 'guardian_mobile'),
            'guardian_whatsapp_number': ('guardian_whatsapp_number', 'guardian_whatsapp'),
            'student_whatsapp_number': ('student_whatsapp_number', 'student_whatsapp'),
            'present_address': ('present_address', 'address'),
            'monthly_tuition_fee': ('monthly_tuition_fee', 'tuition_fee', 'monthly_fee'),
            'uses_transport': ('uses_transport', 'transport', 'use_transport', 'has_transport', 'bus'),
            'monthly_transport_fee': ('monthly_transport_fee', 'transport_fee', 'bus_fee'),
            'assigned_vehicle': ('assigned_vehicle', 'vehicle', 'vehicle_number', 'bus_no'),
        }
        header_map = {normalize(value): index for index, value in enumerate(rows[0])}

        def cell(row, field):
            for alias in aliases[field]:
                if alias in header_map:
                    return row[header_map[alias]]
            return ''

        def split_name(row):
            full_name = str(cell(row, 'name') or '').strip()
            first_name = str(cell(row, 'first_name') or '').strip()
            last_name = str(cell(row, 'last_name') or '').strip()
            if not first_name and full_name:
                parts = full_name.split(maxsplit=1)
                first_name = parts[0]
                last_name = parts[1] if len(parts) > 1 else ''
            return first_name, last_name

        prepared = []
        errors = []
        for row_number, row in enumerate(rows[1:], start=2):
            if not any(value not in (None, '') for value in row):
                continue
            first_name, last_name = split_name(row)
            section_value = str(cell(row, 'section') or '').strip()
            section = None
            if section_value:
                try:
                    import uuid
                    uuid.UUID(section_value)
                    section = AcademicSection.objects.filter(id=section_value, is_active=True).first()
                except Exception:
                    section = None

            if not section:
                section = AcademicSection.objects.filter(
                    class_level=str(cell(row, 'class_level') or '').strip().upper(),
                    group=str(cell(row, 'group') or '').strip().upper(),
                    section_name=str(cell(row, 'section_name') or '').strip(),
                    session=str(cell(row, 'session') or '').strip(),
                    is_active=True,
                ).first()

            if not section:
                errors.append({
                    "row": row_number,
                    "error": "section ID à¦…à¦¥à¦¬à¦¾ class_level, group, section_name à¦“ session-à¦à¦° à¦¸à¦ à¦¿à¦• combination à¦¦à¦¿à¦¤à§‡ à¦¹à¦¬à§‡à¥¤",
                })
                continue

            raw_roll = cell(row, 'roll_number')
            try:
                roll_number = int(float(str(raw_roll).strip()))
            except (ValueError, TypeError):
                roll_number = None

            raw_tuition = cell(row, 'monthly_tuition_fee')
            try:
                monthly_tuition_fee = float(raw_tuition) if raw_tuition not in (None, '') else 0.0
            except (ValueError, TypeError):
                monthly_tuition_fee = 0.0

            raw_transport = str(cell(row, 'uses_transport') or '').strip().lower()
            raw_transport_fee = cell(row, 'monthly_transport_fee') or 0
            try:
                transport_fee = float(raw_transport_fee)
            except (ValueError, TypeError):
                transport_fee = 0.0

            uses_transport = (
                raw_transport in ('true', 'yes', '1', 'y', 'à¦¹à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦') or transport_fee > 0
            )

            vehicle_val = str(cell(row, 'assigned_vehicle') or '').strip()
            assigned_vehicle_pk = None
            if vehicle_val:
                try:
                    from apps.finance.models import TransportVehicle
                    v = (
                        TransportVehicle.objects.filter(vehicle_number__iexact=vehicle_val, is_active=True).first() or
                        TransportVehicle.objects.filter(route_name__icontains=vehicle_val, is_active=True).first()
                    )
                    if not v:
                        try:
                            import uuid
                            uuid.UUID(vehicle_val)
                            v = TransportVehicle.objects.filter(id=vehicle_val, is_active=True).first()
                        except Exception:
                            pass
                    if v:
                        assigned_vehicle_pk = v.pk
                        uses_transport = True
                except Exception:
                    pass

            pin = f"{secrets.randbelow(1000000):06d}"
            prepared.append({
                'row': row_number,
                'data': {
                    'password': pin,
                    'first_name': first_name,
                    'last_name': last_name,
                    'gender': str(cell(row, 'gender') or 'MALE').upper(),
                    'section': section.pk,
                    'roll_number': roll_number,
                    'ssc_roll': str(cell(row, 'ssc_roll') or '').strip(),
                    'passing_year': str(cell(row, 'passing_year') or '').strip(),
                    'hsc_registration_no': str(cell(row, 'hsc_registration_no') or '').strip(),
                    'father_name': str(cell(row, 'father_name') or '').strip(),
                    'mother_name': str(cell(row, 'mother_name') or '').strip(),
                    'guardian_phone': str(cell(row, 'guardian_phone') or '').strip(),
                    'guardian_whatsapp_number': str(cell(row, 'guardian_whatsapp_number') or '').strip(),
                    'student_whatsapp_number': str(cell(row, 'student_whatsapp_number') or '').strip(),
                    'present_address': str(cell(row, 'present_address') or '').strip(),
                    'monthly_tuition_fee': monthly_tuition_fee,
                    'uses_transport': uses_transport,
                    'monthly_transport_fee': transport_fee if uses_transport else 0.0,
                    'assigned_vehicle': assigned_vehicle_pk,
                },
                'section': section,
                'pin': pin,
            })

        for item in prepared:
            serializer = StudentEnrollmentSerializer(data=item['data'], context={'request': request})
            if not serializer.is_valid():
                errors.append({"row": item['row'], "error": serializer.errors})

        if errors:
            return Response(
                {"success": False, "message": "Import à¦¬à¦¾à¦¤à¦¿à¦² à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤ Excel-à¦à¦° errorà¦—à§à¦²à§‹ à¦ à¦¿à¦• à¦•à¦°à§‡ à¦†à¦¬à¦¾à¦° upload à¦•à¦°à§à¦¨à¥¤", "errors": errors},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )

        imported = []
        with transaction.atomic():
            for item in prepared:
                serializer = StudentEnrollmentSerializer(data=item['data'], context={'request': request})
                serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)
                profile = serializer.save()
                profile.set_temporary_pin(item['pin'])
                profile.save(update_fields=['temporary_pin_token'])
                imported.append({
                    'id': str(profile.id),
                    'name': profile.user.get_full_name(),
                    'ssc_roll': profile.ssc_roll,
                    'passing_year': profile.passing_year,
                    'pin': item['pin'],
                    'guardian_whatsapp_number': profile.guardian_whatsapp_number,
                    'student_whatsapp_number': profile.student_whatsapp_number,
                })

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": f"{len(imported)} à¦œà¦¨ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦° account à¦“ profile à¦¤à§ˆà¦°à¦¿ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤",
            "data": imported,
        }, status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)

    def perform_update(self, serializer):
        instance = self.get_object()
        target_section = serializer.validated_data.get('section', instance.section)
        self._verify_incharge_permission(self.request.user, instance.section)
        self._verify_incharge_permission(self.request.user, target_section)
        serializer.save()

    @action(detail=True, methods=['get'], permission_classes=[IsPrincipal], url_path='360')
    def student_360(self, request, pk=None):
        student = self.get_object()
        attendance_qs = student.attendances.order_by('-date')
        attendance_counts = {
            status_value: attendance_qs.filter(status=status_value).count()
            for status_value, _ in StudentAttendance.AttendanceStatus.choices
        }
        invoices = student.invoices.prefetch_related('payments').order_by('-year', '-month')
        exam_results = student.exam_results.select_related('exam').order_by('-exam__start_date')
        subject_marks = student.subject_marks.select_related('exam', 'subject').order_by('-exam__start_date', 'subject__name')

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "data": {
                "profile": StudentDetailSerializer(student, context={'request': request}).data,
                "attendance": {
                    "summary": attendance_counts,
                    "total": attendance_qs.count(),
                    "records": [
                        {
                            "date": record.date,
                            "status": record.status,
                            "status_display": record.get_status_display(),
                            "remarks": record.remarks,
                        }
                        for record in attendance_qs[:100]
                    ],
                },
                "fees": {
                    "summary": {
                        "total": sum((invoice.total_amount for invoice in invoices), Decimal('0.00')),
                        "paid": sum((invoice.paid_amount for invoice in invoices), Decimal('0.00')),
                        "due": sum((invoice.due_amount for invoice in invoices), Decimal('0.00')),
                    },
                    "invoices": [
                        {
                            "id": str(invoice.id),
                            "invoice_number": invoice.invoice_number,
                            "month": invoice.month,
                            "year": invoice.year,
                            "total_amount": invoice.total_amount,
                            "paid_amount": invoice.paid_amount,
                            "due_amount": invoice.due_amount,
                            "status": invoice.status,
                            "payments": [
                                {
                                    "receipt_no": payment.receipt_no,
                                    "amount_paid": payment.amount_paid,
                                    "payment_method": payment.payment_method,
                                    "transaction_reference": payment.transaction_reference,
                                    "created_at": payment.created_at,
                                }
                                for payment in invoice.payments.all()
                            ],
                        }
                        for invoice in invoices[:100]
                    ],
                },
                "exams": {
                    "results": [
                        {
                            "exam": result.exam.name,
                            "exam_id": str(result.exam_id),
                            "total_full_marks": result.total_full_marks,
                            "total_obtained_marks": result.total_obtained_marks,
                            "gpa": result.gpa,
                            "grade_letter": result.grade_letter,
                            "is_passed": result.is_passed,
                            "section_position": result.section_position,
                            "class_position": result.class_position,
                            "failed_subjects_count": result.failed_subjects_count,
                        }
                        for result in exam_results
                    ],
                    "subject_marks": [
                        {
                            "exam": mark.exam.name,
                            "subject": mark.subject.name,
                            "final_total_marks": mark.final_total_marks,
                            "grade_letter": mark.grade_letter,
                            "grade_point": mark.grade_point,
                            "is_passed": mark.is_passed,
                            "is_absent": mark.is_absent,
                        }
                        for mark in subject_marks[:200]
                    ],
                },
            },
        })

    @action(detail=False, methods=['get'], url_path='my-id-card')
    def my_id_card(self, request):
        """
        GET /api/v1/academics/students/my-id-card/
        à¦¡à¦¿à¦œà¦¿à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦² à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§à¦¡à§‡à¦¨à§à¦Ÿ à¦†à¦‡à¦¡à¦¿ à¦•à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¡ à¦¡à§‡à¦Ÿà¦¾ à¦“ à¦•à¦¿à¦‰à¦†à¦°-à¦•à§‹à¦¡ à¦‡à¦¨à¦«à§‹à¥¤
        """
        if request.user.role != User.Role.STUDENT or not hasattr(request.user, 'student_profile'):
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "à¦¶à§à¦§à§à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦°à¦¾ à¦†à¦‡à¦¡à¦¿ à¦•à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¡ à¦ªà§‡à¦¤à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à§‡à¥¤"}, status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)

        student = request.user.student_profile
        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "institution": "STARLIGHT COLLEGE, SYLHET",
            "student_id": student.student_unique_id,
            "name": student.user.get_full_name(),
            "roll": student.roll_number,
            "class_level": student.section.get_class_level_display(),
            "group": student.section.get_group_display(),
            "section": student.section.section_name,
            "session": student.session,
            "guardian_phone": student.guardian_phone,
            "blood_group": "A+",
            "photo_url": student.user.profile_photo.url if student.user.profile_photo else None
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)
    
    @action(detail=False, methods=['get'], permission_classes=[IsTeacherOrPrincipal], url_path='at-risk-tracker')
    def at_risk_tracker(self, request):
        """
        GET /api/v1/academics/students/at-risk-tracker/?section=<uuid>
        à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œ à¦“ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦¸à¦¿à¦ªà¦¾à¦²à§‡à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ à¦¦à§à¦°à§à¦¬à¦² à¦“ à¦…à¦¨à¦¿à§Ÿà¦®à¦¿à¦¤ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦¦à§‡à¦° à¦…à¦Ÿà§‹à¦®à§‡à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦• à¦²à¦¾à¦² à¦¤à¦¾à¦²à¦¿à¦•à¦¾:
        à§§. à¦¯à¦¾à¦¦à§‡à¦° à¦•à§à¦²à¦¾à¦¸à§‡ à¦®à§‹à¦Ÿ à¦‰à¦ªà¦¸à§à¦¥à¦¿à¦¤à¦¿ à§­à§¦%-à¦à¦° à¦¨à¦¿à¦šà§‡ à¦¨à§‡à¦®à§‡ à¦—à§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤
        à§¨. à¦¯à¦¾à¦°à¦¾ à¦à¦•à¦¾à¦§à¦¿à¦• à¦ªà¦°à§€à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à§Ÿ à¦¬à¦¾ à¦…à¦¨à§à¦¤à¦¤ à§¨à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦¬à¦¿à¦·à§Ÿà§‡ à¦«à§‡à¦² à¦•à¦°à§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤
        """
        user = request.user
        section_id = request.query_params.get('section')

        students = StudentProfile.objects.filter(user__is_active=True).select_related('user', 'section')

        if user.role == User.Role.TEACHER:
            # à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦• à¦¶à§à¦§à§ à¦¨à¦¿à¦œ à¦¦à¦¾à§Ÿà¦¿à¦¤à§à¦¬à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¾à¦ªà§à¦¤ à¦¶à¦¾à¦–à¦¾à¦° à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦¦à§‡à¦° à¦à§à¦à¦•à¦¿ à¦¦à§‡à¦–à¦¤à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦¬à§‡à¦¨
            students = students.filter(section__in_charge_teacher=user)
        elif section_id:
            students = students.filter(section_id=section_id)

        at_risk_list = []
        for s in students:
            risk_factors = []
            
            # à§§. à¦‰à¦ªà¦¸à§à¦¥à¦¿à¦¤à¦¿ à¦¬à¦¿à¦¶à§à¦²à§‡à¦·à¦£
            total_days = StudentAttendance.objects.filter(section=s.section).values('date').distinct().count()
            present_days = StudentAttendance.objects.filter(
                student=s, 
                status__in=[StudentAttendance.AttendanceStatus.PRESENT, StudentAttendance.AttendanceStatus.LATE]
            ).count()

            attendance_pct = round((present_days / total_days) * 100, 1) if total_days > 0 else 100.0
            if attendance_pct < 70.0:
                risk_factors.append({
                    "type": "LOW_ATTENDANCE",
                    "severity": "HIGH" if attendance_pct < 50.0 else "MEDIUM",
                    "description": f"à¦‰à¦ªà¦¸à§à¦¥à¦¿à¦¤à¦¿ à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° {attendance_pct}% (à¦®à§‹à¦Ÿ {total_days} à¦•à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¯à¦¦à¦¿à¦¬à¦¸à§‡ à¦‰à¦ªà¦¸à§à¦¥à¦¿à¦¤ {present_days} à¦¦à¦¿à¦¨)à¥¤"
                })

            # à§¨. à¦à¦•à¦¾à¦¡à§‡à¦®à¦¿à¦• à¦«à¦²à¦¾à¦«à¦² à¦“ à¦«à§‡à¦‡à¦²à§à¦¡ à¦¬à¦¿à¦¶à§à¦²à§‡à¦·à¦£
            failed_results = StudentExamResult.objects.filter(student=s, is_passed=False)
            failed_exam_count = failed_results.count()
            
            if failed_exam_count >= 1:
                total_failed_subs = sum(res.failed_subjects_count for res in failed_results)
                risk_factors.append({
                    "type": "ACADEMIC_FAILURE",
                    "severity": "CRITICAL" if failed_exam_count >= 2 else "HIGH",
                    "description": f"à¦ à¦ªà¦°à§à¦¯à¦¨à§à¦¤ {failed_exam_count}à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦ªà¦°à§€à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à§Ÿ à¦…à¦•à§ƒà¦¤à¦•à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¯ (à¦®à§‹à¦Ÿ {total_failed_subs}à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦¬à¦¿à¦·à§Ÿà§‡ à¦«à§‡à¦²)à¥¤"
                })

            # à¦¯à¦¦à¦¿ à¦•à§‹à¦¨à§‹ à¦à¦•à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦à§à¦à¦•à¦¿à¦¤à§‡ à¦ªà§œà§‡ à¦¤à¦¬à§‡ à¦¤à¦¾à¦²à¦¿à¦•à¦¾à§Ÿ à¦¯à§‹à¦— à¦¹à¦¬à§‡
            if risk_factors:
                at_risk_list.append({
                    "student_id": str(s.id),
                    "student_unique_id": s.student_unique_id,
                    "roll_number": s.roll_number,
                    "student_name": s.user.get_full_name(),
                    "section": str(s.section),
                    "guardian_phone": s.guardian_phone,
                    "attendance_percentage": f"{attendance_pct}%",
                    "risk_factors_count": len(risk_factors),
                    "risk_details": risk_factors,
                })

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "total_at_risk_students": len(at_risk_list),
            "watchlist": at_risk_list
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

from datetime import datetime
from apps.academics.models import ClassRoutine, Homework, Subject, Syllabus
from apps.academics.serializers import (
    ClassRoutineSerializer,
    HomeworkSerializer,
    SubjectSerializer,
    SyllabusSerializer,
)

class SubjectViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    """
    à¦ªà¦¾à¦ à§à¦¯ à¦¬à¦¿à¦·à§Ÿ (Subject) à¦¤à§ˆà¦°à¦¿ à¦“ à¦¤à¦¾à¦²à¦¿à¦•à¦¾:
    - à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦¸à¦¿à¦ªà¦¾à¦²: à¦¬à¦¿à¦·à§Ÿ à¦¤à§ˆà¦°à¦¿, à¦†à¦ªà¦¡à§‡à¦Ÿ à¦“ à¦¡à¦¿à¦²à¦¿à¦Ÿ à¦•à¦°à¦¤à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à¦¬à§‡à¦¨à¥¤
    - à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦• à¦“ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€: à¦¬à¦¿à¦·à§Ÿ à¦¤à¦¾à¦²à¦¿à¦•à¦¾ à¦¦à§‡à¦–à¦¤à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à¦¬à§‡à¦¨à¥¤
    """
    queryset = Subject.objects.select_related('default_teacher').filter(is_active=True)
    serializer_class = SubjectSerializer

    def get_permissions(self):
        if self.action in ['list', 'retrieve']:
            return [IsAuthenticated()]
        return [IsPrincipal()]

    def get_queryset(self):
        queryset = super().get_queryset()
        class_level = self.request.query_params.get('class_level')
        group = self.request.query_params.get('group')
        if class_level:
            queryset = queryset.filter(class_level=class_level.upper())
        if group:
            queryset = queryset.filter(group=group.upper())
        return queryset


class ClassRoutineViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    """
    à¦•à§à¦²à¦¾à¦¸ à¦°à§à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦¨ à¦¤à§ˆà¦°à¦¿ à¦“ à¦¶à¦¿à¦¡à¦¿à¦‰à¦² à¦­à¦¿à¦‰à¦¸à§‡à¦Ÿ:
    - à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦¸à¦¿à¦ªà¦¾à¦²: à¦°à§à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦¨ à¦¤à§ˆà¦°à¦¿ à¦“ à¦®à¦¡à¦¿à¦«à¦¾à¦‡ à¦•à¦°à¦¤à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à¦¬à§‡à¦¨à¥¤
    - à¦¸à§à¦ªà§‡à¦¶à¦¾à¦² à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦•à¦¶à¦¨ 'my-schedule': à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦• à¦†à¦œà¦•à§‡à¦° à¦¦à¦¿à¦¨à§‡à¦° à¦¤à¦¾à¦à¦° à¦•à§à¦²à¦¾à¦¸ à¦¶à¦¿à¦¡à¦¿à¦‰à¦² à¦¦à§‡à¦–à¦¬à§‡à¦¨à¥¤
    """
    queryset = ClassRoutine.objects.select_related('section', 'subject', 'teacher').all()
    serializer_class = ClassRoutineSerializer

    def get_permissions(self):
        if self.action in ['list', 'retrieve', 'my_schedule']:
            return [IsAuthenticated()]
        return [IsPrincipal()]

    def get_queryset(self):
        user = self.request.user
        queryset = super().get_queryset()

        # à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§à¦¡à§‡à¦¨à§à¦Ÿ à¦²à¦—à¦‡à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦²à§‡ à¦¸à§à¦¬à§Ÿà¦‚à¦•à§à¦°à¦¿à§Ÿà¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ à¦¤à¦¾à¦° à¦¸à§‡à¦•à¦¶à¦¨à§‡à¦° à¦°à§à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦¨ à¦«à¦¿à¦²à§à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦° à¦¹à¦¬à§‡
        if user.role == User.Role.STUDENT and hasattr(user, 'student_profile'):
            return queryset.filter(section=user.student_profile.section)

        section_id = self.request.query_params.get('section')
        if section_id:
            queryset = queryset.filter(section_id=section_id)

        teacher_id = self.request.query_params.get('teacher')
        if teacher_id:
            queryset = queryset.filter(teacher_id=teacher_id)

        day = self.request.query_params.get('day_of_week')
        if day is not None:
            queryset = queryset.filter(day_of_week=day)

        return queryset

    @action(detail=False, methods=['get'], url_path='my-schedule')
    def my_schedule(self, request):
        """
        GET /api/v1/academics/routines/my-schedule/
        à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦•à§‡à¦° à¦†à¦œà¦•à§‡à¦° à¦ªà¦¿à¦°à¦¿à§Ÿà¦¡à¦—à§à¦²à§‹à¦° à¦¤à¦¾à¦²à¦¿à¦•à¦¾ à¦“ à¦¨à§‹à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦«à¦¿à¦•à§‡à¦¶à¦¨ à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦²à¦¾à¦°à§à¦Ÿà¥¤
        """
        if request.user.role != User.Role.TEACHER:
            return Response(
                {"success": False, "message": "à¦¶à§à¦§à§à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦•à¦¦à§‡à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ à¦à¦‡ à¦¶à¦¿à¦¡à¦¿à¦‰à¦² à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¯à§‹à¦œà§à¦¯à¥¤"},
                status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN
            )

        # à¦ªà¦¾à¦‡à¦¥à¦¨à§‡à¦° weekday (0=Mon, 6=Sun) à¦•à§‡ à¦†à¦®à¦¾à¦¦à§‡à¦° à¦®à¦¡à§‡à¦²à§‡à¦° (0=Sat .. 6=Fri) à¦¸à¦¾à¦¥à§‡ à¦°à§‚à¦ªà¦¾à¦¨à§à¦¤à¦°
        py_weekday = datetime.today().weekday()
        model_day = (py_weekday + 2) % 7

        todays_classes = ClassRoutine.objects.filter(
            teacher=request.user,
            day_of_week=model_day
        ).select_related('section', 'subject').order_time_asc() if hasattr(ClassRoutine.objects, 'order_time_asc') else ClassRoutine.objects.filter(
            teacher=request.user,
            day_of_week=model_day
        ).select_related('section', 'subject').order_by('start_time')

        serializer = self.get_serializer(todays_classes, many=True)
        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "day": ClassRoutine.DayOfWeek(model_day).label,
            "total_classes_today": todays_classes.count(),
            "data": serializer.data
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)
        
    @method_decorator(cache_page(60 * 15)) # à§§à§« à¦®à¦¿à¦¨à¦¿à¦Ÿà§‡à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ à¦¸à¦¾à¦°à§à¦­à¦¾à¦° à¦•à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¶à§‡ à¦¥à¦¾à¦•à¦¬à§‡
    @method_decorator(vary_on_headers("Authorization")) # à¦‡à¦‰à¦œà¦¾à¦°à§‡à¦° à¦Ÿà§‹à¦•à§‡à¦¨ à¦…à¦¨à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¯à¦¼à§€ à¦†à¦²à¦¾à¦¦à¦¾ à¦•à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¶
    def list(self, request, *args, **kwargs):
        """à¦°à§à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦¨ à¦¦à§‡à¦–à¦¾à¦° à¦¸à¦®à§Ÿ à¦•à§‹à¦¨à§‹ à¦¡à§‡à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦¬à§‡à¦¸ à¦•à§‹à§Ÿà§‡à¦°à¦¿ à¦¹à¦¬à§‡ à¦¨à¦¾, à¦¸à¦°à¦¾à¦¸à¦°à¦¿ à¦•à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¶ à¦¥à§‡à¦•à§‡ à¦®à¦¿à¦²à¦¿à¦¸à§‡à¦•à§‡à¦¨à§à¦¡à§‡ à¦²à§‹à¦¡ à¦¹à¦¬à§‡"""
        return super().list(request, *args, **kwargs)

class StudentAttendanceViewSet(viewsets.GenericViewSet):
    """
    à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦¦à§‡à¦° à¦¡à¦¿à¦œà¦¿à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦² à¦¬à¦¾à¦²à§à¦• à¦¹à¦¾à¦œà¦¿à¦°à¦¾ à¦­à¦¿à¦‰à¦¸à§‡à¦Ÿ:
    1. bulk-mark: à¦¶à§à¦°à§‡à¦£à¦¿ à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œ à¦à¦• à¦•à§à¦²à¦¿à¦•à§‡ à¦ªà§à¦°à§‹ à¦•à§à¦²à¦¾à¦¸à§‡à¦° à¦¹à¦¾à¦œà¦¿à¦°à¦¾ à¦¦à§‡à¦¬à§‡à¦¨/à¦†à¦ªà¦¡à§‡à¦Ÿ à¦•à¦°à¦¬à§‡à¦¨à¥¤
    2. section-sheet: à¦•à§‹à¦¨à§‹ à¦¤à¦¾à¦°à¦¿à¦–à§‡à¦° à¦ªà§à¦°à§‹ à¦¸à§‡à¦•à¦¶à¦¨à§‡à¦° à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§à¦¡à§‡à¦¨à§à¦Ÿ à¦¤à¦¾à¦²à¦¿à¦•à¦¾ à¦“ à¦¤à¦¾à¦¦à§‡à¦° à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§à¦¯à¦¾à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦¸ à¦°à¦¿à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦°à§à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦¬à§‡à¥¤
    3. my-attendance: à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§à¦¡à§‡à¦¨à§à¦Ÿ à¦²à¦—à¦‡à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à§‡ à¦¨à¦¿à¦œà§‡à¦° à¦®à¦¾à¦¸à¦¿à¦• à¦‰à¦ªà¦¸à§à¦¥à¦¿à¦¤à¦¿ à¦°à¦¿à¦ªà§‹à¦°à§à¦Ÿ à¦“ à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à§à¦¸à§‡à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà§‡à¦œ (%) à¦¦à§‡à¦–à¦¬à§‡à¥¤
    """
    queryset = StudentAttendance.objects.select_related('student', 'student__user', 'section', 'recorded_by').all()

    def get_permissions(self):
        if self.action == 'my_attendance':
            return [IsAuthenticated()]
        return [IsTeacherOrPrincipal()]

    @action(detail=False, methods=['get'], url_path='section-sheet')
    def section_sheet(self, request):
        """
        GET /api/v1/academics/attendance/section-sheet/?section=<uuid>&date=YYYY-MM-DD
        à¦«à§à¦°à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà¦à¦¨à§à¦¡à§‡ à¦¹à¦¾à¦œà¦¿à¦°à¦¾ à¦¨à§‡à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾à¦° à¦ªà§‡à¦œ à¦²à§‹à¦¡ à¦•à¦°à¦¾à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ à¦à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦•à¦² à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à¦¬à§‡à¥¤
        """
        section_id = request.query_params.get('section')
        if not section_id:
            return Response(
                {"success": False, "message": "section à¦ªà§à¦¯à¦¾à¦°à¦¾à¦®à¦¿à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦° à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¦à¦¾à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦†à¦¬à¦¶à§à¦¯à¦•à¥¤"},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST
            )

        try:
            section = AcademicSection.objects.get(id=section_id)
        except AcademicSection.DoesNotExist:
            return Response(
                {"success": False, "message": "à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¦à¦¤à§à¦¤ à¦¶à¦¾à¦–à¦¾à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦ªà¦¾à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾ à¦¯à¦¾à§Ÿà¦¨à¦¿à¥¤"},
                status=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND
            )

        # à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œ à¦¸à¦¿à¦•à¦¿à¦‰à¦°à¦¿à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦¬à¦¾à¦‰à¦¨à§à¦¡à¦¾à¦°à¦¿ à¦šà§‡à¦•
        if request.user.role == User.Role.TEACHER and section.in_charge_teacher_id != request.user.id:
            raise PermissionDenied(f"à¦†à¦ªà¦¨à¦¿ '{section}' à¦¶à¦¾à¦–à¦¾à¦° à¦¶à§à¦°à§‡à¦£à¦¿ à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œ à¦¨à¦¨à¥¤ à¦¶à§à¦§à§à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° à¦¶à§à¦°à§‡à¦£à¦¿ à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œ à¦¹à¦¾à¦œà¦¿à¦°à¦¾ à¦¦à§‡à¦–à¦¤à§‡ à¦¬à¦¾ à¦¨à¦¿à¦¤à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à¦¬à§‡à¦¨à¥¤")

        target_date_str = request.query_params.get('date')
        if target_date_str:
            try:
                target_date = datetime.strptime(target_date_str, '%Y-%m-%d').date()
            except ValueError:
                return Response(
                    {"success": False, "message": "à¦¤à¦¾à¦°à¦¿à¦– à¦…à¦¬à¦¶à§à¦¯à¦‡ YYYY-MM-DD à¦«à¦°à¦®à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦Ÿà§‡ à¦¹à¦¤à§‡ à¦¹à¦¬à§‡à¥¤"},
                    status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST
                )
        else:
            target_date = timezone.localdate()

        # à¦“à¦‡ à¦¸à§‡à¦•à¦¶à¦¨à§‡à¦° à¦¸à¦¬ à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§à¦¡à§‡à¦¨à§à¦Ÿ à¦¸à¦‚à¦—à§à¦°à¦¹
        students = StudentProfile.objects.filter(section=section, user__is_active=True).select_related('user').order_by('roll_number')
        
        # à¦‡à¦¤à¦¿à¦®à¦§à§à¦¯à§‡ à¦“à¦‡ à¦¤à¦¾à¦°à¦¿à¦–à§‡ à¦¨à§‡à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾ à¦¹à¦¾à¦œà¦¿à¦°à¦¾à¦° à¦®à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦ª à¦¤à§ˆà¦°à¦¿
        existing_attendances = {
            att.student_id: att
            for att in StudentAttendance.objects.filter(section=section, date=target_date)
        }

        sheet_data = []
        for stu in students:
            att = existing_attendances.get(stu.id)
            sheet_data.append({
                "student_id": str(stu.id),
                "student_unique_id": stu.student_unique_id,
                "roll_number": stu.roll_number,
                "student_name": stu.user.get_full_name() or stu.user.username,
                "status": att.status if att else StudentAttendance.AttendanceStatus.PRESENT,
                "remarks": att.remarks if att else "",
                "is_already_marked": att is not None
            })

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "section": str(section),
            "date": target_date.strftime('%Y-%m-%d'),
            "total_students": len(sheet_data),
            "is_submitted": any(item['is_already_marked'] for item in sheet_data),
            "students": sheet_data
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    @action(detail=False, methods=['post'], url_path='bulk-mark')
    def bulk_mark(self, request):
        """
        POST /api/v1/academics/attendance/bulk-mark/
        à¦¶à§à¦°à§‡à¦£à¦¿ à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œ à¦à¦• à¦•à§à¦²à¦¿à¦•à§‡ à¦ªà§à¦°à§‹ à¦¸à§‡à¦•à¦¶à¦¨à§‡à¦° à¦¹à¦¾à¦œà¦¿à¦°à¦¾ à¦¸à§‡à¦­ à¦•à¦°à¦¬à§‡à¦¨à¥¤
        """
        serializer = BulkAttendanceCreateSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        section_id = serializer.validated_data['section_id']
        section = AcademicSection.objects.get(id=section_id)

        # à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œ à¦¸à¦¿à¦•à¦¿à¦‰à¦°à¦¿à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦¬à¦¾à¦‰à¦¨à§à¦¡à¦¾à¦°à¦¿ à¦šà§‡à¦•
        if request.user.role == User.Role.TEACHER and section.in_charge_teacher_id != request.user.id:
            raise PermissionDenied(f"à¦†à¦ªà¦¨à¦¿ '{section}' à¦¶à¦¾à¦–à¦¾à¦° à¦¶à§à¦°à§‡à¦£à¦¿ à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œ à¦¨à¦¨à¥¤ à¦†à¦ªà¦¨à¦¿ à¦à¦‡ à¦•à§à¦²à¦¾à¦¸à§‡à¦° à¦¹à¦¾à¦œà¦¿à¦°à¦¾ à¦¸à¦¾à¦¬à¦®à¦¿à¦Ÿ à¦•à¦°à¦¤à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à¦¬à§‡à¦¨ à¦¨à¦¾à¥¤")

        saved_records = serializer.save_bulk(recorder_user=request.user)

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": f"à¦®à§‹à¦Ÿ {len(saved_records)} à¦œà¦¨ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦° à¦¹à¦¾à¦œà¦¿à¦°à¦¾ à¦¸à¦«à¦²à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ à¦¸à¦‚à¦°à¦•à§à¦·à¦£ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤",
            "date": serializer.validated_data['date'].strftime('%Y-%m-%d'),
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    @action(detail=False, methods=['get'], url_path='my-attendance')
    def my_attendance(self, request):
        """
        GET /api/v1/academics/attendance/my-attendance/
        à¦²à¦—à¦‡à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§à¦¡à§‡à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà§‡à¦° à¦‰à¦ªà¦¸à§à¦¥à¦¿à¦¤à¦¿ à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¨à¦¾à¦²à¦¿à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦•à§à¦¸ à¦“ à¦¶à¦¤à¦•à¦°à¦¾ (%) à¦•à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦²à¦•à§à¦²à§‡à¦¶à¦¨à¥¤
        """
        if request.user.role != User.Role.STUDENT or not hasattr(request.user, 'student_profile'):
            return Response(
                {"success": False, "message": "à¦à¦‡ à¦°à¦¿à¦ªà§‹à¦°à§à¦Ÿà¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦¶à§à¦§à§à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦¦à§‡à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¯à§‹à¦œà§à¦¯à¥¤"},
                status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN
            )

        student = request.user.student_profile
        records = StudentAttendance.objects.filter(student=student).order_by('-date')

        # à¦à¦šà§à¦›à¦¿à¦• à¦«à¦¿à¦²à§à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦°à¦¿à¦‚: ?month=10&year=2026
        month = request.query_params.get('month')
        year = request.query_params.get('year')
        if month and year:
            records = records.filter(date__month=month, date__year=year)

        total_working_days = records.count()
        present_count = records.filter(status=StudentAttendance.AttendanceStatus.PRESENT).count()
        late_count = records.filter(status=StudentAttendance.AttendanceStatus.LATE).count()
        absent_count = records.filter(status=StudentAttendance.AttendanceStatus.ABSENT).count()
        leave_count = records.filter(status=StudentAttendance.AttendanceStatus.LEAVE).count()

        percentage = 0.0
        if total_working_days > 0:
            # à¦ªà§à¦°à§‡à¦œà§‡à¦¨à§à¦Ÿ à¦à¦¬à¦‚ à¦²à§‡à¦Ÿ à¦‰à¦­à§Ÿà¦•à§‡à¦‡ à¦‰à¦ªà¦¸à§à¦¥à¦¿à¦¤à¦¿à¦° à¦…à¦‚à¦¶ à¦¹à¦¿à¦¸à§‡à¦¬à§‡ à¦§à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿ
            percentage = round(((present_count + late_count) / total_working_days) * 100, 2)

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "student_unique_id": student.student_unique_id,
            "total_working_days": total_working_days,
            "present_count": present_count,
            "late_count": late_count,
            "absent_count": absent_count,
            "leave_count": leave_count,
            "attendance_percentage": f"{percentage}%",
            "history": StudentAttendanceRecordSerializer(records[:30], many=True).data
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

class AcademicHolidayViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    """
    à¦à¦•à¦¾à¦¡à§‡à¦®à¦¿à¦• à¦›à§à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦° à¦•à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦²à§‡à¦¨à§à¦¡à¦¾à¦° à¦­à¦¿à¦‰à¦¸à§‡à¦Ÿ:
    - à¦¤à§ˆà¦°à¦¿/à¦†à¦ªà¦¡à§‡à¦Ÿ: à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦¸à¦¿à¦ªà¦¾à¦²à¥¤
    - à¦•à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦²à§‡à¦¨à§à¦¡à¦¾à¦° à¦¦à§‡à¦–à¦¾: à¦¸à¦•à¦² à¦‡à¦‰à¦œà¦¾à¦°à¥¤
    """
    queryset = AcademicHoliday.objects.all()
    serializer_class = AcademicHolidaySerializer

    @method_decorator(cache_page(60))
    @method_decorator(vary_on_headers('Authorization'))
    def list(self, request, *args, **kwargs):
        return super().list(request, *args, **kwargs)

    def get_permissions(self):
        if self.action in ['list', 'retrieve']:
            return [IsAuthenticated()]
        return [IsPrincipal()]

from django.contrib import admin
from apps.common.models import SystemActivityLog

@admin.register(SystemActivityLog)
class SystemActivityLogAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ('action', 'username', 'user_role', 'ip_address', 'created_at')
    list_filter = ('action', 'user_role', 'created_at')
    search_fields = ('username', 'description', 'ip_address')
    readonly_fields = ('user', 'username', 'user_role', 'action', 'ip_address', 'description', 'created_at')

import uuid
from django.db import models

class BaseModel(models.Model):
    """
    à¦¸à¦®à¦¸à§à¦¤ à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦ªà§‡à¦° à¦Ÿà§‡à¦¬à¦¿à¦²à§‡à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¬à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§à¦°à¦¾à¦•à§à¦Ÿ à¦¬à§‡à¦¸ à¦®à¦¡à§‡à¦²à¥¤

    """
    id = models.UUIDField(primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4, editable=False)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True, db_index=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        abstract = True

    à¦ªà§à¦°à§‹ à¦¸à¦¿à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§‡à¦®à§‡à¦° à¦…à¦ªà¦°à¦¿à¦¬à¦°à§à¦¤à¦¨à¦¯à§‹à¦—à§à¦¯ (Immutable) à¦¸à¦¿à¦•à¦¿à¦‰à¦°à¦¿à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦“ à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦•à§à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦­à¦¿à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦…à¦¡à¦¿à¦Ÿ à¦²à¦—à¥¤

        SECURITY_ALERT = 'SECURITY_ALERT', 'à¦¨à¦¿à¦°à¦¾à¦ªà¦¤à§à¦¤à¦¾ à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦²à¦¾à¦°à§à¦Ÿ'

    user_role = models.CharField(max_length=50)

        return f"[{self.action}] {self.username} ({self.user_role}) - {self.created_at.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M')}"

from rest_framework.permissions import BasePermission
from apps.users_auth.models import User


class IsPrincipal(BasePermission):
    """
    à¦¶à§à¦§à§à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦¸à¦¿à¦ªà¦¾à¦²à§‡à¦° (Super Admin) à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ à¦à¦•à§à¦¸à§‡à¦¸à¥¤
    """
    message = "à¦à¦‡ à¦•à¦¾à¦œà¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦•à¦°à¦¾à¦° à¦…à¦¨à§à¦®à¦¤à¦¿ à¦¶à§à¦§à§à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦¸à¦¿à¦ªà¦¾à¦²à§‡à¦° à¦°à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤"

    def has_permission(self, request, view):
        return bool(
            request.user 
            and request.user.is_authenticated 
            and (request.user.role == User.Role.PRINCIPAL or request.user.is_superuser)
        )


class IsTeacherOrPrincipal(BasePermission):
    """
    à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦• à¦à¦¬à¦‚ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦¸à¦¿à¦ªà¦¾à¦² à¦‰à¦­à§Ÿà§‡à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ à¦à¦•à§à¦¸à§‡à¦¸à¥¤

FILE: apps/common/validators.py

from pathlib import Path

from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError


def validate_upload(file, allowed_extensions, max_size_mb=10):
    if not file:
        return file
    extension = Path(file.name).suffix.lower()
    if extension not in allowed_extensions:
        raise ValidationError(
            f"Unsupported file type. Allowed types: {', '.join(sorted(allowed_extensions))}."
        )
    if file.size > max_size_mb * 1024 * 1024:
        raise ValidationError(f"File size must not exceed {max_size_mb} MB.")
    return file

from django.db import connection
from django.http import JsonResponse
from rest_framework import viewsets
from rest_framework.decorators import api_view, permission_classes
from rest_framework.permissions import AllowAny
from apps.common.models import SystemActivityLog
from apps.common.permissions import IsPrincipal
from apps.common.serializers import SystemActivityLogSerializer


@api_view(['GET'])
@permission_classes([AllowAny])
def health_check(request):
    try:
        with connection.cursor() as cursor:
            cursor.execute('SELECT 1')
            cursor.fetchone()
    except Exception:
        return JsonResponse(
            {'status': 'unhealthy', 'database': 'unavailable'},
            status=503,
        )
    return JsonResponse({'status': 'ok', 'database': 'ok'})

class SystemActivityLogViewSet(viewsets.ReadOnlyModelViewSet):
    """
    à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦¸à¦¿à¦ªà¦¾à¦² à¦•à¦°à§à¦¤à§ƒà¦• à¦¸à¦¿à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§‡à¦® à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦•à§à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦­à¦¿à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦…à¦¡à¦¿à¦Ÿ à¦²à¦— à¦ªà¦°à§à¦¯à¦¬à§‡à¦•à§à¦·à¦£à¥¤
    """
    queryset = SystemActivityLog.objects.all()
    serializer_class = SystemActivityLogSerializer
    permission_classes = [IsPrincipal]

    def get_queryset(self):
        queryset = super().get_queryset()
        action_param = self.request.query_params.get('action')
        username = self.request.query_params.get('username')
        if action_param:
            queryset = queryset.filter(action=action_param.upper())
        if username:
            queryset = queryset.filter(username__icontains=username)
        return queryset

from django.contrib import admin
from apps.examination.models import (

    list_filter = ('exam_type', 'session', 'class_level', 'is_published')

class ExamSubjectConfigAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
