from decimal import Decimal
from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError
from django.db import models
from django.utils import timezone
from apps.academics.models import AcademicSection, Subject
from apps.common.models import BaseModel
from apps.users_auth.models import StudentProfile, User

class Exam(BaseModel):
    """
    à¦•à¦²à§‡à¦œà§‡à¦° à¦®à§‚à¦² à¦ªà¦°à§€à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦° à¦•à¦¨à¦«à¦¿à¦—à¦¾à¦°à§‡à¦¶à¦¨ à¦®à¦¡à§‡à¦²à¥¤
    """
    class ExamType(models.TextChoices):
        SEMESTER = 'SEMESTER', 'à¦¸à§‡à¦®à¦¿à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦° à¦ªà¦°à§€à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾ (Semester Exam)'
        MODEL_TEST = 'MODEL_TEST', 'à¦®à¦¡à§‡à¦² à¦Ÿà§‡à¦¸à§à¦Ÿ (Model Test - No Tutorial)'
        ANNUAL = 'ANNUAL', 'à¦¬à¦¾à¦°à§à¦·à¦¿à¦• à¦ªà¦°à§€à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾ (Annual Exam)'
        PRE_TEST = 'PRE_TEST', 'à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¾à¦•-à¦¨à¦¿à¦°à§à¦¬à¦¾à¦šà¦¨à§€ à¦ªà¦°à§€à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾ (Pre-Test)'
        TEST = 'TEST', 'à¦¨à¦¿à¦°à§à¦¬à¦¾à¦šà¦¨à§€ à¦ªà¦°à§€à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾ (Test Exam)'

    name = models.CharField(max_length=150, help_text="à¦¯à§‡à¦®à¦¨: 2nd Semester - 2025")
    exam_type = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=ExamType.choices, default=ExamType.SEMESTER, db_index=True)
    session = models.CharField(max_length=20, db_index=True, help_text="à¦¯à§‡à¦®à¦¨: 2025-2026")
    class_level = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=AcademicSection.ClassLevel.choices, db_index=True)
    
    start_date = models.DateField(help_text="à¦ªà¦°à§€à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾ à¦¬à¦¾ à¦¸à§‡à¦®à¦¿à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦° à¦¶à§à¦°à§à¦° à¦¤à¦¾à¦°à¦¿à¦– (à¦¹à¦¾à¦œà¦¿à¦°à¦¾ à¦—à¦£à¦¨à¦¾à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯)")
    end_date = models.DateField(help_text="à¦ªà¦°à§€à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾ à¦¶à§‡à¦·à§‡à¦° à¦¤à¦¾à¦°à¦¿à¦–")

    # à¦«à§à¦²à§‡à¦•à§à¦¸à¦¿à¦¬à¦² à¦•à¦¨à¦­à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¸à¦¨ à¦…à¦ªà¦¶à¦¨ (à¦®à¦¡à§‡à¦² à¦Ÿà§‡à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§‡ à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦‰à¦Ÿà§‹à¦°à¦¿à§Ÿà¦¾à¦² à¦›à¦¾à§œà¦¾ à§§à§¦à§¦%, à¦¸à§‡à¦®à¦¿à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦°à§‡ à§®à§¦% + à§¨à§¦%)
    has_tutorial_conversion = models.BooleanField(
        default=True,
        help_text="à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦• à¦¦à§‡à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾ à¦¥à¦¾à¦•à¦²à§‡ à§®à§¦% à¦à¦•à§à¦¸à¦¾à¦® + à§¨à§¦% à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦‰à¦Ÿà§‹à¦°à¦¿à§Ÿà¦¾à¦² à¦¹à¦¬à§‡à¥¤ à¦†à¦¨à¦šà§‡à¦• à¦¥à¦¾à¦•à¦²à§‡ à¦¸à¦°à¦¾à¦¸à¦°à¦¿ à§§à§¦à§¦% à¦à¦•à§à¦¸à¦¾à¦® à¦¨à¦®à§à¦¬à¦° à¦•à¦¾à¦‰à¦¨à§à¦Ÿ à¦¹à¦¬à§‡à¥¤"
    )
    exam_weight_percentage = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5, decimal_places=2, default=80.00)
    tutorial_weight_percentage = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5, decimal_places=2, default=20.00)

    is_published = models.BooleanField(default=False, db_index=True, help_text="à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦• à¦¦à¦¿à¦²à§‡ à¦«à¦²à¦¾à¦«à¦² à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦¦à§‡à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ à¦¦à§ƒà¦¶à§à¦¯à¦®à¦¾à¦¨ à¦¹à¦¬à§‡")
    created_by = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Exam'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Exams'
        ordering = ['-start_date']

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        if self.exam_type == self.ExamType.MODEL_TEST:
            self.has_tutorial_conversion = False
            self.exam_weight_percentage = Decimal('100.00')
            self.tutorial_weight_percentage = Decimal('0.00')
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.name} [{self.get_class_level_display()}] ({self.session})"


class ExamSubjectConfig(BaseModel):
    """
    à¦ªà¦°à§€à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à§Ÿ à¦¬à¦¿à¦·à§Ÿà§‡à¦° à¦ªà§‚à¦°à§à¦£à¦®à¦¾à¦¨ à¦“ à¦¨à¦®à§à¦¬à¦° à¦¬à¦¿à¦­à¦¾à¦œà¦¨ (CQ, MCQ, Practical, Tutorial)à¥¤
    """
    exam = models.ForeignKey(Exam, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='subject_configs')
    subject = models.ForeignKey(Subject, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='exam_configs')
    
    full_marks = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=100.00)
    cq_full_marks = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=70.00)
    mcq_full_marks = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=30.00)
    practical_full_marks = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    tutorial_full_marks = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=20.00)
    pass_marks = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=33.00)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Exam Subject Configuration'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Exam Subject Configurations'
        unique_together = ('exam', 'subject')

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.exam.name} - {self.subject.name} (Full: {self.full_marks})"


class SubjectMarksEntry(BaseModel):
    """
    à¦¬à¦¿à¦·à§Ÿà¦­à¦¿à¦¤à§à¦¤à¦¿à¦• à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦¦à§‡à¦° à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¾à¦ªà§à¦¤ à¦¨à¦®à§à¦¬à¦° à¦à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà§à¦°à¦¿ à¦Ÿà§‡à¦¬à¦¿à¦²à¥¤
    à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦• à¦à¦•à¦‡ à¦Ÿà§‡à¦¬à¦¿à¦²à§‡ CQ, MCQ, Practical à¦à¦¬à¦‚ Tutorial à¦¨à¦®à§à¦¬à¦° à¦à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà§à¦°à¦¿ à¦•à¦°à¦¬à§‡à¦¨à¥¤
    """
    exam = models.ForeignKey(Exam, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='marks_entries')
    subject = models.ForeignKey(Subject, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='marks_entries')
    student = models.ForeignKey(StudentProfile, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='subject_marks')
    section = models.ForeignKey(AcademicSection, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='subject_marks')
    
    # à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦•à§‡à¦° à¦‡à¦¨à¦ªà§à¦Ÿ à¦«à¦¿à¦²à§à¦¡
    cq_marks = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    mcq_marks = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    practical_marks = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    tutorial_marks = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    is_absent = models.BooleanField(default=False)

    # à¦¸à¦¿à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§‡à¦® à¦•à¦°à§à¦¤à§ƒà¦• à¦¸à§à¦¬à§Ÿà¦‚à¦•à§à¦°à¦¿à§Ÿà¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ à¦¹à¦¿à¦¸à¦¾à¦¬à¦•à§ƒà¦¤ à¦«à¦²à¦¾à¦«à¦²
    obtained_semester_marks = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=0.00) # CQ + MCQ + Practical
    converted_semester_marks = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=0.00) # 80% (or 100%)
    final_total_marks = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=0.00) # Converted + Tutorial
    
    grade_letter = models.CharField(max_length=5, default='F', db_index=True) # A+, A, A-, B, C, D, F
    grade_point = models.DecimalField(max_digits=4, decimal_places=2, default=0.00) # 5.0, 4.0 ... 0.0
    is_passed = models.BooleanField(default=False, db_index=True)

    entered_by = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Subject Marks Entry'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Subject Marks Entries'
        unique_together = ('exam', 'subject', 'student')
        ordering = ['student__roll_number']

    def calculate_results(self):
        if self.is_absent:
            self.cq_marks = Decimal('0.00')
            self.mcq_marks = Decimal('0.00')
            self.practical_marks = Decimal('0.00')
            self.tutorial_marks = Decimal('0.00')
            self.obtained_semester_marks = Decimal('0.00')
            self.converted_semester_marks = Decimal('0.00')
            self.final_total_marks = Decimal('0.00')
            self.grade_letter = 'F'
            self.grade_point = Decimal('0.00')
            self.is_passed = False
            return

        self.cq_marks = Decimal(str(self.cq_marks or '0.00'))
        self.mcq_marks = Decimal(str(self.mcq_marks or '0.00'))
        self.practical_marks = Decimal(str(self.practical_marks or '0.00'))
        self.tutorial_marks = Decimal(str(self.tutorial_marks or '0.00'))
        config = self.exam.subject_configs.filter(subject=self.subject).first()
        if config is None:
            raise ValidationError("à¦à¦‡ à¦¬à¦¿à¦·à§Ÿà¦Ÿà¦¿à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ à¦ªà¦°à§€à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦° à¦¨à¦®à§à¦¬à¦° à¦•à¦¨à¦«à¦¿à¦—à¦¾à¦°à§‡à¦¶à¦¨ à¦ªà¦¾à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾ à¦¯à¦¾à§Ÿà¦¨à¦¿à¥¤")

        # à§§. à¦¸à§‡à¦®à¦¿à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦°à§‡à¦° à¦®à§‚à¦² à¦²à¦¿à¦–à¦¿à¦¤ à¦“ à¦¬à§à¦¯à¦¬à¦¹à¦¾à¦°à¦¿à¦• à¦¨à¦®à§à¦¬à¦°
        self.obtained_semester_marks = self.cq_marks + self.mcq_marks + self.practical_marks

        # à§¨. à¦•à¦¨à¦­à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¸à¦¨ à¦¶à¦¤à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à¦¿à¦¸à¦¾à¦¬
        if self.exam.has_tutorial_conversion:
            weight_factor = self.exam.exam_weight_percentage / Decimal('100.00')
            self.converted_semester_marks = round(self.obtained_semester_marks * weight_factor, 1)
            self.final_total_marks = round(self.converted_semester_marks + self.tutorial_marks, 1)
        else:
            self.converted_semester_marks = self.obtained_semester_marks
            self.final_total_marks = self.obtained_semester_marks

        # à§©. à¦•à¦¨à¦«à¦¿à¦—à¦¾à¦° à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦ªà§‚à¦°à§à¦£à¦®à¦¾à¦¨à§‡à¦° à¦¶à¦¤à¦¾à¦‚à¦¶ à¦…à¦¨à§à¦¯à¦¾à§Ÿà§€ à¦—à§à¦°à§‡à¦¡
        total = self.final_total_marks
        percentage = (total / config.full_marks) * Decimal('100.00') if config.full_marks else Decimal('0.00')
        if percentage >= Decimal('80.00'):
            self.grade_letter, self.grade_point = 'A+', Decimal('5.00')
        elif percentage >= Decimal('70.00'):
            self.grade_letter, self.grade_point = 'A', Decimal('4.00')
        elif percentage >= Decimal('60.00'):
            self.grade_letter, self.grade_point = 'A-', Decimal('3.50')
        elif percentage >= Decimal('50.00'):
            self.grade_letter, self.grade_point = 'B', Decimal('3.00')
        elif percentage >= Decimal('40.00'):
            self.grade_letter, self.grade_point = 'C', Decimal('2.00')
        elif percentage >= Decimal('33.00'):
            self.grade_letter, self.grade_point = 'D', Decimal('1.00')
        else:
            self.grade_letter, self.grade_point = 'F', Decimal('0.00')

        self.is_passed = total >= config.pass_marks

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        self.calculate_results()
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.student.roll_number} - {self.subject.name}: {self.final_total_marks} ({self.grade_letter})"


class CoCurricularEntry(BaseModel):
    """
    à¦¸à¦¹-à¦ªà¦¾à¦ à§à¦¯à¦•à§à¦°à¦®à¦¿à¦• à¦•à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¯à¦•à§à¦°à¦® (Co-curricular Activity, à¦ªà§‚à¦°à§à¦£à¦®à¦¾à¦¨ à§§à§¨à§ª):
    Attn (à¦¹à¦¾à¦œà¦¿à¦°à¦¾) à¦¸à§à¦¬à§Ÿà¦‚à¦•à§à¦°à¦¿à§Ÿà¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ Sprint 3-à¦à¦° à¦¹à¦¾à¦œà¦¿à¦°à¦¾ à¦¥à§‡à¦•à§‡ à¦†à¦¸à¦¬à§‡, à¦à¦¬à¦‚ Ethics à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œ à¦à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà§à¦°à¦¿ à¦¦à§‡à¦¬à§‡à¦¨à¥¤
    """
    exam = models.ForeignKey(Exam, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='cocurricular_entries')
    student = models.ForeignKey(StudentProfile, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='cocurricular_entries')
    section = models.ForeignKey(AcademicSection, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='cocurricular_entries')

    attendance_marks = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=0.00, help_text="à¦¡à¦¿à¦œà¦¿à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦² à¦¹à¦¾à¦œà¦¿à¦°à¦¾ à¦¥à§‡à¦•à§‡ à¦…à¦Ÿà§‹ à¦¹à¦¿à¦¸à¦¾à¦¬à¦•à§ƒà¦¤")
    ethics_marks = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=0.00, help_text="à¦¶à§à¦°à§‡à¦£à¦¿ à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œ à¦•à¦°à§à¦¤à§ƒà¦• à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¦à¦¤à§à¦¤")
    total_marks = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    
    grade_letter = models.CharField(max_length=5, default='F')
    grade_point = models.DecimalField(max_digits=4, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Co-Curricular Entry'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Co-Curricular Entries'
        unique_together = ('exam', 'student')

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        self.total_marks = round(self.attendance_marks + self.ethics_marks, 2)
        # à§§à§¨à§ª à¦¨à¦®à§à¦¬à¦°à§‡à¦° à¦“à¦ªà¦° à¦­à¦¿à¦¤à§à¦¤à¦¿ à¦•à¦°à§‡ à¦¶à¦¤à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à¦¿à¦¸à¦¾à¦¬: (à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¾à¦ªà§à¦¤ à¦¨à¦®à§à¦¬à¦° / à§§à§¨à§ª) * à§§à§¦à§¦
        scaled_score = (self.total_marks / Decimal('124.00')) * Decimal('100.00') if self.total_marks > 0 else Decimal('0.00')

        if scaled_score >= Decimal('80.00'):
            self.grade_letter, self.grade_point = 'A+', Decimal('5.00')
        elif scaled_score >= Decimal('70.00'):
            self.grade_letter, self.grade_point = 'A', Decimal('4.00')
        elif scaled_score >= Decimal('60.00'):
            self.grade_letter, self.grade_point = 'A-', Decimal('3.50')
        elif scaled_score >= Decimal('50.00'):
            self.grade_letter, self.grade_point = 'B', Decimal('3.00')
        elif scaled_score >= Decimal('40.00'):
            self.grade_letter, self.grade_point = 'C', Decimal('2.00')
        elif scaled_score >= Decimal('33.00'):
            self.grade_letter, self.grade_point = 'D', Decimal('1.00')
        else:
            self.grade_letter, self.grade_point = 'F', Decimal('0.00')

        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.student.roll_number} - Co-curricular: {self.total_marks} ({self.grade_letter})"


class StudentExamResult(BaseModel):
    """
    à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦° à¦¸à¦¾à¦®à¦—à§à¦°à¦¿à¦• à¦ªà¦°à§€à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦° à¦šà§‚à§œà¦¾à¦¨à§à¦¤ à¦«à¦²à¦¾à¦«à¦², à¦œà¦¿à¦ªà¦¿à¦, à¦®à§‹à¦Ÿ à¦¨à¦®à§à¦¬à¦° à¦à¦¬à¦‚ à¦®à§‡à¦§à¦¾ à¦¸à§à¦¥à¦¾à¦¨ (Merit Position)à¥¤
    """
    exam = models.ForeignKey(Exam, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='processed_results')
    student = models.ForeignKey(StudentProfile, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='exam_results')
    section = models.ForeignKey(AcademicSection, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='exam_results')

    total_full_marks = models.DecimalField(max_digits=7, decimal_places=2, default=824.00, help_text="à§­à§¦à§¦ + à§§à§¨à§ª = à§®à§¨à§ª")
    total_obtained_marks = models.DecimalField(max_digits=7, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    
    gpa = models.DecimalField(max_digits=4, decimal_places=2, default=0.00, db_index=True)
    grade_letter = models.CharField(max_length=5, default='F', db_index=True)
    is_passed = models.BooleanField(default=False, db_index=True)

    failed_subjects_count = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0)
    failed_subject_names = models.TextField(blank=True, help_text="à¦…à¦•à§ƒà¦¤à¦•à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¯ à¦¬à¦¿à¦·à§Ÿà§‡à¦° à¦•à¦®à¦¾-à¦¸à§‡à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à§‡à¦Ÿà§‡à¦¡ à¦¨à¦¾à¦®")

    # à¦®à§‡à¦§à¦¾ à¦¤à¦¾à¦²à¦¿à¦•à¦¾ à¦¸à§à¦¥à¦¾à¦¨
    section_position = models.PositiveIntegerField(null=True, blank=True, db_index=True)
    class_position = models.PositiveIntegerField(null=True, blank=True, db_index=True)

    # à¦ªà¦°à§€à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦° à¦¸à¦®à§Ÿà¦¸à§€à¦®à¦¾à¦° à¦®à¦§à§à¦¯à¦•à¦¾à¦° à¦‰à¦ªà¦¸à§à¦¥à¦¿à¦¤à¦¿ à¦¸à¦¾à¦®à¦¾à¦°à¦¿
    working_days = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0)
    present_days = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0)
    absent_days = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0)

    processed_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Student Exam Result'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Student Exam Results'
        unique_together = ('exam', 'student')
        ordering = ['-is_passed', '-gpa', '-total_obtained_marks', 'student__roll_number']
        indexes = [
            # à¦°à§‡à¦œà¦¾à¦²à§à¦Ÿà§‡à¦° à¦¦à¦¿à¦¨ à¦à¦• à¦•à§à¦²à¦¿à¦•à§‡ à¦®à§‡à¦§à¦¾ à¦¤à¦¾à¦²à¦¿à¦•à¦¾ à¦²à§‹à¦¡ à¦¹à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾
            models.Index(fields=['exam', 'is_passed', '-gpa']),
            models.Index(fields=['section', 'is_passed']),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.student.roll_number} - {self.student.user.get_full_name()} | GPA: {self.gpa} ({self.grade_letter}) - Pos: Sec {self.section_position}, Class {self.class_position}"

class InvigilationDuty(BaseModel):

        verbose_name = 'Invigilation Duty'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Invigilation Duties'
        ordering = ['duty_date', 'start_time']

    def __str__(self):

class QuestionPaperSubmission(BaseModel):
    """
    à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦• à¦•à¦°à§à¦¤à§ƒà¦• à¦ªà¦¾à¦¸à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾à¦°à§à¦¡-à¦ªà§à¦°à¦Ÿà§‡à¦•à§à¦Ÿà§‡à¦¡ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¶à§à¦¨à¦ªà¦¤à§à¦° à¦†à¦ªà¦²à§‹à¦¡ à¦“ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦¸à¦¿à¦ªà¦¾à¦²à§‡à¦° à¦¤à¦¦à¦¾à¦°à¦•à¦¿à¥¤
    """
    class PaperStatus(models.TextChoices):
        SUBMITTED = 'SUBMITTED', 'à¦œà¦®à¦¾ à¦¦à§‡à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡ (Submitted)'
        ACCEPTED = 'ACCEPTED', 'à¦—à§ƒà¦¹à§€à¦¤ (Accepted)'
        REVISION_REQUIRED = 'REVISION', 'à¦¸à¦‚à¦¶à§‹à¦§à¦¨ à¦ªà§à¦°à§Ÿà§‹à¦œà¦¨ (Revision Required)'

    exam = models.ForeignKey(Exam, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='question_papers')
    subject = models.ForeignKey(Subject, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='question_papers')
    teacher = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='submitted_questions')
    
    question_file = models.FileField(upload_to='question_papers/%Y/', help_text="à¦ªà¦¾à¦¸à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾à¦°à§à¦¡ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦Ÿà§‡à¦•à§à¦Ÿà§‡à¦¡ PDF à¦¬à¦¾ Doc à¦«à¦¾à¦‡à¦²")
    file_password_hint = models.CharField(max_length=150, blank=True, help_text="à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¶à§à¦¨à¦ªà¦¤à§à¦°à§‡à¦° à¦ªà¦¾à¦¸à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾à¦°à§à¦¡ (à¦¶à§à¦§à§à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦¸à¦¿à¦ªà¦¾à¦² à¦¦à§‡à¦–à¦¤à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦¬à§‡à¦¨)")
    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=PaperStatus.choices, default=PaperStatus.SUBMITTED)
    principal_remarks = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Question Paper Submission'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Question Paper Submissions'
        unique_together = ('exam', 'subject')

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.subject.name} - {self.exam.name} ({self.status})"

class ExamSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    class_level_display = serializers.CharField(source='get_class_level_display', read_only=True)
    exam_type_display = serializers.CharField(source='get_exam_type_display', read_only=True)

    class Meta:
        model = Exam
        fields = [
            'id',
            'name',
            'exam_type',
            'exam_type_display',
            'session',
            'class_level',
            'class_level_display',
            'start_date',
            'end_date',
            'has_tutorial_conversion',
            'exam_weight_percentage',
            'tutorial_weight_percentage',
            'is_published',
            'created_at',
        ]
        read_only_fields = ['id', 'created_at']


class ExamSubjectConfigSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):

class BulkSubjectMarksEntrySerializer(serializers.Serializer):
    """
    à¦¬à¦¿à¦·à§Ÿà¦­à¦¿à¦¤à§à¦¤à¦¿à¦• à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦• à¦•à¦°à§à¦¤à§ƒà¦• à¦•à§à¦²à¦¾à¦¸à§‡à¦° à¦¸à¦¬ à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§à¦¡à§‡à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà§‡à¦° CQ, MCQ, Practical, Tutorial à¦à¦• à¦•à§à¦²à¦¿à¦•à§‡ à¦à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà§à¦°à¦¿ à¦•à¦°à¦¾à¦° à¦¸à¦¿à¦°à¦¿à§Ÿà¦¾à¦²à¦¾à¦‡à¦œà¦¾à¦°à¥¤
    """
    exam_id = serializers.UUIDField()
    subject_id = serializers.UUIDField()
    section_id = serializers.UUIDField()
    marks_list = SingleStudentMarkItemSerializer(many=True)

    def validate(self, attrs):
        try:
            exam = Exam.objects.get(id=attrs['exam_id'])
        except Exam.DoesNotExist:
            raise serializers.ValidationError({"exam_id": "à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¦à¦¤à§à¦¤ à¦ªà¦°à§€à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦–à§à¦à¦œà§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾ à¦¯à¦¾à§Ÿà¦¨à¦¿à¥¤"})
        try:
            subject = Subject.objects.get(id=attrs['subject_id'])
        except Subject.DoesNotExist:
            raise serializers.ValidationError({"subject_id": "à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¦à¦¤à§à¦¤ à¦¬à¦¿à¦·à§Ÿà¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦–à§à¦à¦œà§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾ à¦¯à¦¾à§Ÿà¦¨à¦¿à¥¤"})
        try:
            section = AcademicSection.objects.get(id=attrs['section_id'])
        except AcademicSection.DoesNotExist:
            raise serializers.ValidationError({"section_id": "à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¦à¦¤à§à¦¤ à¦¶à§à¦°à§‡à¦£à¦¿/à¦¶à¦¾à¦–à¦¾à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦–à§à¦à¦œà§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾ à¦¯à¦¾à§Ÿà¦¨à¦¿à¥¤"})

        if section.class_level != exam.class_level or section.session != exam.session:
            raise serializers.ValidationError({"section_id": "à¦à¦‡ à¦¶à¦¾à¦–à¦¾à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦ªà¦°à§€à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦° à¦¶à§à¦°à§‡à¦£à¦¿ à¦¬à¦¾ à¦¸à§‡à¦¶à¦¨à§‡à¦° à¦…à¦¨à§à¦¤à¦°à§à¦­à§à¦•à§à¦¤ à¦¨à§Ÿà¥¤"})
        try:
            config = ExamSubjectConfig.objects.get(exam=exam, subject=subject)
        except ExamSubjectConfig.DoesNotExist:
            raise serializers.ValidationError({"subject_id": "à¦à¦‡ à¦¬à¦¿à¦·à§Ÿà¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦ªà¦°à§€à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ à¦•à¦¨à¦«à¦¿à¦—à¦¾à¦° à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà¦¨à¦¿à¥¤"})

        if self.context.get('request') and self.context['request'].user.role == User.Role.TEACHER:
            if section.in_charge_teacher_id != self.context['request'].user.id:
                raise serializers.ValidationError({"section_id": "à¦¶à§à¦§à§à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° à¦¨à¦¿à¦œà§‡à¦° à¦¦à¦¾à§Ÿà¦¿à¦¤à§à¦¬à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¾à¦ªà§à¦¤ à¦¶à¦¾à¦–à¦¾à¦° à¦¨à¦®à§à¦¬à¦° à¦à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà§à¦°à¦¿ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¯à¦¾à¦¬à§‡à¥¤"})

        for item in attrs['marks_list']:
            student = StudentProfile.objects.filter(
                id=item['student_id'], section=section, user__is_active=True
            ).first()
            if not student:
                raise serializers.ValidationError({"marks_list": "à¦¤à¦¾à¦²à¦¿à¦•à¦¾à¦° à¦¸à¦¬ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€ à¦¨à¦¿à¦°à§à¦¬à¦¾à¦šà¦¿à¦¤ à¦¶à¦¾à¦–à¦¾à¦° à¦…à¦¨à§à¦¤à¦°à§à¦­à§à¦•à§à¦¤ à¦¹à¦¤à§‡ à¦¹à¦¬à§‡à¥¤"})
            limits = {
                'cq_marks': config.cq_full_marks,
                'mcq_marks': config.mcq_full_marks,
                'practical_marks': config.practical_full_marks,
                'tutorial_marks': config.tutorial_full_marks,
            }
            for field, maximum in limits.items():
                if item[field] < 0 or item[field] > maximum:
                    raise serializers.ValidationError({
                        "marks_list": f"{field} à¦à¦° à¦¨à¦®à§à¦¬à¦° à§¦ à¦¥à§‡à¦•à§‡ {maximum} à¦à¦° à¦®à¦§à§à¦¯à§‡ à¦¹à¦¤à§‡ à¦¹à¦¬à§‡à¥¤"
                    })
        return attrs

    @transaction.atomic
    def save_marks(self, teacher_user):
        exam = Exam.objects.get(id=self.validated_data['exam_id'])
        subject = Subject.objects.get(id=self.validated_data['subject_id'])
        section = AcademicSection.objects.get(id=self.validated_data['section_id'])
        marks_list = self.validated_data['marks_list']

        saved_entries = []
        for item in marks_list:
            entry, _ = SubjectMarksEntry.objects.update_or_create(
                exam=exam,
                subject=subject,
                student_id=item['student_id'],
                defaults={
                    'section': section,
                    'cq_marks': item.get('cq_marks', Decimal('0.00')),
                    'mcq_marks': item.get('mcq_marks', Decimal('0.00')),
                    'practical_marks': item.get('practical_marks', Decimal('0.00')),
                    'tutorial_marks': item.get('tutorial_marks', Decimal('0.00')),
                    'is_absent': item.get('is_absent', False),
                    'entered_by': teacher_user,
                }
            )
            saved_entries.append(entry)

        return saved_entries


class SingleEthicsItemSerializer(serializers.Serializer):
    student_id = serializers.UUIDField()
    ethics_marks = serializers.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)


class BulkCoCurricularEntrySerializer(serializers.Serializer):
    """
    à¦¶à§à¦°à§‡à¦£à¦¿ à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œ à¦•à¦°à§à¦¤à§ƒà¦• Co-curricular à¦¨à¦®à§à¦¬à¦° à¦¤à§ˆà¦°à¦¿:
    à¦¹à¦¾à¦œà¦¿à¦°à¦¾ à¦¸à§à¦¬à§Ÿà¦‚à¦•à§à¦°à¦¿à§Ÿà¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ à¦¡à¦¿à¦œà¦¿à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦² à¦¹à¦¾à¦œà¦¿à¦°à¦¾ à¦¥à§‡à¦•à§‡ à¦•à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦²à¦•à§à¦²à§‡à¦Ÿ à¦¹à¦¬à§‡ à¦à¦¬à¦‚ à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œà§‡à¦° à¦ªà¦¾à¦ à¦¾à¦¨à§‹ Ethics à¦¨à¦®à§à¦¬à¦° à¦¯à§à¦•à§à¦¤ à¦¹à¦¬à§‡à¥¤
    """
    exam_id = serializers.UUIDField()
    section_id = serializers.UUIDField()
    ethics_list = SingleEthicsItemSerializer(many=True)

    def validate(self, attrs):
        try:
            exam = Exam.objects.get(id=attrs['exam_id'])
        except Exam.DoesNotExist:
            raise serializers.ValidationError({"exam_id": "à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¦à¦¤à§à¦¤ à¦ªà¦°à§€à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦–à§à¦à¦œà§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾ à¦¯à¦¾à§Ÿà¦¨à¦¿à¥¤"})
        try:
            section = AcademicSection.objects.get(id=attrs['section_id'])
        except AcademicSection.DoesNotExist:
            raise serializers.ValidationError({"section_id": "à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¦à¦¤à§à¦¤ à¦¶à§à¦°à§‡à¦£à¦¿/à¦¶à¦¾à¦–à¦¾à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦–à§à¦à¦œà§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾ à¦¯à¦¾à§Ÿà¦¨à¦¿à¥¤"})

        if section.class_level != exam.class_level or section.session != exam.session:
            raise serializers.ValidationError({"section_id": "à¦à¦‡ à¦¶à¦¾à¦–à¦¾à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦ªà¦°à§€à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦° à¦¶à§à¦°à§‡à¦£à¦¿ à¦¬à¦¾ à¦¸à§‡à¦¶à¦¨à§‡à¦° à¦…à¦¨à§à¦¤à¦°à§à¦­à§à¦•à§à¦¤ à¦¨à§Ÿà¥¤"})

        request = self.context.get('request')
        if request and request.user.role == User.Role.TEACHER:
            if section.in_charge_teacher_id != request.user.id:
                raise serializers.ValidationError({
                    "section_id": "à¦¶à§à¦§à§à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° à¦¨à¦¿à¦œà§‡à¦° à¦¦à¦¾à§Ÿà¦¿à¦¤à§à¦¬à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¾à¦ªà§à¦¤ à¦¶à¦¾à¦–à¦¾à¦° à¦¨à¦®à§à¦¬à¦° à¦à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà§à¦°à¦¿ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¯à¦¾à¦¬à§‡à¥¤"
                })

        for item in attrs['ethics_list']:
            if not StudentProfile.objects.filter(
                id=item['student_id'], section=section, user__is_active=True
            ).exists():
                raise serializers.ValidationError({
                    "ethics_list": "à¦¤à¦¾à¦²à¦¿à¦•à¦¾à¦° à¦¸à¦¬ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€ à¦¨à¦¿à¦°à§à¦¬à¦¾à¦šà¦¿à¦¤ à¦¶à¦¾à¦–à¦¾à¦° à¦…à¦¨à§à¦¤à¦°à§à¦­à§à¦•à§à¦¤ à¦¹à¦¤à§‡ à¦¹à¦¬à§‡à¥¤"
                })
        return attrs

    @transaction.atomic
    def save_co_curricular(self):
        exam = Exam.objects.get(id=self.validated_data['exam_id'])
        section = AcademicSection.objects.get(id=self.validated_data['section_id'])
        ethics_list = self.validated_data['ethics_list']

        # à¦ªà¦°à§€à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦° à¦¸à¦®à§Ÿà¦¸à§€à¦®à¦¾à¦° à¦®à¦§à§à¦¯à¦•à¦¾à¦° à¦®à§‹à¦Ÿ à¦•à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¯à¦¦à¦¿à¦¬à¦¸ à¦“ à¦‰à¦ªà¦¸à§à¦¥à¦¿à¦¤à¦¿à¦° à¦¤à¦¥à§à¦¯ à¦¸à¦‚à¦—à§à¦°à¦¹
        total_days = StudentAttendance.objects.filter(
            section=section,
            date__range=[exam.start_date, exam.end_date]
        ).values('date').distinct().count()

        saved_entries = []
        for item in ethics_list:
            student = StudentProfile.objects.get(id=item['student_id'])
            
            # à¦¡à¦¿à¦œà¦¿à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦² à¦¹à¦¾à¦œà¦¿à¦°à¦¾ à¦¥à§‡à¦•à§‡ à¦¸à§à¦¬à§Ÿà¦‚à¦•à§à¦°à¦¿à§Ÿ à¦‰à¦ªà¦¸à§à¦¥à¦¿à¦¤à¦¿ à¦—à¦£à¦¨à¦¾
            if total_days > 0:
                present_days = StudentAttendance.objects.filter(
                    student=student,
                    date__range=[exam.start_date, exam.end_date],
                    status__in=[StudentAttendance.AttendanceStatus.PRESENT, StudentAttendance.AttendanceStatus.LATE]
                ).count()
                # à¦¸à¦°à§à¦¬à§‹à¦šà§à¦š à§¬à§¦ à¦¨à¦®à§à¦¬à¦°à§‡à¦° à¦®à¦§à§à¦¯à§‡ à¦¹à¦¾à¦œà¦¿à¦°à¦¾à¦° à¦¸à§à¦•à§‡à¦²à¦¿à¦‚
                calculated_attn = round((Decimal(present_days) / Decimal(total_days)) * Decimal('60.00'), 1)
            else:
                calculated_attn = Decimal('0.00')

            cocurricular, _ = CoCurricularEntry.objects.update_or_create(
                exam=exam,
                student=student,
                defaults={
                    'section': section,
                    'attendance_marks': calculated_attn,
                    'ethics_marks': item.get('ethics_marks', Decimal('0.00')),
                }
            )
            saved_entries.append(cocurricular)

        return saved_entries

from datetime import date
from decimal import Decimal

from django.test import TestCase
from django.db import connection
from django.test.utils import CaptureQueriesContext
from rest_framework.test import APIRequestFactory, force_authenticate

from apps.academics.models import AcademicSection, Subject, StudentAttendance
from apps.examination.models import (
    Exam,
    ExamSubjectConfig,
    QuestionPaperSubmission,
    StudentExamResult,
    SubjectMarksEntry,
)
from apps.examination.views import (
    MarksEntryViewSet,
    QuestionPaperSubmissionViewSet,
    StudentExamResultViewSet,
)
from apps.users_auth.models import StudentProfile, User


class SubjectMarksEntryTests(TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.teacher = User.objects.create_user(
            username='teacher',
            password='password',
            role=User.Role.TEACHER,
        )
        self.student_user = User.objects.create_user(
            username='student',
            password='password',
            role=User.Role.STUDENT,
        )
        self.section = AcademicSection.objects.create(
            class_level=AcademicSection.ClassLevel.XI,
            group=AcademicSection.GroupChoices.SCIENCE,
            section_name='A',
            session='2026-2027',
            in_charge_teacher=self.teacher,
        )
        self.student = StudentProfile.objects.create(
            user=self.student_user,
            section=self.section,
            roll_number=1,
            session='2026-2027',
            father_name='Father',
            mother_name='Mother',
            guardian_phone='01700000000',
        )
        self.subject = Subject.objects.create(
            name='Physics',
            code='174',
            class_level=AcademicSection.ClassLevel.XI,
            group=AcademicSection.GroupChoices.SCIENCE,
        )
        self.exam = Exam.objects.create(
            name='Semester 1',
            session='2026-2027',
            class_level=AcademicSection.ClassLevel.XI,
            start_date=date(2026, 1, 1),
            end_date=date(2026, 1, 10),
            has_tutorial_conversion=False,
        )
        ExamSubjectConfig.objects.create(
            exam=self.exam,
            subject=self.subject,
            full_marks=Decimal('100.00'),
            cq_full_marks=Decimal('100.00'),
            mcq_full_marks=Decimal('0.00'),
            practical_full_marks=Decimal('0.00'),
            tutorial_full_marks=Decimal('0.00'),
            pass_marks=Decimal('60.00'),
        )

    def test_configured_pass_marks_control_pass_status(self):
        entry = SubjectMarksEntry.objects.create(
            exam=self.exam,
            subject=self.subject,
            student=self.student,
            section=self.section,
            cq_marks=Decimal('50.00'),
        )

        self.assertEqual(entry.grade_letter, 'B')
        self.assertFalse(entry.is_passed)


class ExaminationSecurityTests(SubjectMarksEntryTests):
    def setUp(self):
        super().setUp()
        self.other_teacher = User.objects.create_user(
            username='other-teacher',
            password='password',
            role=User.Role.TEACHER,
        )
        self.other_section = AcademicSection.objects.create(
            class_level=AcademicSection.ClassLevel.XI,
            group=AcademicSection.GroupChoices.SCIENCE,
            section_name='B',
            session='2026-2027',
            in_charge_teacher=self.other_teacher,
        )
        self.factory = APIRequestFactory()

    def test_teacher_cannot_read_another_teachers_question_paper(self):
        paper = QuestionPaperSubmission.objects.create(
            exam=self.exam,
            subject=self.subject,
            teacher=self.other_teacher,
            question_file='question_papers/test.pdf',
            file_password_hint='secret',
        )
        request = self.factory.get('/api/v1/examination/questions/')
        force_authenticate(request, user=self.teacher)
        response = QuestionPaperSubmissionViewSet.as_view({'get': 'list'})(request)

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(response.data['count'], 0)
        self.assertNotIn(str(paper.id), str(response.data))

    def test_teacher_cannot_read_another_sections_mark_sheet(self):
        request = self.factory.get('/api/v1/examination/marks/mark-sheet/', {
            'exam': str(self.exam.id),
            'subject': str(self.subject.id),
            'section': str(self.other_section.id),
        })
        force_authenticate(request, user=self.teacher)
        response = MarksEntryViewSet.as_view({'get': 'get_mark_sheet'})(request)

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 403)

    def test_teacher_cannot_write_cocurricular_marks_for_another_section(self):
        request = self.factory.post('/api/v1/examination/marks/save-cocurricular/', {
            'exam_id': str(self.exam.id),
            'section_id': str(self.other_section.id),
            'ethics_list': [],
        }, format='json')
        force_authenticate(request, user=self.teacher)
        response = MarksEntryViewSet.as_view({'post': 'save_cocurricular'})(request)

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 400)
        self.assertIn('section_id', response.data)


class ResultProcessingTests(SubjectMarksEntryTests):
    def setUp(self):
        super().setUp()
        self.principal = User.objects.create_user(
            username='principal',
            password='password',
            role=User.Role.PRINCIPAL,
        )

    def test_process_creates_and_reprocesses_result(self):
        SubjectMarksEntry.objects.create(
            exam=self.exam,
            subject=self.subject,
            student=self.student,
            section=self.section,
            cq_marks=Decimal('80.00'),
        )
        request = APIRequestFactory().post(
            '/api/v1/examination/results/process/',
            {'exam_id': str(self.exam.id), 'section_id': str(self.section.id)},
            format='json',
        )
        force_authenticate(request, user=self.principal)
        with CaptureQueriesContext(connection) as queries:
            response = StudentExamResultViewSet.as_view(
                {'post': 'process_result'}
            )(request)

        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertLessEqual(len(queries), 30)
        result = StudentExamResult.objects.get(exam=self.exam, student=self.student)
        self.assertTrue(result.is_passed)
        self.assertEqual(result.section_position, 1)

        request = APIRequestFactory().post(
            '/api/v1/examination/results/process/',
            {'exam_id': str(self.exam.id), 'section_id': str(self.section.id)},
            format='json',
        )
        force_authenticate(request, user=self.principal)
        response = StudentExamResultViewSet.as_view(
            {'post': 'process_result'}
        )(request)
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(
            StudentExamResult.objects.filter(
                exam=self.exam, student=self.student
            ).count(),
            1,
        )

from django.urls import include, path
from rest_framework.routers import DefaultRouter
from apps.examination.views import (
    ExamRoomViewSet,
    ExamSubjectConfigViewSet,
    ExamViewSet,
    ExaminationReportsViewSet,
    InvigilationDutyViewSet,
    MarksEntryViewSet,
    QuestionPaperSubmissionViewSet,
    StudentExamResultViewSet,
)

app_name = 'examination'

router = DefaultRouter()
router.register(r'exams', ExamViewSet, basename='exam')
router.register(r'configs', ExamSubjectConfigViewSet, basename='exam-config')
router.register(r'marks', MarksEntryViewSet, basename='marks')
router.register(r'results', StudentExamResultViewSet, basename='result')
router.register(r'rooms', ExamRoomViewSet, basename='exam-room')
router.register(r'invigilations', InvigilationDutyViewSet, basename='invigilation')
router.register(r'questions', QuestionPaperSubmissionViewSet, basename='question-paper')
router.register(r'reports', ExaminationReportsViewSet, basename='report')

urlpatterns = [
    path('', include(router.urls)),
]

class ExamViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    """
    à¦ªà¦°à§€à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾ à¦¤à§ˆà¦°à¦¿ à¦“ à¦•à¦¨à¦«à¦¿à¦—à¦¾à¦°à§‡à¦¶à¦¨ à¦­à¦¿à¦‰à¦¸à§‡à¦Ÿ (à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦¸à¦¿à¦ªà¦¾à¦² à¦ªà¦°à¦¿à¦šà¦¾à¦²à¦¿à¦¤)à¥¤
    """
    queryset = Exam.objects.all()
    serializer_class = ExamSerializer

    def get_permissions(self):
        if self.action in ['list', 'retrieve', 'my_admit_card']:
            return [IsAuthenticated()]
        return [IsPrincipal()]

    def perform_create(self, serializer):
        serializer.save(created_by=self.request.user)

    @action(detail=True, methods=['get'], url_path='my-admit-card')
    def my_admit_card(self, request, pk=None):
        """
        GET /api/v1/examination/exams/<id>/my-admit-card/
        à¦¸à§à¦®à¦¾à¦°à§à¦Ÿ à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¡à¦®à¦¿à¦Ÿ à¦•à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¡ à¦‡à¦žà§à¦œà¦¿à¦¨ (Due-Lock à¦²à¦œà¦¿à¦•):
        à¦•à§‹à¦¨à§‹ à¦¬à¦•à§‡à§Ÿà¦¾ à¦¥à¦¾à¦•à¦²à§‡ à¦¸à¦°à¦¾à¦¸à¦°à¦¿ 403 Forbidden à¦¸à¦¹ à¦²à¦¾à¦² à¦¸à¦¤à¦°à§à¦•à¦¬à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¤à¦¾ à¦†à¦¸à¦¬à§‡à¥¤
        """
        if request.user.role != User.Role.STUDENT or not hasattr(request.user, 'student_profile'):
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "à¦¶à§à¦§à§à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦°à¦¾ à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¡à¦®à¦¿à¦Ÿ à¦•à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¡ à¦ªà§‡à¦¤à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à§‡à¥¤"}, status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)

        exam = self.get_object()
        student = request.user.student_profile

        # à¦¬à¦•à§‡à§Ÿà¦¾ à¦šà§‡à¦• (Due-Lock Logic)
        total_due = StudentInvoice.objects.filter(
            student=student,
            due_amount__gt=Decimal('0.00')
        ).aggregate(total=Sum('due_amount'))['total'] or Decimal('0.00')

        if total_due > Decimal('0.00'):
            return Response({
                "success": False,
                "is_locked": True,
                "message": "à¦…à¦¨à§à¦—à§à¦°à¦¹à¦ªà§‚à¦°à§à¦¬à¦• à¦¤à§‹à¦®à¦¾à¦° à¦¶à§à¦°à§‡à¦£à¦¿ à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œà§‡à¦° à¦•à¦¾à¦›à§‡ à¦¬à¦•à§‡à§Ÿà¦¾ à¦ªà¦°à¦¿à¦¶à§‹à¦§ à¦•à¦°à§‡ à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¡à¦®à¦¿à¦Ÿ à¦•à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¡ à¦†à¦¨à¦²à¦• à¦•à¦°à§‹à¥¤",
                "total_due_amount": float(total_due),
                "warning": "à¦¬à¦•à§‡à§Ÿà¦¾ à¦«à¦¿ à¦ªà¦°à¦¿à¦¶à§‹à¦§ à¦¬à§à¦¯à¦¤à§€à¦¤ à¦ªà¦°à§€à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à§Ÿ à¦…à¦‚à¦¶à¦—à§à¦°à¦¹à¦£ à¦¨à¦¿à¦·à¦¿à¦¦à§à¦§à¥¤"
            }, status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)

        seat_assignment = ExamSeatAssignment.objects.filter(exam=exam, student=student).first()
        subject_configs = exam.subject_configs.select_related('subject')
        routine_slots = [
            {
                "subject_name": sc.subject.name,
                "subject_code": sc.subject.code,
                "full_marks": float(sc.full_marks),
            }
            for sc in subject_configs
        ]

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "is_locked": False,
            "institution": {
                "name": "STARLIGHT COLLEGE",
                "address": "Sunargown R/A, Dokshin surma, Sylhet",
                "eiin": "135342",
            },
            "admit_card": {
                "exam_name": exam.name,
                "session": exam.session,
                "student_name": student.user.get_full_name(),
                "student_id": student.student_unique_id,
                "roll_number": student.roll_number,
                "class_level": student.section.get_class_level_display(),
                "group": student.section.get_group_display(),
                "section": student.section.section_name,
                "seat_info": {
                    "room_number": seat_assignment.room.room_number if seat_assignment else "à¦¬à¦°à¦¾à¦¦à§à¦¦ à¦¹à§Ÿà¦¨à¦¿",
                    "seat_number": seat_assignment.seat_number if seat_assignment else "à¦…à¦ªà§‡à¦•à§à¦·à¦®à¦¾à¦£",
                },
                "subjects": routine_slots,
                "instructions": [
                    "à¦ªà¦°à§€à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾ à¦¶à§à¦°à§à¦° à§§à§« à¦®à¦¿à¦¨à¦¿à¦Ÿ à¦ªà§‚à¦°à§à¦¬à§‡ à¦ªà¦°à§€à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦° à¦¹à¦²à§‡ à¦‰à¦ªà¦¸à§à¦¥à¦¿à¦¤ à¦¹à¦¤à§‡ à¦¹à¦¬à§‡à¥¤",
                    "à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¡à¦®à¦¿à¦Ÿ à¦•à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¡ à¦›à¦¾à§œà¦¾ à¦•à§‹à¦¨à§‹ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦•à§‡ à¦ªà¦°à§€à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦° à¦¹à¦²à§‡ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¬à§‡à¦¶ à¦•à¦°à¦¤à§‡ à¦¦à§‡à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾ à¦¹à¦¬à§‡ à¦¨à¦¾à¥¤",
                    "à¦®à§‹à¦¬à¦¾à¦‡à¦² à¦«à§‹à¦¨ à¦¬à¦¾ à¦•à§‹à¦¨à§‹ à¦¡à¦¿à¦œà¦¿à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦² à¦¡à¦¿à¦­à¦¾à¦‡à¦¸ à¦¸à¦®à§à¦ªà§‚à¦°à§à¦£ à¦¨à¦¿à¦·à¦¿à¦¦à§à¦§à¥¤"
                ]
            }
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    @action(detail=True, methods=['post'], permission_classes=[IsPrincipal], url_path='generate-seat-plan')
    @transaction.atomic
    def generate_seat_plan(self, request, pk=None):
        """
        POST /api/v1/examination/exams/<id>/generate-seat-plan/
        à¦¸à§à¦¬à§Ÿà¦‚à¦•à§à¦°à¦¿à§Ÿ à¦¸à¦¿à¦Ÿ à¦ªà§à¦²à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¨ à¦œà§‡à¦¨à¦¾à¦°à§‡à¦Ÿà¦° (à¦°à§à¦®à§‡à¦° à¦§à¦¾à¦°à¦£à¦•à§à¦·à¦®à¦¤à¦¾ à¦…à¦¨à§à¦¯à¦¾à§Ÿà§€ à¦•à§à¦°à¦®à¦¾à¦¨à§à¦¸à¦¾à¦°à§‡ à¦†à¦¸à¦¨ à¦¬à¦£à§à¦Ÿà¦¨)à¥¤
        """
        exam = self.get_object()
        room_ids = request.data.get('room_ids', [])

        if not room_ids:
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "à¦•à¦®à¦ªà¦•à§à¦·à§‡ à¦à¦•à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦°à§à¦® à¦¨à¦¿à¦°à§à¦¬à¦¾à¦šà¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦†à¦¬à¦¶à§à¦¯à¦•à¥¤"}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        rooms = ExamRoom.objects.filter(id__in=room_ids, is_active=True).order_by('room_number')
        students = StudentProfile.objects.filter(
            section__class_level=exam.class_level,
            section__session=exam.session,
            user__is_active=True,
        ).order_by('roll_number')

        total_capacity = sum(r.capacity for r in rooms)
        if len(students) > total_capacity:
            return Response({
                "success": False,
                "message": f"à¦¨à¦¿à¦°à§à¦¬à¦¾à¦šà¦¿à¦¤ à¦°à§à¦®à¦—à§à¦²à§‹à¦¤à§‡ à¦®à§‹à¦Ÿ à¦†à¦¸à¦¨ {total_capacity}à¦Ÿà¦¿, à¦•à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦¤à§ à¦ªà¦°à§€à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€ à¦°à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡ {len(students)} à¦œà¦¨à¥¤ à¦†à¦°à¦“ à¦°à§à¦® à¦¯à§à¦•à§à¦¤ à¦•à¦°à§à¦¨à¥¤"
            }, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        ExamSeatAssignment.objects.filter(exam=exam).delete()

        student_idx = 0
        assigned_records = []

        for room in rooms:
            for seat_no in range(1, room.capacity + 1):
                if student_idx >= len(students):
                    break
                
                assignment = ExamSeatAssignment(
                    exam=exam,
                    student=students[student_idx],
                    room=room,
                    seat_number=seat_no
                )
                assigned_records.append(assignment)
                student_idx += 1

        ExamSeatAssignment.objects.bulk_create(assigned_records)

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": f"{exam.name} à¦à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ à¦®à§‹à¦Ÿ {len(assigned_records)} à¦œà¦¨ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦° à¦†à¦¸à¦¨ à¦¬à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¸ à¦¸à¦«à¦²à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ à¦¤à§ˆà¦°à¦¿ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤",
            "total_seated": len(assigned_records),
            "rooms_used": rooms.count()
        }, status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)


class ExamRoomViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    queryset = ExamRoom.objects.all()
    serializer_class = ExamRoomSerializer
    permission_classes = [IsPrincipal]


class InvigilationDutyViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    """
    à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦•à¦¦à§‡à¦° à¦¹à¦² à¦—à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¡ à¦°à§‹à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦° à¦­à¦¿à¦‰à¦¸à§‡à¦Ÿà¥¤
    """
    queryset = InvigilationDuty.objects.select_related('exam', 'room', 'teacher').all()
    serializer_class = InvigilationDutySerializer
    permission_classes = [IsTeacherOrPrincipal]

    def get_queryset(self):
        user = self.request.user
        queryset = super().get_queryset()
        if user.role == User.Role.TEACHER:
            return queryset.filter(teacher=user)
        return queryset


class QuestionPaperSubmissionViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    """
    à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦• à¦•à¦°à§à¦¤à§ƒà¦• à¦ªà¦¾à¦¸à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾à¦°à§à¦¡-à¦ªà§à¦°à¦Ÿà§‡à¦•à§à¦Ÿà§‡à¦¡ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¶à§à¦¨à¦ªà¦¤à§à¦° à¦†à¦ªà¦²à§‹à¦¡à¥¤
    """
    queryset = QuestionPaperSubmission.objects.select_related(
        'exam', 'subject', 'teacher'
    ).order_by('-created_at')
    serializer_class = QuestionPaperSubmissionSerializer
    permission_classes = [IsTeacherOrPrincipal]

    def get_queryset(self):
        queryset = super().get_queryset()
        if self.request.user.role == User.Role.TEACHER:
            return queryset.filter(teacher=self.request.user)
        return queryset

    def get_serializer_class(self):
        if self.request.user.role == User.Role.TEACHER:
            return TeacherQuestionPaperSubmissionSerializer
        return QuestionPaperSubmissionSerializer

    def perform_create(self, serializer):
        serializer.save(teacher=self.request.user)


class ExamSubjectConfigViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):

    à¦ªà¦°à§€à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦° à¦¬à¦¿à¦·à§Ÿà¦­à¦¿à¦¤à§à¦¤à¦¿à¦• à¦ªà§‚à¦°à§à¦£à¦®à¦¾à¦¨ à¦“ à¦ªà¦¾à¦¶ à¦¨à¦®à§à¦¬à¦° à¦¨à¦¿à¦°à§à¦§à¦¾à¦°à¦£à¥¤

    serializer_class = ExamSubjectConfigSerializer
    permission_classes = [IsPrincipal]


class MarksEntryViewSet(viewsets.GenericViewSet):
    permission_classes = [IsTeacherOrPrincipal]

    @action(detail=False, methods=['get'], url_path='mark-sheet')
    def get_mark_sheet(self, request):
        exam_id = request.query_params.get('exam')
        subject_id = request.query_params.get('subject')
        section_id = request.query_params.get('section')

        if not exam_id or not subject_id or not section_id:
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "exam, subject à¦à¦¬à¦‚ section à¦ªà§à¦¯à¦¾à¦°à¦¾à¦®à¦¿à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦° à¦ªà§à¦°à§Ÿà§‹à¦œà¦¨à¥¤"}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        exam = Exam.objects.filter(id=exam_id).first()
        section = AcademicSection.objects.filter(id=section_id).first()
        subject = Subject.objects.filter(id=subject_id).first()
        if not exam or not section or not subject:
            return Response(
                {"success": False, "message": "à¦ªà¦°à§€à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾, à¦¬à¦¿à¦·à§Ÿ à¦…à¦¥à¦¬à¦¾ à¦¶à¦¾à¦–à¦¾ à¦–à§à¦à¦œà§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾ à¦¯à¦¾à§Ÿà¦¨à¦¿à¥¤"},
                status=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            )
        if section.class_level != exam.class_level or section.session != exam.session:
            return Response(
                {"success": False, "message": "à¦à¦‡ à¦¶à¦¾à¦–à¦¾à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦ªà¦°à§€à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦° à¦…à¦¨à§à¦¤à¦°à§à¦­à§à¦•à§à¦¤ à¦¨à§Ÿà¥¤"},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )
        if not ExamSubjectConfig.objects.filter(exam=exam, subject=subject).exists():
            return Response(
                {"success": False, "message": "à¦à¦‡ à¦¬à¦¿à¦·à§Ÿà¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦ªà¦°à§€à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ à¦•à¦¨à¦«à¦¿à¦—à¦¾à¦° à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà¦¨à¦¿à¥¤"},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )
        if request.user.role == User.Role.TEACHER and section.in_charge_teacher_id != request.user.id:
            raise PermissionDenied("à¦¶à§à¦§à§à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° à¦¨à¦¿à¦œà§‡à¦° à¦¦à¦¾à§Ÿà¦¿à¦¤à§à¦¬à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¾à¦ªà§à¦¤ à¦¶à¦¾à¦–à¦¾à¦° mark sheet à¦¦à§‡à¦–à¦¾ à¦¯à¦¾à¦¬à§‡à¥¤")

        students = StudentProfile.objects.filter(section=section, user__is_active=True).select_related('user').order_by('roll_number')
        existing_marks = {
            m.student_id: m
            for m in SubjectMarksEntry.objects.filter(exam_id=exam_id, subject_id=subject_id, section_id=section_id)
        }

        sheet = []
        for s in students:
            m = existing_marks.get(s.id)
            sheet.append({
                "student_id": str(s.id),
                "student_unique_id": s.student_unique_id,
                "roll_number": s.roll_number,
                "student_name": s.user.get_full_name() or s.user.username,
                "cq_marks": float(m.cq_marks) if m else 0.0,
                "mcq_marks": float(m.mcq_marks) if m else 0.0,
                "practical_marks": float(m.practical_marks) if m else 0.0,
                "tutorial_marks": float(m.tutorial_marks) if m else 0.0,
                "final_total_marks": float(m.final_total_marks) if m else 0.0,
                "grade_letter": m.grade_letter if m else 'N/A',
                "is_absent": m.is_absent if m else False,
            })

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "total_students": len(sheet),
            "students": sheet
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    @action(detail=False, methods=['post'], url_path='bulk-save')
    def bulk_save_marks(self, request):
        serializer = BulkSubjectMarksEntrySerializer(
            data=request.data,
            context={'request': request},
        )
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)
        saved = serializer.save_marks(teacher_user=request.user)

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": f"à¦®à§‹à¦Ÿ {len(saved)} à¦œà¦¨ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦° à¦¨à¦®à§à¦¬à¦° à¦“ à¦—à§à¦°à§‡à¦¡ à¦¸à¦«à¦²à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ à¦¹à¦¿à¦¸à¦¾à¦¬ à¦•à¦°à§‡ à¦¸à¦‚à¦°à¦•à§à¦·à¦£ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤",
            "saved_count": len(saved)
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    @action(detail=False, methods=['post'], url_path='save-cocurricular')
    def save_cocurricular(self, request):
        serializer = BulkCoCurricularEntrySerializer(
            data=request.data,
            context={'request': request},
        )
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)
        saved = serializer.save_co_curricular()

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": f"à¦®à§‹à¦Ÿ {len(saved)} à¦œà¦¨ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦° à¦¸à¦¹-à¦ªà¦¾à¦ à§à¦¯à¦•à§à¦°à¦®à¦¿à¦• à¦•à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¯à¦•à§à¦°à¦® à¦«à¦²à¦¾à¦«à¦² à¦¸à¦«à¦²à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ à¦¤à§ˆà¦°à¦¿ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤",
            "saved_count": len(saved)
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class StudentExamResultViewSet(viewsets.ReadOnlyModelViewSet):
    queryset = StudentExamResult.objects.select_related('exam', 'student', 'student__user', 'section').all()
    serializer_class = StudentExamResultSerializer

    def get_permissions(self):
        if self.action == 'process_result':
            return [IsPrincipal()]
        return [IsAuthenticated()]

    def get_queryset(self):
        user = self.request.user
        queryset = super().get_queryset()

        if user.role == User.Role.STUDENT and hasattr(user, 'student_profile'):
            return queryset.filter(student=user.student_profile, exam__is_published=True)

        if user.role == User.Role.TEACHER:
            return queryset.filter(section__in_charge_teacher=user)

        exam_id = self.request.query_params.get('exam')
        if exam_id:
            queryset = queryset.filter(exam_id=exam_id)

        section_id = self.request.query_params.get('section')
        if section_id:
            queryset = queryset.filter(section_id=section_id)

        return queryset

    @action(detail=False, methods=['post'], url_path='process')
    @transaction.atomic
    def process_result(self, request):
        serializer = ResultProcessRequestSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        exam_id = serializer.validated_data['exam_id']
        section_id = serializer.validated_data.get('section_id')

        exam = Exam.objects.get(id=exam_id)
        students_qs = StudentProfile.objects.filter(
            section__class_level=exam.class_level,
            section__session=exam.session,
            user__is_active=True,
        )

        if section_id:
            students_qs = students_qs.filter(section_id=section_id)

        working_days = StudentAttendance.objects.filter(
            section__class_level=exam.class_level,
            section__session=exam.session,
            date__range=[exam.start_date, exam.end_date]
        ).values('date').distinct().count()

        config_full_marks = sum(
            config.full_marks for config in exam.subject_configs.all()
        ) + Decimal('124.00')
        attendance_prefetch = Prefetch(
            'attendances',
            queryset=StudentAttendance.objects.filter(
                date__range=[exam.start_date, exam.end_date]
            ),
            to_attr='exam_attendance',
        )
        students = list(
            students_qs.select_related('section').prefetch_related(
                Prefetch(
                    'subject_marks',
                    queryset=SubjectMarksEntry.objects.filter(exam=exam).select_related('subject'),
                    to_attr='exam_marks',
                ),
                Prefetch(
                    'cocurricular_entries',
                    queryset=CoCurricularEntry.objects.filter(exam=exam),
                    to_attr='exam_cocurricular',
                ),
                attendance_prefetch,
            )
        )
        existing_results = {
            result.student_id: result
            for result in StudentExamResult.objects.filter(exam=exam, student_id__in=[s.id for s in students])
        }
        to_create = []
        to_update = []
        processed_results = []
        for student in students:
            marks_entries = student.exam_marks
            cocurricular = student.exam_cocurricular[0] if student.exam_cocurricular else None

            total_obtained = sum(m.final_total_marks for m in marks_entries)
            failed_subjects = [m.subject.name for m in marks_entries if not m.is_passed]

            if cocurricular:
                total_obtained += cocurricular.total_marks
                if cocurricular.grade_letter == 'F':
                    failed_subjects.append('Co-curricular Activity')

            failed_count = len(failed_subjects)

            if failed_count > 0:
                is_passed = False
                final_gpa = Decimal('0.00')
                overall_grade = 'F'
            else:
                is_passed = True
                total_gp = sum(m.grade_point for m in marks_entries)
                total_subjects_count = len(marks_entries)

                if cocurricular:
                    total_gp += cocurricular.grade_point
                    total_subjects_count += 1

                final_gpa = round(Decimal(total_gp) / Decimal(total_subjects_count), 2) if total_subjects_count > 0 else Decimal('0.00')

                if final_gpa >= Decimal('5.00'):
                    overall_grade = 'A+'
                elif final_gpa >= Decimal('4.00'):
                    overall_grade = 'A'
                elif final_gpa >= Decimal('3.50'):
                    overall_grade = 'A-'
                elif final_gpa >= Decimal('3.00'):
                    overall_grade = 'B'
                elif final_gpa >= Decimal('2.00'):
                    overall_grade = 'C'
                elif final_gpa >= Decimal('1.00'):
                    overall_grade = 'D'
                else:
                    overall_grade = 'F'

            present_days = sum(
                attendance.status in [
                    StudentAttendance.AttendanceStatus.PRESENT,
                    StudentAttendance.AttendanceStatus.LATE,
                ]
                for attendance in student.exam_attendance
            )
            absent_days = sum(
                attendance.status == StudentAttendance.AttendanceStatus.ABSENT
                for attendance in student.exam_attendance
            )

            result_obj = existing_results.get(student.id)
            result_values = {
                    'section': student.section,
                    'total_full_marks': config_full_marks,
                    'total_obtained_marks': total_obtained,
                    'gpa': final_gpa,
                    'grade_letter': overall_grade,
                    'is_passed': is_passed,
                    'failed_subjects_count': failed_count,
                    'failed_subject_names': ", ".join(failed_subjects),
                    'working_days': working_days,
                    'present_days': present_days,
                    'absent_days': absent_days,
                    'section_position': None,
                    'class_position': None,
                }
            if result_obj:
                for field, value in result_values.items():
                    setattr(result_obj, field, value)
                to_update.append(result_obj)
            else:
                result_obj = StudentExamResult(
                    exam=exam,
                    student=student,
                    processed_at=timezone.now(),
                    **result_values,
                )
                to_create.append(result_obj)
            processed_results.append(result_obj)

        if to_create:
            StudentExamResult.objects.bulk_create(to_create)
        if to_update:
            StudentExamResult.objects.bulk_update(
                to_update,
                fields=[
                    'section', 'total_full_marks', 'total_obtained_marks', 'gpa',
                    'grade_letter', 'is_passed', 'failed_subjects_count',
                    'failed_subject_names', 'working_days', 'present_days',
                    'absent_days', 'section_position', 'class_position',
                ],
            )

        passed_in_class = StudentExamResult.objects.filter(
            exam=exam,
            is_passed=True
        ).order_by('-gpa', '-total_obtained_marks', 'student__roll_number')

        ranked_results = []
        for idx, res in enumerate(passed_in_class, start=1):
            res.class_position = idx
            ranked_results.append(res)
        if ranked_results:
            StudentExamResult.objects.bulk_update(ranked_results, ['class_position'])

        passed_by_section = {}
        for result in StudentExamResult.objects.filter(
            exam=exam,
            section__class_level=exam.class_level,
            section__session=exam.session,
            is_passed=True,
        ).select_related('section', 'student').order_by(
            'section_id', '-gpa', '-total_obtained_marks', 'student__roll_number'
        ):
            passed_by_section.setdefault(result.section_id, []).append(result)

        section_ranked_results = []
        for passed_in_sec in passed_by_section.values():
            for idx, res in enumerate(passed_in_sec, start=1):
                res.section_position = idx
                section_ranked_results.append(res)
        if section_ranked_results:
            StudentExamResult.objects.bulk_update(section_ranked_results, ['section_position'])

        total_passed = len(passed_in_class)
        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": f"{exam.name} à¦à¦° à¦«à¦²à¦¾à¦«à¦² à¦“ à¦®à§‡à¦§à¦¾ à¦¤à¦¾à¦²à¦¿à¦•à¦¾ à¦¸à¦«à¦²à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¸à§‡à¦¸ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤",
            "total_processed": len(processed_results),
            "total_passed": total_passed,
            "total_failed": len(processed_results) - total_passed
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    @action(detail=False, methods=['get'], url_path='my-result')
    def my_result(self, request):
        if request.user.role != User.Role.STUDENT or not hasattr(request.user, 'student_profile'):
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "à¦¶à§à¦§à§à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦°à¦¾ à¦à¦‡ à¦°à§‡à¦œà¦¾à¦²à§à¦Ÿ à¦¦à§‡à¦–à¦¤à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à§‡à¥¤"}, status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)

        exam_id = request.query_params.get('exam')
        if not exam_id:
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "exam à¦ªà§à¦¯à¦¾à¦°à¦¾à¦®à¦¿à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦° à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¦à¦¾à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦†à¦¬à¦¶à§à¦¯à¦•à¥¤"}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        result = StudentExamResult.objects.filter(
            exam_id=exam_id,
            student=request.user.student_profile,
            exam__is_published=True
        ).first()

        if not result:
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "à¦«à¦²à¦¾à¦«à¦² à¦à¦–à¦¨à§‹ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦•à¦¾à¦¶à¦¿à¦¤ à¦¹à§Ÿà¦¨à¦¿ à¦…à¦¥à¦¬à¦¾ à¦–à§à¦à¦œà§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾ à¦¯à¦¾à§Ÿà¦¨à¦¿à¥¤"}, status=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND)

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "data": StudentExamResultSerializer(result).data
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class ExaminationReportsViewSet(viewsets.ViewSet):
    """
    à¦ªà¦¿à¦¡à¦¿à¦à¦« à¦“ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦Ÿ à¦°à¦¿à¦ªà§‹à¦°à§à¦Ÿà§‡à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ à¦¡à§‡à¦¡à¦¿à¦•à§‡à¦Ÿà§‡à¦¡ à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¨à¦¾à¦²à¦¿à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦•à§à¦¸ à¦“ à¦®à¦¾à¦°à§à¦•à¦¶à¦¿à¦Ÿ à¦­à¦¿à¦‰à¦¸à§‡à¦Ÿà¥¤
    """
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    @action(detail=False, methods=['get'], url_path='progress-report')
    def progress_report(self, request):
        exam_id = request.query_params.get('exam')
        student_id = request.query_params.get('student')
        user = request.user

        if user.role == User.Role.STUDENT and hasattr(user, 'student_profile'):
            student_id = str(user.student_profile.id)

        if not exam_id or not student_id:
            return Response(
                {"success": False, "message": "exam à¦à¦¬à¦‚ student à¦†à¦‡à¦¡à¦¿ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¦à¦¾à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦†à¦¬à¦¶à§à¦¯à¦•à¥¤"},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST
            )

        try:
            exam = Exam.objects.get(id=exam_id)
            student = StudentProfile.objects.select_related('user', 'section').get(id=student_id)
        except (Exam.DoesNotExist, StudentProfile.DoesNotExist):
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "à¦ªà¦°à§€à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾ à¦…à¦¥à¦¬à¦¾ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€ à¦–à§à¦à¦œà§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾ à¦¯à¦¾à§Ÿà¦¨à¦¿à¥¤"}, status=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND)

        overall_result = StudentExamResult.objects.filter(exam=exam, student=student).first()
        subject_entries = SubjectMarksEntry.objects.filter(exam=exam, student=student).select_related('subject')

        subject_rows = []
        for entry in subject_entries:
            highest_mark = SubjectMarksEntry.objects.filter(
                exam=exam,
                subject=entry.subject,
                section__class_level=exam.class_level,
                section__session=exam.session,
            ).aggregate(max_mark=Max('final_total_marks'))['max_mark'] or Decimal('0.00')

            subject_rows.append({
                "subject_name": entry.subject.name,
                "subject_code": entry.subject.code,
                "full_marks": float(
                    exam.subject_configs.filter(subject=entry.subject).values_list(
                        'full_marks', flat=True
                    ).first() or Decimal('0.00')
                ),
                "cq": float(entry.cq_marks),
                "mcq": float(entry.mcq_marks),
                "practical": float(entry.practical_marks),
                "semester_obtained": float(entry.obtained_semester_marks),
                "semester_converted": float(entry.converted_semester_marks),
                "tutorial": float(entry.tutorial_marks),
                "total_marks": float(entry.final_total_marks),
                "letter_grade": entry.grade_letter,
                "grade_point": float(entry.grade_point),
                "highest_marks": float(highest_mark),
            })

        cocurricular = CoCurricularEntry.objects.filter(exam=exam, student=student).first()
        cocurricular_highest = CoCurricularEntry.objects.filter(
            exam=exam,
            section__class_level=exam.class_level,
            section__session=exam.session,
        ).aggregate(max_cocurr=Max('total_marks'))['max_cocurr'] or Decimal('0.00')

        cocurricular_data = {
            "title": "Co-curricular Activity",
            "full_marks": 124.0,
            "attn": float(cocurricular.attendance_marks) if cocurricular else 0.0,
            "ethics": float(cocurricular.ethics_marks) if cocurricular else 0.0,
            "total_marks": float(cocurricular.total_marks) if cocurricular else 0.0,
            "converted": float(cocurricular.total_marks) if cocurricular else 0.0,
            "letter_grade": cocurricular.grade_letter if cocurricular else 'F',
            "grade_point": float(cocurricular.grade_point) if cocurricular else 0.0,
            "highest_marks": float(cocurricular_highest),
        }

        summary_data = {
            "working_days": overall_result.working_days if overall_result else 0,
            "present_days": overall_result.present_days if overall_result else 0,
            "absent_days": overall_result.absent_days if overall_result else 0,
            "total_marks": float(overall_result.total_full_marks) if overall_result else 824.0,
            "total_obtained": float(overall_result.total_obtained_marks) if overall_result else 0.0,
            "grade": overall_result.grade_letter if overall_result else 'F',
            "gpa": float(overall_result.gpa) if overall_result else 0.00,
            "section_position": overall_result.section_position if overall_result else None,
            "class_position": overall_result.class_position if overall_result else None,
        }

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "institution": {
                "name": "STARLIGHT COLLEGE",
                "address": "Sunargown R/A, Dokshin surma, Sylhet",
                "eiin": "135342",
            },
            "exam_info": {
                "report_title": "Progress Report",
                "exam_name": exam.name,
                "session": exam.session,
            },
            "student_info": {
                "student_name": student.user.get_full_name() or student.user.username,
                "student_id": student.student_unique_id,
                "student_roll": student.roll_number,
                "version": "Bangla",
                "class_level": student.section.get_class_level_display(),
                "section": student.section.section_name,
                "gender": student.get_gender_display(),
                "group": student.section.get_group_display(),
            },
            "grading_scale": [
                {"mark_range": "80-100", "letter_grade": "A+", "grade_point": 5.0},
                {"mark_range": "70-79", "letter_grade": "A", "grade_point": 4.0},
                {"mark_range": "60-69", "letter_grade": "A-", "grade_point": 3.5},
                {"mark_range": "50-59", "letter_grade": "B", "grade_point": 3.0},
                {"mark_range": "40-49", "letter_grade": "C", "grade_point": 2.0},
                {"mark_range": "33-39", "letter_grade": "D", "grade_point": 1.0},
                {"mark_range": "00-32", "letter_grade": "F", "grade_point": 0.0},
            ],
            "subjects": subject_rows,
            "co_curricular": cocurricular_data,
            "overall_summary": summary_data,
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    @action(detail=False, methods=['get'], url_path='subject-analysis')
    def subject_analysis(self, request):
        exam_id = request.query_params.get('exam')
        group = request.query_params.get('group')

        if not exam_id:
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "exam à¦†à¦‡à¦¡à¦¿ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¦à¦¾à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦†à¦¬à¦¶à§à¦¯à¦•à¥¤"}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        exam = Exam.objects.get(id=exam_id)
        subjects_qs = Subject.objects.filter(exam_configs__exam=exam).distinct()
        if group:
            subjects_qs = subjects_qs.filter(group=group.upper())

        analysis_rows = []
        for idx, subj in enumerate(subjects_qs, start=1):
            entries = SubjectMarksEntry.objects.filter(exam_id=exam_id, subject=subj)
            total_students = entries.count()

            if total_students > 0:
                passed_count = entries.filter(is_passed=True).count()
                failed_count = entries.filter(is_passed=False).count()
                pass_pct = round((passed_count / total_students) * 100.0, 2)
                fail_pct = round((failed_count / total_students) * 100.0, 2)
            else:
                passed_count, failed_count, pass_pct, fail_pct = 0, 0, 0.0, 0.0

            analysis_rows.append({
                "sl": idx,
                "subject_name": subj.name,
                "subject_code": subj.code,
                "total_students": total_students,
                "number_of_student_passed": passed_count,
                "pass_percentage": f"{pass_pct}%",
                "number_of_student_failed": failed_count,
                "failed_percentage": f"{fail_pct}%",
            })

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "report_title": "SUBJECT WISE RESULT ANALYSIS",
            "exam_id": exam_id,
            "data": analysis_rows,
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    @action(detail=False, methods=['get'], url_path='failed-statistics')
    def failed_statistics(self, request):
        exam_id = request.query_params.get('exam')
        gender = request.query_params.get('gender', 'MALE').upper()

        if not exam_id:
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "exam à¦†à¦‡à¦¡à¦¿ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¦à¦¾à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦†à¦¬à¦¶à§à¦¯à¦•à¥¤"}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        failed_results = StudentExamResult.objects.filter(
            exam_id=exam_id,
            is_passed=False,
            student__gender=gender
        ).select_related('student', 'student__user').order_by('student__roll_number')

        failed_rows = []
        for idx, res in enumerate(failed_results, start=1):
            failed_rows.append({
                "sl": idx,
                "student_name": res.student.user.get_full_name() or res.student.user.username,
                "student_id": res.student.student_unique_id,
                "roll": res.student.roll_number,
                "failed_subjects": res.failed_subject_names,
                "total_failed_count": res.failed_subjects_count,
            })

        gender_label = "à¦›à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦°-à¦¸à¦•à¦² à¦—à§à¦°à§à¦ª" if gender == 'MALE' else "à¦›à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦°à§€-à¦¸à¦•à¦² à¦—à§à¦°à§à¦ª"

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "report_title": f"à¦¬à¦¿à¦·à§Ÿ à¦­à¦¿à¦¤à§à¦¤à¦¿à¦• à¦…à¦•à§ƒà¦¤à¦•à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¯ à¦¬à¦¿à¦¬à¦°à¦£à§€ ({gender_label})",
            "gender": gender,
            "total_failed_students": len(failed_rows),
            "data": failed_rows,
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    @action(detail=False, methods=['get'], url_path='merit-list')
    def merit_list(self, request):
        exam_id = request.query_params.get('exam')
        section_id = request.query_params.get('section')

        if not exam_id:
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "exam à¦†à¦‡à¦¡à¦¿ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¦à¦¾à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦†à¦¬à¦¶à§à¦¯à¦•à¥¤"}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        passed_results = StudentExamResult.objects.filter(
            exam_id=exam_id,
            is_passed=True
        ).select_related('student', 'student__user', 'section').order_by('class_position')

        if section_id:
            passed_results = passed_results.filter(section_id=section_id).order_by('section_position')

        merit_rows = []
        for idx, res in enumerate(passed_results, start=1):
            merit_rows.append({
                "sl": idx,
                "student_name": res.student.user.get_full_name() or res.student.user.username,
                "student_id": res.student.student_unique_id,
                "roll": res.student.roll_number,
                "section": res.section.section_name,
                "sub_total_full": float(res.total_full_marks),
                "obtained_marks": float(res.total_obtained_marks),
                "grade": res.grade_letter,
                "gpa": float(res.gpa),
                "section_position": res.section_position,
                "class_position": res.class_position,
            })

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "report_title": "OFFICIAL MERIT LIST",
            "total_passed": len(merit_rows),
            "data": merit_rows,
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

from django.contrib import admin
from apps.finance.models import (
    CollegeExpense,
    DriverAdvanceDeduction,
    ExpenseCategory,
    FineWaiverApplication,
    PaymentTransaction,
    StudentInvoice,
    TeacherPayroll,
    TeacherSalaryClaim,
    TransportVehicle,
    VehicleBreakdownAlert,
    VehicleDailyLog,
    VehicleMonthlyBill,
    BankDeposit,
    InchargeCashHandover,
    Supplier,
    SupplierPayment,
    SupplierPurchase,
)

@admin.register(StudentInvoice)
class StudentInvoiceAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ('invoice_number', 'student', 'month', 'year', 'total_amount', 'paid_amount', 'due_amount', 'status')
    list_filter = ('status', 'year', 'month')
    search_fields = ('invoice_number', 'student__student_unique_id')

@admin.register(PaymentTransaction)
class PaymentTransactionAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ('receipt_no', 'invoice', 'amount_paid', 'payment_method', 'transaction_reference', 'collected_by')
    list_filter = ('payment_method',)
    search_fields = ('receipt_no', 'transaction_reference')

@admin.register(FineWaiverApplication)
class FineWaiverApplicationAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):

    list_filter = ('incharge_status', 'principal_status')

@admin.register(ExpenseCategory)

@admin.register(CollegeExpense)

    list_filter = ('ran_today', 'date')

@admin.register(DriverAdvanceDeduction)
class DriverAdvanceDeductionAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ('vehicle', 'date', 'amount', 'purpose', 'recorded_by')
    list_filter = ('date',)

@admin.register(VehicleMonthlyBill)
class VehicleMonthlyBillAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ('bill_number', 'vehicle', 'month', 'year', 'total_days_run', 'gross_bill', 'total_deductions', 'net_payable', 'status')
    list_filter = ('status', 'year', 'month')



@admin.register(InchargeCashHandover)
class InchargeCashHandoverAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ('in_charge', 'handover_date', 'amount', 'status', 'received_by', 'received_at')
    list_filter = ('status', 'handover_date')
    search_fields = ('in_charge__username', 'in_charge__first_name')


@admin.register(BankDeposit)
class BankDepositAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ('bank_name', 'account_number', 'deposit_date', 'amount', 'slip_number', 'deposited_by')
    list_filter = ('bank_name', 'deposit_date')
    search_fields = ('account_number', 'slip_number')


@admin.register(Supplier)
class SupplierAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ('name', 'service_type', 'phone', 'contact_person', 'is_active')
    search_fields = ('name', 'phone')


@admin.register(SupplierPurchase)
class SupplierPurchaseAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ('invoice_no', 'supplier', 'purchase_date', 'total_bill', 'paid_amount', 'due_amount')
    list_filter = ('purchase_date', 'supplier')
    search_fields = ('invoice_no', 'supplier__name')


@admin.register(SupplierPayment)
class SupplierPaymentAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ('purchase', 'payment_date', 'amount_paid', 'payment_method', 'paid_by')
    list_filter = ('payment_date', 'payment_method')
