import uuid
from decimal import Decimal
from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError
from django.db import models
from django.utils import timezone
from apps.academics.models import AcademicSection
from apps.common.models import BaseModel
from apps.users_auth.models import StudentProfile, User

class StudentInvoice(BaseModel):
    """
    à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦° à¦®à¦¾à¦¸à¦¿à¦• à¦¬à§‡à¦¤à¦¨, à¦ªà¦°à¦¿à¦¬à¦¹à¦¨ à¦«à¦¿, à¦…à¦¨à§à¦ªà¦¸à§à¦¥à¦¿à¦¤à¦¿à¦° à¦œà¦°à¦¿à¦®à¦¾à¦¨à¦¾ à¦“ à¦¬à¦•à§‡à§Ÿà¦¾ à¦‡à¦¨à¦­à§Ÿà§‡à¦¸à¥¤
    """
    class InvoiceStatus(models.TextChoices):
        UNPAID = 'UNPAID', 'à¦…à¦ªà¦°à¦¿à¦¶à§‹à¦§à¦¿à¦¤ (Unpaid)'
        PARTIAL = 'PARTIAL', 'à¦†à¦‚à¦¶à¦¿à¦• à¦ªà¦°à¦¿à¦¶à§‹à¦§à¦¿à¦¤ (Partially Paid)'
        PAID = 'PAID', 'à¦¸à¦®à§à¦ªà§‚à¦°à§à¦£ à¦ªà¦°à¦¿à¦¶à§‹à¦§à¦¿à¦¤ (Paid)'

    invoice_number = models.CharField(max_length=50, unique=True, editable=False, db_index=True)
    student = models.ForeignKey(StudentProfile, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='invoices')
    section = models.ForeignKey(AcademicSection, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='invoices')
    
    month = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField(help_text="à¦®à¦¾à¦¸à§‡à¦° à¦•à§à¦°à¦®à¦¿à¦• à¦¨à¦®à§à¦¬à¦° (à§§ = à¦œà¦¾à¦¨à§à§Ÿà¦¾à¦°à¦¿, à§§à§¨ = à¦¡à¦¿à¦¸à§‡à¦®à§à¦¬à¦°)")
    year = models.PositiveIntegerField(help_text="à¦¯à§‡à¦®à¦¨: 2026")
    
    # à¦«à¦¿-à¦à¦° à¦–à¦¾à¦¤à¦¸à¦®à§‚à¦¹
    tuition_fee = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    transport_fee = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    absence_fine = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0.00, help_text="à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¤à¦¿à¦¦à¦¿à¦¨ à§§à§¦à§¦ à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦•à¦¾ à¦œà¦°à¦¿à¦®à¦¾à¦¨à¦¾")
    other_fees = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    
    # à¦®à¦“à¦•à§à¦« à¦¬à¦¾ à¦›à¦¾à§œ (à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œà§‡à¦° à¦¸à§à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à¦¿à¦¶à§‡ à¦…à¦§à§à¦¯à¦•à§à¦·à§‡à¦° à¦…à¦¨à§à¦®à§‹à¦¦à¦¨à§‡ à¦†à¦¸à¦¬à§‡)
    waiver_amount = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    
    # à¦†à¦°à§à¦¥à¦¿à¦• à¦®à§‹à¦Ÿ à¦à¦¬à¦‚ à¦ªà¦°à¦¿à¦¶à§‹à¦§ à¦¹à¦¿à¦¸à¦¾à¦¬
    total_amount = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    paid_amount = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    due_amount = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    
    status = models.CharField(max_length=15, choices=InvoiceStatus.choices, default=InvoiceStatus.UNPAID, db_index=True)
    due_date = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True)
    created_by = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Student Invoice'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Student Invoices'
        unique_together = ('student', 'month', 'year')
        ordering = ['-year', '-month', 'student__roll_number']
        indexes = [
            # à¦¬à¦•à§‡à§Ÿà¦¾ à¦“ à¦®à¦¾à¦¸à§‡à¦° à¦­à¦¿à¦¤à§à¦¤à¦¿à¦¤à§‡ à¦šà§‹à¦–à§‡à¦° à¦ªà¦²à¦•à§‡ à¦¸à¦¾à¦°à§à¦š
            models.Index(fields=['student', 'due_amount']),
            models.Index(fields=['year', 'month', 'status']),
        ]

    def calculate_totals(self):
        tuition_fee = Decimal(str(self.tuition_fee or 0))
        transport_fee = Decimal(str(self.transport_fee or 0))
        absence_fine = Decimal(str(self.absence_fine or 0))
        other_fees = Decimal(str(self.other_fees or 0))
        waiver_amount = Decimal(str(self.waiver_amount or 0))
        paid_amount = Decimal(str(self.paid_amount or 0))
        gross_total = tuition_fee + transport_fee + absence_fine + other_fees
        self.total_amount = max(Decimal('0.00'), gross_total - waiver_amount)
        self.due_amount = max(Decimal('0.00'), self.total_amount - paid_amount)

        if paid_amount >= self.total_amount and self.total_amount > Decimal('0.00'):
            self.status = self.InvoiceStatus.PAID
        elif paid_amount > Decimal('0.00'):
            self.status = self.InvoiceStatus.PARTIAL
        else:
            self.status = self.InvoiceStatus.UNPAID

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        if not self.invoice_number:
            unique_suffix = uuid.uuid4().hex[:6].upper()
            self.invoice_number = f"INV-{self.year}{self.month:02d}-{unique_suffix}"
        self.calculate_totals()
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.invoice_number} | {self.student.student_unique_id} - {self.month}/{self.year} ({self.status})"


class PaymentTransaction(BaseModel):
    """
    à¦«à¦¿ à¦œà¦®à¦¾à¦° à¦Ÿà§à¦°à¦¾à¦¨à¦œà§‡à¦•à¦¶à¦¨ à¦“ à¦®à¦¾à¦¨à¦¿ à¦°à¦¿à¦¸à¦¿à¦Ÿ (à¦•à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¶, à¦¬à¦¿à¦•à¦¾à¦¶, à¦¬à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦‚à¦• à¦‡à¦¤à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¦à¦¿)à¥¤
    """
    class PaymentMethod(models.TextChoices):
        CASH = 'CASH', 'à¦•à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¶ à¦•à¦¾à¦‰à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦° (Cash)'
        BKASH = 'BKASH', 'à¦¬à¦¿à¦•à¦¾à¦¶ (bKash)'
        NAGAD = 'NAGAD', 'à¦¨à¦—à¦¦ (Nagad)'
        BANK = 'BANK', 'à¦¬à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦‚à¦• à¦Ÿà§à¦°à¦¾à¦¨à§à¦¸à¦«à¦¾à¦° (Bank)'
        OTHER = 'OTHER', 'à¦…à¦¨à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¨à§à¦¯ (Other)'

    receipt_no = models.CharField(max_length=50, unique=True, editable=False, db_index=True)
    invoice = models.ForeignKey(StudentInvoice, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='payments')
    amount_paid = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2)
    payment_method = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=PaymentMethod.choices, default=PaymentMethod.CASH)
    transaction_reference = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, help_text="bKash TrxID à¦¬à¦¾ à¦¬à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦‚à¦• à¦¸à§à¦²à¦¿à¦ª à¦¨à¦®à§à¦¬à¦°")
    collected_by = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True)
    remarks = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Payment Transaction'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Payment Transactions'
        ordering = ['-created_at']

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        if not self.receipt_no:
            self.receipt_no = f"REC-{timezone.now().strftime('%Y%m%d')}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6].upper()}"
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.receipt_no} - {self.amount_paid} BDT ({self.payment_method})"

class CollegeExpense(BaseModel):
    """
    à¦•à¦²à§‡à¦œà§‡à¦° à¦¦à§ˆà¦¨à¦¨à§à¦¦à¦¿à¦¨ à¦–à¦°à¦šà§‡à¦° à¦­à¦¾à¦‰à¦šà¦¾à¦° à¦®à¦¡à§‡à¦²à¥¤
    à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦•à¦¾à¦‰à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¨à§à¦Ÿ à¦¡à§à¦°à¦¾à¦«à¦Ÿ à¦à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà§à¦°à¦¿ à¦¦à§‡à¦¬à§‡à¦¨ à¦à¦¬à¦‚ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦¸à¦¿à¦ªà¦¾à¦² à¦…à¦¨à§à¦®à§‹à¦¦à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦¬à§‡à¦¨à¥¤

    amount = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, help_text="à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦•à¦¾à¦° à¦ªà¦°à¦¿à¦®à¦¾à¦£")
    voucher_no = models.CharField(max_length=50, unique=True, editable=False, db_index=True)

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):

        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

class TeacherPayroll(BaseModel):
    """
    à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦•à¦¦à§‡à¦° à¦®à¦¾à¦¸à¦¿à¦• à¦ªà§‡-à¦°à§‹à¦² à¦¶à¦¿à¦Ÿ à¦®à¦¡à§‡à¦² (à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦•à¦¾à¦‰à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¨à§à¦Ÿ à¦œà§‡à¦¨à¦¾à¦°à§‡à¦Ÿ à¦•à¦°à¦¬à§‡à¦¨ -> à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦¸à¦¿à¦ªà¦¾à¦² à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦ªà§à¦°à§à¦­ à¦•à¦°à¦¬à§‡à¦¨ -> à¦¡à¦¿à¦œà¦¬à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¸ à¦¹à¦¬à§‡)à¥¤
    """
    class PayrollStatus(models.TextChoices):
        PENDING = 'PENDING', 'à¦…à¦ªà§‡à¦•à§à¦·à¦®à¦¾à¦£ (Pending Approval)'
        APPROVED = 'APPROVED', 'à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦¸à¦¿à¦ªà¦¾à¦² à¦•à¦°à§à¦¤à§ƒà¦• à¦…à¦¨à§à¦®à§‹à¦¦à¦¿à¦¤ (Approved)'
        DISBURSED = 'DISBURSED', 'à¦ªà¦°à¦¿à¦¶à§‹à¦§à¦¿à¦¤ (Disbursed/Paid)'
        REJECTED = 'REJECTED', 'à¦¬à¦¾à¦¤à¦¿à¦² (Rejected)'

    teacher = models.ForeignKey(
        User,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='payrolls',
        limit_choices_to={'role': User.Role.TEACHER}
    )
    month = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField(help_text="à¦®à¦¾à¦¸à§‡à¦° à¦•à§à¦°à¦®à¦¿à¦• à¦¨à¦®à§à¦¬à¦° (à§§-à§§à§¨)")
    year = models.PositiveIntegerField()
    
    base_salary = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2)
    bonus_allowance = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    deductions = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    net_salary = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    
    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=PayrollStatus.choices, default=PayrollStatus.PENDING, db_index=True)
    payment_method = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=PaymentTransaction.PaymentMethod.choices, default=PaymentTransaction.PaymentMethod.BANK)
    payment_reference = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, help_text="à¦šà§‡à¦• à¦¨à¦®à§à¦¬à¦° à¦¬à¦¾ à¦¬à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦‚à¦• à¦Ÿà§à¦°à¦¾à¦¨à¦œà§‡à¦•à¦¶à¦¨ à¦†à¦‡à¦¡à¦¿")
    
    approved_by = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, related_name='approved_payrolls')
    approved_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    disbursed_by = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, related_name='disbursed_payrolls')
    disbursed_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    remarks = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Teacher Payroll'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Teacher Payrolls'
        unique_together = ('teacher', 'month', 'year')
        ordering = ['-year', '-month']

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        self.net_salary = max(Decimal('0.00'), (self.base_salary + self.bonus_allowance) - self.deductions)
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.teacher.get_full_name()} - {self.month}/{self.year} (Net: {self.net_salary} BDT)"

    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True)

        verbose_name = 'Transport Vehicle'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Transport Vehicles'
        ordering = ['route_name']

    def __str__(self):

class VehicleMonthlyBill(BaseModel):
    """
    à¦—à¦¾à§œà¦¿à¦° à¦®à¦¾à¦²à¦¿à¦• à¦¬à¦¾ à¦¡à§à¦°à¦¾à¦‡à¦­à¦¾à¦°à§‡à¦° à¦®à¦¾à¦¸à¦¿à¦• à¦¨à¦¿à¦Ÿ à¦¬à¦¿à¦² à¦®à¦¡à§‡à¦²:
    (à¦¯à¦¤à¦¦à¦¿à¦¨ à¦—à¦¾à§œà¦¿ à¦šà¦²à§‡à¦›à§‡ * à¦¦à§ˆà¦¨à¦¿à¦• à¦°à§‡à¦Ÿ) - à¦¤à§‡à¦²à§‡à¦° à¦…à¦—à§à¦°à¦¿à¦® à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦•à¦¾ = à¦¨à¦¿à¦Ÿ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¦à§‡à§Ÿ à¦¬à¦¿à¦²
    """
    class BillStatus(models.TextChoices):
        DRAFT = 'DRAFT', 'à¦–à¦¸à§œà¦¾ (Draft)'
        APPROVED = 'APPROVED', 'à¦…à¦¨à§à¦®à§‹à¦¦à¦¿à¦¤ (Approved)'
        SETTLED = 'SETTLED', 'à¦ªà¦°à¦¿à¦¶à§‹à¦§à¦¿à¦¤ (Settled/Paid)'

    bill_number = models.CharField(max_length=50, unique=True, editable=False)
    vehicle = models.ForeignKey(TransportVehicle, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='monthly_bills')
    month = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField()
    year = models.PositiveIntegerField()

    total_days_run = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0)
    daily_rate = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2)
    gross_bill = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    total_deductions = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    net_payable = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)

    status = models.CharField(max_length=15, choices=BillStatus.choices, default=BillStatus.DRAFT, db_index=True)
    settled_by = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True)
    settled_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    payment_reference = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Vehicle Monthly Bill'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Vehicle Monthly Bills'
        unique_together = ('vehicle', 'month', 'year')
        ordering = ['-year', '-month']

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        if not self.bill_number:
            self.bill_number = f"VBILL-{self.year}{self.month:02d}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6].upper()}"
        self.gross_bill = self.total_days_run * self.daily_rate
        self.net_payable = max(Decimal('0.00'), self.gross_bill - self.total_deductions)
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.bill_number} | {self.vehicle.vehicle_number} ({self.net_payable} BDT) - {self.status}"

    resolved_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    reported_by = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Vehicle Breakdown Alert'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Vehicle Breakdown Alerts'

class SupplierPurchase(BaseModel):
    """
    à¦­à§‡à¦¨à§à¦¡à¦° à¦¥à§‡à¦•à§‡ à¦•à§‡à¦¨à¦¾à¦•à¦¾à¦Ÿà¦¾ à¦¬à¦¾ à¦•à¦¾à¦œà§‡à¦° à¦¬à¦¿à¦²à¥¤
    """
    supplier = models.ForeignKey(Supplier, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='purchases')
    invoice_no = models.CharField(max_length=100, unique=True)
    purchase_date = models.DateField(default=timezone.localdate)
    item_description = models.TextField(help_text="à¦¯à§‡à¦®à¦¨: à§«à§¦à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦ªà¦°à§€à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦° à¦¬à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¨à¦¾à¦° à¦“ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¶à§à¦¨à¦ªà¦¤à§à¦° à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦‚")
    total_bill = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2)
    paid_amount = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    due_amount = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Supplier Purchase'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Supplier Purchases'
        ordering = ['-purchase_date']
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=['supplier', 'due_amount']),
        ]

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        self.due_amount = max(Decimal('0.00'), self.total_bill - self.paid_amount)
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.supplier.name} - Inv: {self.invoice_no} (Due: {self.due_amount})"


class SupplierPayment(BaseModel):
    """
    à¦¦à§‹à¦•à¦¾à¦¨à¦¦à¦¾à¦°/à¦­à§‡à¦¨à§à¦¡à¦°à¦¦à§‡à¦° à¦¬à¦•à§‡à§Ÿà¦¾ à¦•à¦¿à¦¸à§à¦¤à¦¿à¦° à¦ªà¦°à¦¿à¦¶à§‹à¦§ à¦®à¦¡à§‡à¦²à¥¤
    """
    purchase = models.ForeignKey(SupplierPurchase, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='payments')
    payment_date = models.DateField(default=timezone.localdate)
    amount_paid = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2)
    payment_method = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=PaymentTransaction.PaymentMethod.choices, default=PaymentTransaction.PaymentMethod.CASH)
    payment_receipt_no = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True)
    paid_by = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True)
    remarks = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Supplier Payment'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Supplier Payments'
        ordering = ['-payment_date']

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)
        # à¦®à§‚à¦² à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à¦šà§‡à¦œ à¦¬à¦¿à¦²à§‡ à¦ªà¦°à¦¿à¦¶à§‹à¦§ à¦¸à¦®à¦¨à§à¦¬à§Ÿ à¦•à¦°à¦¾
        p = self.purchase
        total_paid = p.payments.aggregate(total=models.Sum('amount_paid'))['total'] or Decimal('0.00')
        p.paid_amount = total_paid
        p.save()

    def __str__(self):
        return f"Payment of {self.amount_paid} to {self.purchase.supplier.name}"

class PaymentTransactionSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    collected_by_name = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    payment_method_display = serializers.CharField(source='get_payment_method_display', read_only=True)

    class Meta:
        model = PaymentTransaction
        fields = [
            'id',
            'receipt_no',
            'invoice',
            'amount_paid',
            'payment_method',
            'payment_method_display',
            'transaction_reference',
            'collected_by',
            'collected_by_name',
            'remarks',
            'created_at',
        ]
        read_only_fields = ['id', 'receipt_no', 'collected_by', 'created_at']

    def get_collected_by_name(self, obj) -> str | None:
        if obj.collected_by:
            return obj.collected_by.get_full_name() or obj.collected_by.username
        return None

    def validate_amount_paid(self, value):
        if value <= Decimal('0.00'):
            raise serializers.ValidationError("à¦œà¦®à¦¾à¦° à¦ªà¦°à¦¿à¦®à¦¾à¦£ à§¦ à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦•à¦¾à¦° à¦¬à§‡à¦¶à¦¿ à¦¹à¦¤à§‡ à¦¹à¦¬à§‡à¥¤")
        return value


class StudentInvoiceSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    student_unique_id = serializers.CharField(source='student.student_unique_id', read_only=True)
    student_name = serializers.CharField(source='student.user.get_full_name', read_only=True)
    roll_number = serializers.IntegerField(source='student.roll_number', read_only=True)
    section_name = serializers.CharField(source='section.__str__', read_only=True)
    status_display = serializers.CharField(source='get_status_display', read_only=True)
    payments = PaymentTransactionSerializer(many=True, read_only=True)

    class Meta:
        model = StudentInvoice
        fields = [
            'id',

        read_only_fields = [
            'id', 'invoice_number', 'total_amount', 'paid_amount',

class InvoiceGenerateSerializer(serializers.Serializer):
    """
    à¦à¦• à¦¬à¦¾ à¦à¦•à¦¾à¦§à¦¿à¦• à¦®à¦¾à¦¸à§‡à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ à¦ªà§à¦°à§‹ à¦¸à§‡à¦•à¦¶à¦¨ à¦¬à¦¾ à¦¨à¦¿à¦°à§à¦¦à¦¿à¦·à§à¦Ÿ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦° à¦‡à¦¨à¦­à§Ÿà§‡à¦¸ à¦¬à¦¾à¦²à§à¦• à¦¤à§ˆà¦°à¦¿à¦° à¦¸à¦¿à¦°à¦¿à§Ÿà¦¾à¦²à¦¾à¦‡à¦œà¦¾à¦°à¥¤
    """
    section_id = serializers.UUIDField(required=False, allow_null=True)
    student_id = serializers.UUIDField(required=False, allow_null=True)
    months = serializers.ListField(
        child=serializers.IntegerField(min_value=1, max_value=12),
        allow_empty=False,
        help_text="à¦à¦• à¦¬à¦¾ à¦à¦•à¦¾à¦§à¦¿à¦• à¦®à¦¾à¦¸, à¦¯à§‡à¦®à¦¨: [10, 11]"
    )
    year = serializers.IntegerField(default=2026)
    due_date = serializers.DateField(required=False, allow_null=True)
    other_fees = serializers.DecimalField(
        max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=Decimal('0.00')
    )

    def validate(self, attrs):
        if not attrs.get('section_id') and not attrs.get('student_id'):
            raise serializers.ValidationError("à¦…à¦¬à¦¶à§à¦¯à¦‡ 'section_id' à¦…à¦¥à¦¬à¦¾ 'student_id'-à¦à¦° à¦¯à§‡à¦•à§‹à¦¨à§‹ à¦à¦•à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¦à¦¾à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦¤à§‡ à¦¹à¦¬à§‡à¥¤")
        return attrs

    @transaction.atomic
    def generate(self, created_by_user):
        section_id = self.validated_data.get('section_id')
        student_id = self.validated_data.get('student_id')
        months = self.validated_data['months']
        year = self.validated_data['year']
        due_date = self.validated_data.get('due_date')
        other_fees = self.validated_data.get('other_fees', Decimal('0.00'))

        if student_id:
            students = StudentProfile.objects.filter(id=student_id, user__is_active=True)
        else:
            students = StudentProfile.objects.filter(section_id=section_id, user__is_active=True)

        students = list(students.select_related('section'))
        student_ids = [student.id for student in students]
        existing = set(
            StudentInvoice.objects.filter(
                student_id__in=student_ids, month__in=months, year=year
            ).values_list('student_id', 'month')
        )
        attendance_counts = {}
        attendance_rows = StudentAttendance.objects.filter(
                student_id__in=student_ids,
                date__year=year,
                date__month__in=months,
                status=StudentAttendance.AttendanceStatus.ABSENT,
            ).values('student_id', 'date__month').annotate(absent_days=Count('id'))
        for row in attendance_rows:
            attendance_counts[(row['student_id'], row['date__month'])] = row['absent_days']

        invoices_to_create = []
        for student in students:
            for month in months:
                if (student.id, month) in existing:
                    continue
                absence_fine = Decimal(str(
                    attendance_counts.get((student.id, month), 0) * 100
                ))
                transport_fee = (
                    student.monthly_transport_fee
                    if student.uses_transport else Decimal('0.00')
                )
                invoices_to_create.append(StudentInvoice(
                    invoice_number=f"INV-{year}{month:02d}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6].upper()}",
                    student=student,
                    section=student.section,
                    month=month,
                    year=year,
                    tuition_fee=student.monthly_tuition_fee,
                    transport_fee=transport_fee,
                    absence_fine=absence_fine,
                    other_fees=other_fees,
                    due_date=due_date,
                    created_by=created_by_user,
                ))

        for invoice in invoices_to_create:
            invoice.calculate_totals()
        created_invoices = StudentInvoice.objects.bulk_create(invoices_to_create)

        return created_invoices

    class Meta:
        model = Supplier
        fields = ['id', 'name', 'service_type', 'contact_person', 'phone', 'address', 'total_due', 'is_active', 'created_at']

    def get_total_due(self, obj) -> float:
        if hasattr(obj, 'total_due_amount'):
            total = obj.total_due_amount or Decimal('0.00')
        else:
            total = obj.purchases.aggregate(
                due=models.Sum('due_amount')
            )['due'] or Decimal('0.00')
        return float(total)

from django.test import TestCase
from django.test.utils import CaptureQueriesContext
from django.db import connection
from datetime import date
from decimal import Decimal

from apps.academics.models import AcademicSection, StudentAttendance
from apps.finance.models import StudentInvoice
from apps.finance.serializers import InvoiceGenerateSerializer
from apps.users_auth.models import StudentProfile, User


class InvoiceGenerationTests(TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.user = User.objects.create_user(
            username='invoice-admin',
            password='password',
            role=User.Role.PRINCIPAL,
        )
        self.section = AcademicSection.objects.create(
            class_level=AcademicSection.ClassLevel.XI,
            group=AcademicSection.GroupChoices.SCIENCE,
            section_name='A',
            session='2026-2027',
        )
        self.student_user = User.objects.create_user(
            username='invoice-student',
            password='password',
            role=User.Role.STUDENT,
        )
        self.student = StudentProfile.objects.create(
            user=self.student_user,
            section=self.section,
            roll_number=1,
            session='2026-2027',
            father_name='Father',
            mother_name='Mother',
            guardian_phone='01700000000',
            monthly_tuition_fee=Decimal('5000.00'),
            monthly_transport_fee=Decimal('1200.00'),
            uses_transport=True,
        )
        StudentAttendance.objects.create(
            student=self.student,
            section=self.section,
            date=date(2026, 10, 3),
            status=StudentAttendance.AttendanceStatus.ABSENT,
            recorded_by=self.user,
        )

    def test_generation_batches_data_and_skips_existing_invoice(self):
        serializer = InvoiceGenerateSerializer(data={
            'section_id': self.section.id,
            'months': [10, 11],
            'year': 2026,
        })
        self.assertTrue(serializer.is_valid(), serializer.errors)

        with CaptureQueriesContext(connection) as queries:
            created = serializer.generate(self.user)

        self.assertEqual(len(created), 2)
        self.assertLessEqual(len(queries), 6)
        october = StudentInvoice.objects.get(
            student=self.student, month=10, year=2026
        )
        self.assertEqual(october.absence_fine, Decimal('100.00'))
        self.assertEqual(october.total_amount, Decimal('6300.00'))
        self.assertEqual(october.due_amount, Decimal('6300.00'))

        rerun = InvoiceGenerateSerializer(data={
            'section_id': self.section.id,
            'months': [10, 11],
            'year': 2026,
        })
        self.assertTrue(rerun.is_valid(), rerun.errors)
        self.assertEqual(rerun.generate(self.user), [])
        self.assertEqual(
            StudentInvoice.objects.filter(student=self.student, year=2026).count(),
            2,
        )

from django.urls import include, path
from rest_framework.routers import DefaultRouter
from apps.finance.views import (
    BankDepositViewSet,
    CollegeExpenseViewSet,
    DailyCashClosingView,
    DriverAdvanceDeductionViewSet,
    ExpenseCategoryViewSet,
    FinancialBalanceSheetView,
    FineWaiverApplicationViewSet,
    InchargeCashHandoverViewSet,
    MasterDefaulterListView,
    StudentInvoiceViewSet,
    SupplierPurchaseViewSet,
    SupplierViewSet,
    TeacherPayrollViewSet,
    TeacherSalaryClaimViewSet,
    TransportVehicleViewSet,
    VehicleDailyLogViewSet,
    VehicleMonthlyBillViewSet,
)

app_name = 'finance'

router = DefaultRouter()
router.register(r'invoices', StudentInvoiceViewSet, basename='invoice')
router.register(r'waivers', FineWaiverApplicationViewSet, basename='waiver')
router.register(r'categories', ExpenseCategoryViewSet, basename='expense-category')
router.register(r'expenses', CollegeExpenseViewSet, basename='expense')
router.register(r'payrolls', TeacherPayrollViewSet, basename='payroll')
router.register(r'salary-claims', TeacherSalaryClaimViewSet, basename='salary-claim')
router.register(r'vehicles', TransportVehicleViewSet, basename='vehicle')
router.register(r'vehicle-logs', VehicleDailyLogViewSet, basename='vehicle-log')
router.register(r'driver-advances', DriverAdvanceDeductionViewSet, basename='driver-advance')
router.register(r'vehicle-bills', VehicleMonthlyBillViewSet, basename='vehicle-bill')
router.register(r'cash-handovers', InchargeCashHandoverViewSet, basename='cash-handover')
router.register(r'bank-deposits', BankDepositViewSet, basename='bank-deposit')
router.register(r'suppliers', SupplierViewSet, basename='supplier')
router.register(r'supplier-purchases', SupplierPurchaseViewSet, basename='supplier-purchase')

urlpatterns = [
    path('', include(router.urls)),
    path('analytics/balance-sheet/', FinancialBalanceSheetView.as_view(), name='balance-sheet'),
    path('analytics/daily-closing/', DailyCashClosingView.as_view(), name='daily-closing'),
    path('analytics/defaulters/', MasterDefaulterListView.as_view(), name='master-defaulters'),
]

class StudentInvoiceViewSet(viewsets.ReadOnlyModelViewSet):
    """
    à¦‡à¦¨à¦­à§Ÿà§‡à¦¸ à¦ªà¦°à¦¿à¦šà¦¾à¦²à¦¨à¦¾, à¦¬à¦¾à¦²à§à¦• à¦¬à¦¿à¦² à¦œà§‡à¦¨à¦¾à¦°à§‡à¦¶à¦¨ à¦à¦¬à¦‚ à¦«à¦¿ à¦•à¦¾à¦²à§‡à¦•à¦¶à¦¨ à¦­à¦¿à¦‰à¦¸à§‡à¦Ÿà¥¤
    """
    queryset = StudentInvoice.objects.select_related('student', 'student__user', 'section').prefetch_related('payments').all()
    serializer_class = StudentInvoiceSerializer

    def get_permissions(self):
        if self.action in ['generate_invoices']:
            return [IsAccountantOrPrincipal()]
        return [IsAuthenticated()]

    def get_queryset(self):
        user = self.request.user
        queryset = super().get_queryset()
        if self.action == 'retrieve':
            queryset = queryset.prefetch_related('payments')
        else:
            queryset = queryset.prefetch_related(None)

        if user.role == User.Role.STUDENT and hasattr(user, 'student_profile'):
            return queryset.filter(student=user.student_profile)
        elif user.role == User.Role.TEACHER:
            queryset = queryset.filter(section__in_charge_teacher=user)

        section_id = self.request.query_params.get('section')
        if section_id:
            queryset = queryset.filter(section_id=section_id)

        status_param = self.request.query_params.get('status')
        if status_param:
            queryset = queryset.filter(status=status_param.upper())

        return queryset

    @action(detail=False, methods=['post'], url_path='generate')
    def generate_invoices(self, request):
        serializer = InvoiceGenerateSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)
        invoices = serializer.generate(created_by_user=request.user)

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": f"à¦®à§‹à¦Ÿ {len(invoices)} à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦¨à¦¤à§à¦¨ à¦‡à¦¨à¦­à§Ÿà§‡à¦¸ à¦¸à¦«à¦²à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ à¦œà§‡à¦¨à¦¾à¦°à§‡à¦Ÿ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤",
            "total_generated": len(invoices)
        }, status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)

    @action(detail=True, methods=['post'], url_path='collect-payment')
    @transaction.atomic
    def collect_payment(self, request, pk=None):
        invoice = self.get_object()
        user = request.user

        # à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à¦®à¦¿à¦¶à¦¨ à¦“ à¦¬à¦¾à¦‰à¦¨à§à¦¡à¦¾à¦°à¦¿ à¦šà§‡à¦•: à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦•à¦¾à¦‰à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¨à§à¦Ÿ, à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦¸à¦¿à¦ªà¦¾à¦² à¦…à¦¥à¦¬à¦¾ à¦¨à¦¿à¦œ à¦¸à§‡à¦•à¦¶à¦¨à§‡à¦° à¦¶à§à¦°à§‡à¦£à¦¿ à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œ
        if user.role not in [User.Role.ACCOUNTANT, User.Role.PRINCIPAL, User.Role.TEACHER] and not user.is_superuser:
            raise PermissionDenied("à¦«à¦¿ à¦†à¦¦à¦¾à§Ÿà§‡à¦° à¦…à¦¨à§à¦®à¦¤à¦¿ à¦†à¦ªà¦¨à¦¾à¦° à¦¨à§‡à¦‡à¥¤")

        if user.role == User.Role.TEACHER:
            if invoice.section.in_charge_teacher_id != user.id:
                raise PermissionDenied(f"à¦†à¦ªà¦¨à¦¿ '{invoice.section}' à¦¶à¦¾à¦–à¦¾à¦° à¦¶à§à¦°à§‡à¦£à¦¿ à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œ à¦¨à¦¨à¥¤ à¦¶à§à¦§à§à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° à¦¶à§à¦°à§‡à¦£à¦¿ à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œ à¦¨à¦¿à¦œ à¦•à§à¦²à¦¾à¦¸à§‡à¦° à¦«à¦¿ à¦—à§à¦°à¦¹à¦£ à¦•à¦°à¦¤à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à¦¬à§‡à¦¨à¥¤")

        invoice = StudentInvoice.objects.select_for_update().select_related(
            'section', 'section__in_charge_teacher'
        ).get(pk=invoice.pk)
        serializer = PaymentTransactionSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        amount_to_pay = serializer.validated_data['amount_paid']

        if invoice.status == StudentInvoice.InvoiceStatus.PAID:
            return Response(
                {"success": False, "message": "à¦à¦‡ à¦‡à¦¨à¦­à§Ÿà§‡à¦¸à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦° à¦¸à¦®à§à¦ªà§‚à¦°à§à¦£ à¦¬à¦¿à¦² à¦‡à¦¤à¦¿à¦®à¦§à§à¦¯à§‡ à¦ªà¦°à¦¿à¦¶à§‹à¦§à¦¿à¦¤à¥¤"},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST
            )

        if amount_to_pay > invoice.due_amount:
            return Response(
                {"success": False, "message": f"à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¦à¦¤à§à¦¤ à¦ªà¦°à¦¿à¦®à¦¾à¦£ à¦¬à¦•à§‡à§Ÿà¦¾ à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦•à¦¾à¦° à¦šà§‡à§Ÿà§‡ à¦¬à§‡à¦¶à¦¿à¥¤ à¦¬à¦°à§à¦¤à¦®à¦¾à¦¨ à¦¬à¦•à§‡à§Ÿà¦¾: {invoice.due_amount} à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦•à¦¾à¥¤"},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST
            )

        payment = serializer.save(invoice=invoice, collected_by=request.user)
        
        # à¦‡à¦¨à¦­à§Ÿà§‡à¦¸ à¦†à¦ªà¦¡à§‡à¦Ÿ
        invoice.paid_amount += amount_to_pay
        invoice.save()

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": f"{amount_to_pay} à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦•à¦¾ à¦¸à¦«à¦²à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ à¦—à§ƒà¦¹à§€à¦¤ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤ à¦°à¦¸à¦¿à¦¦ à¦¨à¦®à§à¦¬à¦°: {payment.receipt_no}",
            "receipt": PaymentTransactionSerializer(payment).data,
            "invoice_status": invoice.status,
            "current_due": invoice.due_amount
        }, status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)


class FineWaiverApplicationViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    serializer_class = FineWaiverApplicationSerializer

    def get_permissions(self):
        return [IsAuthenticated()]

    def get_queryset(self):
        user = self.request.user
        queryset = FineWaiverApplication.objects.select_related('student', 'student__user', 'invoice').all()

        if user.role in [User.Role.PRINCIPAL, User.Role.ACCOUNTANT] or user.is_superuser:
            return queryset

        if user.role == User.Role.TEACHER:
            return queryset.filter(invoice__section__in_charge_teacher=user)

        if user.role == User.Role.STUDENT and hasattr(user, 'student_profile'):
            return queryset.filter(student=user.student_profile)

        return queryset.none()

    def perform_create(self, serializer):
        user = self.request.user
        if user.role != User.Role.STUDENT or not hasattr(user, 'student_profile'):
            raise PermissionDenied("à¦¶à§à¦§à§à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦°à¦¾ à¦®à¦“à¦•à§à¦«à§‡à¦° à¦†à¦¬à§‡à¦¦à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦¤à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à§‡à¥¤")

        invoice = serializer.validated_data['invoice']
        if invoice.student != user.student_profile:
            raise PermissionDenied("à¦†à¦ªà¦¨à¦¿ à¦¶à§à¦§à§à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° à¦¨à¦¿à¦œà§‡à¦° à¦‡à¦¨à¦­à§Ÿà§‡à¦¸à§‡à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ à¦®à¦“à¦•à§à¦«à§‡à¦° à¦†à¦¬à§‡à¦¦à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦¤à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à§‡à¦¨à¥¤")

        serializer.save(student=user.student_profile)

    @action(detail=True, methods=['patch'], url_path='incharge-recommend')
    def incharge_recommend(self, request, pk=None):
        waiver_app = self.get_object()
        user = request.user

        if user.role != User.Role.PRINCIPAL and waiver_app.invoice.section.in_charge_teacher_id != user.id:
            raise PermissionDenied("à¦†à¦ªà¦¨à¦¿ à¦à¦‡ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦° à¦¶à§à¦°à§‡à¦£à¦¿ à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œ à¦¨à¦¨à¥¤")

        status_input = request.data.get('status', '').upper()
        if status_input not in [
            FineWaiverApplication.InChargeWaiverStatus.RECOMMENDED,
            FineWaiverApplication.InChargeWaiverStatus.REJECTED
        ]:
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "status à¦¹à¦¿à¦¸à§‡à¦¬à§‡ RECOMMENDED à¦…à¦¥à¦¬à¦¾ REJECTED à¦¦à¦¿à¦¨à¥¤"}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        recommended_amount = Decimal(str(request.data.get('recommended_waiver_amount', '0.00')))
        if recommended_amount > waiver_app.invoice.absence_fine and recommended_amount > waiver_app.invoice.total_amount:
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "à¦¸à§à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à¦¿à¦¶à¦•à§ƒà¦¤ à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦•à¦¾à¦° à¦ªà¦°à¦¿à¦®à¦¾à¦£ à¦®à§‚à¦² à¦¬à¦¿à¦²à§‡à¦° à¦šà§‡à§Ÿà§‡ à¦¬à§‡à¦¶à¦¿ à¦¹à¦¤à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à§‡ à¦¨à¦¾à¥¤"}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        waiver_app.incharge_status = status_input
        waiver_app.recommended_waiver_amount = recommended_amount
        waiver_app.incharge_remarks = request.data.get('remarks', '')
        waiver_app.reviewed_by_incharge = user
        waiver_app.incharge_reviewed_at = timezone.now()
        waiver_app.save()

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": f"à¦†à¦¬à§‡à¦¦à¦¨à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦¤à§‡ à¦¸à¦«à¦²à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œà§‡à¦° à¦®à¦¤à¦¾à¦®à¦¤ à¦¸à¦‚à¦°à¦•à§à¦·à¦¿à¦¤ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡ ({waiver_app.get_incharge_status_display()})à¥¤",
            "data": FineWaiverApplicationSerializer(waiver_app).data
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    @action(detail=True, methods=['patch'], permission_classes=[IsPrincipal], url_path='principal-approve')
    @transaction.atomic
    def principal_approve(self, request, pk=None):
        waiver_app = self.get_object()
        status_input = request.data.get('status', '').upper()

        if status_input not in [
            FineWaiverApplication.PrincipalWaiverStatus.APPROVED,
            FineWaiverApplication.PrincipalWaiverStatus.REJECTED
        ]:
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "status à¦¹à¦¿à¦¸à§‡à¦¬à§‡ APPROVED à¦…à¦¥à¦¬à¦¾ REJECTED à¦¦à¦¿à¦¨à¥¤"}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        approved_amount = Decimal(str(request.data.get('approved_waiver_amount', waiver_app.recommended_waiver_amount)))
        
        waiver_app.principal_status = status_input
        waiver_app.approved_waiver_amount = approved_amount if status_input == FineWaiverApplication.PrincipalWaiverStatus.APPROVED else Decimal('0.00')
        waiver_app.principal_remarks = request.data.get('remarks', '')
        waiver_app.approved_by_principal = request.user
        waiver_app.principal_reviewed_at = timezone.now()
        waiver_app.save()

        if status_input == FineWaiverApplication.PrincipalWaiverStatus.APPROVED:
            invoice = waiver_app.invoice
            invoice.waiver_amount += approved_amount
            invoice.save()

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": f"à¦®à¦“à¦•à§à¦«à§‡à¦° à¦†à¦¬à§‡à¦¦à¦¨ à¦¸à¦«à¦²à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ '{waiver_app.get_principal_status_display()}' à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡ à¦à¦¬à¦‚ à¦‡à¦¨à¦­à§Ÿà§‡à¦¸à§‡à¦° à¦¬à¦•à§‡à§Ÿà¦¾ à¦†à¦ªà¦¡à§‡à¦Ÿ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤",
            "updated_invoice": StudentInvoiceSerializer(waiver_app.invoice).data
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class ExpenseCategoryViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    queryset = ExpenseCategory.objects.filter(is_active=True)
    serializer_class = ExpenseCategorySerializer
    permission_classes = [IsAccountantOrPrincipal]


class CollegeExpenseViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    queryset = CollegeExpense.objects.select_related('category', 'recorded_by', 'approved_by').all()
    serializer_class = CollegeExpenseSerializer
    permission_classes = [IsAccountantOrPrincipal]

    def get_queryset(self):
        queryset = super().get_queryset()
        
        category_id = self.request.query_params.get('category')
        if category_id:
            queryset = queryset.filter(category_id=category_id)

        status_param = self.request.query_params.get('status')
        if status_param:
            queryset = queryset.filter(status=status_param.upper())

        start_date = self.request.query_params.get('start_date')
        end_date = self.request.query_params.get('end_date')
        if start_date and end_date:
            queryset = queryset.filter(expense_date__range=[start_date, end_date])

        return queryset

    def perform_create(self, serializer):
        serializer.save(recorded_by=self.request.user)

    @action(detail=True, methods=['patch'], permission_classes=[IsPrincipal], url_path='verify')
    def verify_expense(self, request, pk=None):
        expense = self.get_object()
        new_status = request.data.get('status', '').upper()

        if new_status not in [CollegeExpense.ExpenseStatus.APPROVED, CollegeExpense.ExpenseStatus.REJECTED]:
            return Response(
                {"success": False, "message": "status à¦¹à¦¿à¦¸à§‡à¦¬à§‡ APPROVED à¦…à¦¥à¦¬à¦¾ REJECTED à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¦à¦¾à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à§à¦¨à¥¤"},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST
            )

        expense.status = new_status
        expense.approved_by = request.user
        expense.approved_at = timezone.now()
        expense.rejection_reason = request.data.get('rejection_reason', '') if new_status == CollegeExpense.ExpenseStatus.REJECTED else ''
        expense.save()

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": f"à¦­à¦¾à¦‰à¦šà¦¾à¦° {expense.voucher_no} à¦¸à¦«à¦²à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ '{expense.get_status_display()}' à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤",
            "data": CollegeExpenseSerializer(expense).data
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class FinancialBalanceSheetView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAccountantOrPrincipal]

    def get(self, request):
        now = timezone.localdate()
        year = int(request.query_params.get('year', now.year))
        month = request.query_params.get('month')
        start_date = request.query_params.get('start_date')
        end_date = request.query_params.get('end_date')

        payments_qs = PaymentTransaction.objects.all()
        expenses_qs = CollegeExpense.objects.filter(status=CollegeExpense.ExpenseStatus.APPROVED)
        invoices_qs = StudentInvoice.objects.all()

        if start_date and end_date:
            payments_qs = payments_qs.filter(created_at__date__range=[start_date, end_date])
            expenses_qs = expenses_qs.filter(expense_date__range=[start_date, end_date])
            invoices_qs = invoices_qs.filter(created_at__date__range=[start_date, end_date])
            timeframe_label = f"{start_date} to {end_date}"
        elif month:
            month = int(month)
            payments_qs = payments_qs.filter(created_at__year=year, created_at__month=month)
            expenses_qs = expenses_qs.filter(expense_date__year=year, expense_date__month=month)
            invoices_qs = invoices_qs.filter(year=year, month=month)
            timeframe_label = f"Month: {month:02d}, Year: {year}"
        else:
            payments_qs = payments_qs.filter(created_at__year=year)
            expenses_qs = expenses_qs.filter(expense_date__year=year)
            invoices_qs = invoices_qs.filter(year=year)
            timeframe_label = f"Full Year: {year}"

        total_collected = payments_qs.aggregate(total=Sum('amount_paid'))['total'] or Decimal('0.00')
        total_expense = expenses_qs.aggregate(total=Sum('amount'))['total'] or Decimal('0.00')
        net_balance = total_collected - total_expense

        total_due_receivable = invoices_qs.aggregate(due=Sum('due_amount'))['due'] or Decimal('0.00')
        total_billed = invoices_qs.aggregate(billed=Sum('total_amount'))['billed'] or Decimal('0.00')

        category_breakdown = expenses_qs.values('category__name').annotate(
            total_spent=Sum('amount'),
            voucher_count=Count('id')
        ).order_by('-total_spent')

        payment_methods_breakdown = payments_qs.values('payment_method').annotate(
            total_amount=Sum('amount_paid'),
            tx_count=Count('id')
        ).order_by('-total_amount')

        pending_expenses = CollegeExpense.objects.filter(status=CollegeExpense.ExpenseStatus.PENDING).aggregate(
            count=Count('id'),
            total=Sum('amount')
        )

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "timeframe": timeframe_label,
            "summary": {
                "total_fee_collected": float(total_collected),
                "total_approved_expense": float(total_expense),
                "net_cash_in_hand": float(net_balance),
                "total_student_due_receivable": float(total_due_receivable),
                "total_invoiced_amount": float(total_billed),
            },
            "category_wise_expense": [
                {
                    "category": item['category__name'],
                    "total_amount": float(item['total_spent']),
                    "voucher_count": item['voucher_count'],
                }
                for item in category_breakdown
            ],
            "payment_channel_breakdown": [
                {
                    "method": item['payment_method'],
                    "total_collected": float(item['total_amount']),
                    "transactions": item['tx_count'],
                }
                for item in payment_methods_breakdown
            ],
            "pending_audit_alert": {
                "pending_vouchers": pending_expenses['count'] or 0,
                "pending_amount": float(pending_expenses['total'] or Decimal('0.00')),
            }
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class TeacherPayrollViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    queryset = TeacherPayroll.objects.select_related('teacher', 'approved_by', 'disbursed_by').all()
    serializer_class = TeacherPayrollSerializer

    def get_permissions(self):
        if self.action in ['list', 'retrieve']:
            return [IsAuthenticated()]
        return [IsAccountantOrPrincipal()]

    def get_queryset(self):
        user = self.request.user
        queryset = super().get_queryset()
        if user.role == User.Role.TEACHER:
            return queryset.filter(teacher=user)
        return queryset

    @action(detail=False, methods=['post'], url_path='bulk-generate')
    @transaction.atomic
    def bulk_generate(self, request):
        month = request.data.get('month')
        year = request.data.get('year')

        if not month or not year:
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "month à¦à¦¬à¦‚ year à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¦à¦¾à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦†à¦¬à¦¶à§à¦¯à¦•à¥¤"}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        teachers = User.objects.filter(role=User.Role.TEACHER, is_active=True).select_related('teacher_profile')
        created_count = 0

        for teacher in teachers:
            if TeacherPayroll.objects.filter(teacher=teacher, month=month, year=year).exists():
                continue

            base_salary = getattr(teacher.teacher_profile, 'base_salary', Decimal('0.00')) if hasattr(teacher, 'teacher_profile') else Decimal('0.00')

            TeacherPayroll.objects.create(
                teacher=teacher,
                month=month,
                year=year,
                base_salary=base_salary,
                bonus_allowance=Decimal('0.00'),
                deductions=Decimal('0.00'),
                status=TeacherPayroll.PayrollStatus.PENDING,
            )
            created_count += 1

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": f"{month}/{year} à¦®à¦¾à¦¸à§‡à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ à¦®à§‹à¦Ÿ {created_count} à¦œà¦¨ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦•à§‡à¦° à¦ªà§‡-à¦°à§‹à¦² à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¸à§à¦¤à§à¦¤ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤",
            "total_generated": created_count
        }, status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)

    @action(detail=True, methods=['patch'], permission_classes=[IsPrincipal], url_path='approve')
    def approve_payroll(self, request, pk=None):
        payroll = self.get_object()
        payroll.status = TeacherPayroll.PayrollStatus.APPROVED
        payroll.approved_by = request.user
        payroll.approved_at = timezone.now()
        payroll.remarks = request.data.get('remarks', payroll.remarks)
        payroll.save()

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": f"à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦• {payroll.teacher.get_full_name()} à¦à¦° à¦¬à§‡à¦¤à¦¨ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦¸à¦¿à¦ªà¦¾à¦² à¦•à¦°à§à¦¤à§ƒà¦• à¦…à¦¨à§à¦®à§‹à¦¦à¦¿à¦¤ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤",
            "data": TeacherPayrollSerializer(payroll).data
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    @action(detail=True, methods=['patch'], url_path='disburse')
    def disburse_payroll(self, request, pk=None):
        payroll = self.get_object()
        if payroll.status != TeacherPayroll.PayrollStatus.APPROVED:
            return Response(
                {"success": False, "message": "à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦¸à¦¿à¦ªà¦¾à¦²à§‡à¦° à¦…à¦¨à§à¦®à§‹à¦¦à¦¨ à¦›à¦¾à§œà¦¾ à¦¬à§‡à¦¤à¦¨ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¦à¦¾à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¯à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ à¦¨à¦¾à¥¤"},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST
            )

        payroll.status = TeacherPayroll.PayrollStatus.DISBURSED
        payroll.disbursed_by = request.user
        payroll.disbursed_at = timezone.now()
        payroll.payment_reference = request.data.get('payment_reference', 'DISBURSED-CASH-OR-BANK')
        payroll.save()

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": f"à¦¬à§‡à¦¤à¦¨ à¦¸à¦«à¦²à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ à¦ªà¦°à¦¿à¦¶à§‹à¦§à¦¿à¦¤ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡ ({payroll.net_salary} BDT)à¥¤",
            "data": TeacherPayrollSerializer(payroll).data
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class TeacherSalaryClaimViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    serializer_class = TeacherSalaryClaimSerializer

    def get_permissions(self):
        return [IsAuthenticated()]

    def get_queryset(self):
        user = self.request.user
        queryset = TeacherSalaryClaim.objects.select_related('teacher', 'approved_by', 'disbursed_by').all()

        if user.role == User.Role.TEACHER:
            return queryset.filter(teacher=user)
        if user.role in [User.Role.PRINCIPAL, User.Role.ACCOUNTANT] or user.is_superuser:
            return queryset
        return queryset.none()

    def perform_create(self, serializer):
        if self.request.user.role != User.Role.TEACHER:
            raise PermissionDenied("à¦¶à§à¦§à§à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦•à¦—à¦£ à¦¬à§‡à¦¤à¦¨à§‡à¦° à¦†à¦¬à§‡à¦¦à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦¤à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à¦¬à§‡à¦¨à¥¤")
        serializer.save(teacher=self.request.user)

    @action(detail=True, methods=['patch'], permission_classes=[IsPrincipal], url_path='principal-approve')
    def principal_approve(self, request, pk=None):
        claim = self.get_object()
        status_input = request.data.get('status', 'APPROVED').upper()

        if status_input == 'REJECTED':
            claim.status = TeacherSalaryClaim.ClaimStatus.REJECTED
        else:
            claim.status = TeacherSalaryClaim.ClaimStatus.APPROVED_BY_PRINCIPAL

        claim.principal_remarks = request.data.get('remarks', '')
        claim.approved_by = request.user
        claim.approved_at = timezone.now()
        claim.save()

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": f"à¦¦à¦¾à¦¬à¦¿à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦¸à¦¿à¦ªà¦¾à¦² à¦•à¦°à§à¦¤à§ƒà¦• '{claim.get_status_display()}' à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤",
            "data": TeacherSalaryClaimSerializer(claim).data
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    @action(detail=True, methods=['patch'], permission_classes=[IsAccountantOrPrincipal], url_path='disburse')
    def disburse_claim(self, request, pk=None):
        claim = self.get_object()
        if claim.status != TeacherSalaryClaim.ClaimStatus.APPROVED_BY_PRINCIPAL:
            return Response(
                {"success": False, "message": "à¦¶à§à¦§à§à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦¸à¦¿à¦ªà¦¾à¦² à¦•à¦°à§à¦¤à§ƒà¦• à¦…à¦¨à§à¦®à§‹à¦¦à¦¿à¦¤ à¦•à§à¦²à§‡à¦‡à¦®à¦‡ à¦ªà¦°à¦¿à¦¶à§‹à¦§ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¸à¦®à§à¦­à¦¬à¥¤"},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST
            )

        claim.status = TeacherSalaryClaim.ClaimStatus.DISBURSED
        claim.disbursed_by = request.user
        claim.disbursed_at = timezone.now()
        claim.payment_reference = request.data.get('payment_reference', 'CASH/MFS')
        claim.save()

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": f"à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦•à¦•à§‡ à¦•à§à¦²à§‡à¦‡à¦®à§‡à¦° {claim.requested_amount} à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦•à¦¾ à¦¸à¦«à¦²à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¦à¦¾à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤",
            "data": TeacherSalaryClaimSerializer(claim).data
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class TransportVehicleViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    queryset = TransportVehicle.objects.all()
    serializer_class = TransportVehicleSerializer
    permission_classes = [IsAccountantOrPrincipal]
    
    @action(detail=False, methods=['get'], url_path='my-route')
    def my_route(self, request):
        if request.user.role != User.Role.STUDENT or not hasattr(request.user, 'student_profile'):
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "à¦¶à§à¦§à§à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦°à¦¾ à¦à¦‡ à¦¤à¦¥à§à¦¯ à¦¦à§‡à¦–à¦¤à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à§‡à¥¤"}, status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)

        student = request.user.student_profile
        if not student.uses_transport or not student.assigned_vehicle:
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "à¦†à¦ªà¦¨à¦¿ à¦•à§‹à¦¨à§‹ à¦ªà¦°à¦¿à¦¬à¦¹à¦¨ à¦¸à§à¦¬à¦¿à¦§à¦¾à¦° à¦¸à¦¾à¦¥à§‡ à¦¯à§à¦•à§à¦¤ à¦¨à¦¨à¥¤"}, status=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND)

        vehicle = student.assigned_vehicle
        active_alert = VehicleBreakdownAlert.objects.filter(vehicle=vehicle, is_resolved=False).first()

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "vehicle_number": vehicle.vehicle_number,
            "driver_name": vehicle.driver_name,
            "driver_phone": vehicle.driver_phone,
            "route_name": vehicle.route_name,
            "stoppages": vehicle.stoppages,
            "has_emergency_breakdown": active_alert is not None,
            "active_alert": VehicleBreakdownAlertSerializer(active_alert).data if active_alert else None,
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    @action(detail=True, methods=['post'], permission_classes=[IsAccountantOrPrincipal], url_path='emergency-breakdown')
    @transaction.atomic
    def emergency_breakdown(self, request, pk=None):
        vehicle = self.get_object()
        location = request.data.get('location', 'à¦…à¦œà¦¾à¦¨à¦¾ à¦¸à§à¦¥à¦¾à¦¨')
        reason = request.data.get('reason', 'à¦¡à§à¦°à¦¾à¦‡à¦­à¦¾à¦° à¦•à¦°à§à¦¤à§ƒà¦• à¦«à§‹à¦¨à§‡ à¦°à¦¿à¦ªà§‹à¦°à§à¦Ÿà¦•à§ƒà¦¤ à¦¯à¦¾à¦¨à§à¦¤à§à¦°à¦¿à¦• à¦¤à§à¦°à§à¦Ÿà¦¿')
        
        default_msg = f"à¦œà¦°à§à¦°à¦¿ à¦¬à¦¿à¦œà§à¦žà¦ªà§à¦¤à¦¿: {vehicle.route_name}-à¦à¦° à¦—à¦¾à§œà¦¿à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦¤à§‡ ({vehicle.vehicle_number}) {location}-à¦ à¦¯à¦¾à¦¨à§à¦¤à§à¦°à¦¿à¦• à¦¤à§à¦°à§à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦¦à§‡à¦–à¦¾ à¦¦à¦¿à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤ à¦¬à¦¿à¦•à¦²à§à¦ª à¦¬à§à¦¯à¦¬à¦¸à§à¦¥à¦¾ à¦—à§à¦°à¦¹à¦£ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à¦šà§à¦›à§‡à¥¤"
        alert_msg = request.data.get('alert_message', default_msg)

        alert = VehicleBreakdownAlert.objects.create(
            vehicle=vehicle,
            location=location,
            reason=reason,
            alert_message=alert_msg,
            reported_by=request.user
        )

        passengers = vehicle.passengers.filter(user__is_active=True).select_related('user')
        targeted_recipients = [
            {
                "student_id": p.student_unique_id,
                "student_name": p.user.get_full_name(),
                "guardian_phone": p.guardian_phone
            }
            for p in passengers
        ]

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": f"{vehicle.vehicle_number} à¦—à¦¾à§œà¦¿à¦° à¦œà¦°à§à¦°à¦¿ à¦¬à¦¿à¦•à¦² à¦¨à§‹à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦¶ à¦œà¦¾à¦°à¦¿ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤ à¦®à§‹à¦Ÿ {len(targeted_recipients)} à¦œà¦¨ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€ à¦“ à¦…à¦­à¦¿à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à¦• à¦¤à¦¾à§Žà¦•à§à¦·à¦£à¦¿à¦• à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦²à¦¾à¦°à§à¦Ÿ à¦ªà¦¾à¦¬à§‡à¦¨à¥¤",
            "alert": VehicleBreakdownAlertSerializer(alert).data,
            "dispatched_recipients_count": len(targeted_recipients),
            "targeted_recipients": targeted_recipients
        }, status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)

class VehicleMonthlyBillViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    queryset = VehicleMonthlyBill.objects.select_related('vehicle', 'settled_by').all()
    serializer_class = VehicleMonthlyBillSerializer
    permission_classes = [IsAccountantOrPrincipal]

    @action(detail=False, methods=['post'], url_path='generate')
    @transaction.atomic
    def generate_monthly_bill(self, request):
        vehicle_id = request.data.get('vehicle_id')
        month = request.data.get('month')
        year = request.data.get('year')

        if not vehicle_id or not month or not year:
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "vehicle_id, month à¦à¦¬à¦‚ year à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¦à¦¾à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦†à¦¬à¦¶à§à¦¯à¦•à¥¤"}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        try:
            vehicle = TransportVehicle.objects.get(id=vehicle_id)
        except TransportVehicle.DoesNotExist:
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¦à¦¤à§à¦¤ à¦—à¦¾à§œà¦¿à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦–à§à¦à¦œà§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾ à¦¯à¦¾à§Ÿà¦¨à¦¿à¥¤"}, status=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND)

        total_days_run = VehicleDailyLog.objects.filter(
            vehicle=vehicle,
            date__year=year,
            date__month=month,
            ran_today=True
        ).count()

        advances_sum = DriverAdvanceDeduction.objects.filter(
            vehicle=vehicle,
            date__year=year,
            date__month=month
        ).aggregate(total=Sum('amount'))['total'] or Decimal('0.00')

        bill, created = VehicleMonthlyBill.objects.update_or_create(
            vehicle=vehicle,
            month=month,
            year=year,
            defaults={
                'total_days_run': total_days_run,
                'daily_rate': vehicle.daily_rate,
                'total_deductions': advances_sum,
            }
        )

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": f"{vehicle.vehicle_number} à¦à¦° {month}/{year} à¦®à¦¾à¦¸à§‡à¦° à¦¨à¦¿à¦Ÿ à¦¬à¦¿à¦² à¦¸à¦«à¦²à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¸à§à¦¤à§à¦¤ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤",
            "data": VehicleMonthlyBillSerializer(bill).data
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK if not created else status.HTTP_201_CREATED)

    @action(detail=True, methods=['patch'], url_path='settle')
    def settle_bill(self, request, pk=None):
        bill = self.get_object()
        bill.status = VehicleMonthlyBill.BillStatus.SETTLED
        bill.settled_by = request.user
        bill.settled_at = timezone.now()
        bill.payment_reference = request.data.get('payment_reference', 'CASH/BANK')
        bill.save()

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": f"à¦—à¦¾à§œà¦¿à¦° à¦¨à¦¿à¦Ÿ à¦¬à¦¿à¦² ({bill.net_payable} BDT) à¦¸à¦«à¦²à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ à¦ªà¦°à¦¿à¦¶à§‹à¦§à¦¿à¦¤ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤",
            "data": VehicleMonthlyBillSerializer(bill).data
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    permission_classes = [IsTeacherOrPrincipal]

    def get_queryset(self):

            return queryset.filter(in_charge=user)
        if user.role in [User.Role.ACCOUNTANT, User.Role.PRINCIPAL] or user.is_superuser:
            return queryset
        return queryset.none()

    def perform_create(self, serializer):
        if self.request.user.role != User.Role.TEACHER:
            raise PermissionDenied("à¦¶à§à¦§à§à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° à¦¶à§à¦°à§‡à¦£à¦¿ à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œ à¦•à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¶ à¦¹à¦¸à§à¦¤à¦¾à¦¨à§à¦¤à¦°à§‡à¦° à¦°à¦¿à¦•à§‹à¦¯à¦¼à§‡à¦¸à§à¦Ÿ à¦ªà¦¾à¦ à¦¾à¦¤à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à§‡à¦¨à¥¤")
        serializer.save(in_charge=self.request.user)

    @action(detail=True, methods=['patch'], permission_classes=[IsAccountantOrPrincipal], url_path='verify-cash')
    def verify_cash(self, request, pk=None):

            "success": True,
            "message": f"à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦• {handover.in_charge.get_full_name()} à¦à¦° à¦¨à¦¿à¦•à¦Ÿ à¦¹à¦¤à§‡ {handover.amount} à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦•à¦¾ à¦¸à¦«à¦²à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ à¦¨à¦—à¦¦ à¦—à§à¦°à¦¹à¦£ à¦“ à¦­à§‡à¦°à¦¿à¦«à¦¾à¦‡ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤",
            "data": InchargeCashHandoverSerializer(handover).data
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    @action(detail=True, methods=['post'], url_path='pay')
    def make_payment(self, request, pk=None):

        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)
        payment = serializer.save(purchase=purchase, paid_by=request.user)

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": f"à¦­à§‡à¦¨à§à¦¡à¦°à¦•à§‡ {payment.amount_paid} à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦•à¦¾ à¦ªà¦°à¦¿à¦¶à§‹à¦§ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤ à¦…à¦¬à¦¶à¦¿à¦·à§à¦Ÿ à¦¬à¦•à§‡à§Ÿà¦¾: {purchase.due_amount} à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦•à¦¾à¥¤",
            "purchase_status": SupplierPurchaseSerializer(purchase).data
        }, status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)


class DailyCashClosingView(APIView):
    """
    GET /api/v1/finance/analytics/daily-closing/?date=YYYY-MM-DD
    à¦¦à§ˆà¦¨à¦¿à¦• à¦•à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¶ à¦•à§à¦²à§‹à¦œà¦¿à¦‚ à¦…à¦¡à¦¿à¦Ÿ à¦°à¦¿à¦ªà§‹à¦°à§à¦Ÿ (à¦¡à¦¾à¦¬à¦²-à¦•à¦¾à¦‰à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦‚ à¦®à§à¦•à§à¦¤ à¦¨à¦¿à¦°à§à¦­à§à¦² à¦¹à¦¿à¦¸à¦¾à¦¬)à¥¤
    """
    permission_classes = [IsAccountantOrPrincipal]

    def get(self, request):
        date_str = request.query_params.get('date')
        target_date = datetime.strptime(date_str, '%Y-%m-%d').date() if date_str else timezone.localdate()

        # à§§. à¦¸à¦°à¦¾à¦¸à¦°à¦¿ à¦•à¦¾à¦‰à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦°à§‡ à¦œà¦®à¦¾ à¦¹à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾ à¦•à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¶ (à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦•à¦¾à¦‰à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¨à§à¦Ÿ à¦¬à¦¾ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦¸à¦¿à¦ªà¦¾à¦² à¦•à¦°à§à¦¤à§ƒà¦• à¦¸à¦‚à¦—à§ƒà¦¹à§€à¦¤)
        counter_cash_collected = PaymentTransaction.objects.filter(
            created_at__date=target_date,
            payment_method=PaymentTransaction.PaymentMethod.CASH,
            collected_by__role__in=[User.Role.ACCOUNTANT, User.Role.PRINCIPAL]
        ).aggregate(total=Sum('amount_paid'))['total'] or Decimal('0.00')

        # à§¨. à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œà¦¦à§‡à¦° à¦¸à¦‚à¦—à§ƒà¦¹à§€à¦¤ à¦•à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¶ à¦¯à¦¾ à¦†à¦œ à¦­à§‡à¦°à¦¿à¦«à¦¾à¦‡ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡ à¦¡à§à¦°à§Ÿà¦¾à¦°à§‡ à¦¢à§à¦•à§‡à¦›à§‡
        incharge_handover_cash = InchargeCashHandover.objects.filter(
            handover_date=target_date,
            status=InchargeCashHandover.HandoverStatus.VERIFIED
        ).aggregate(total=Sum('amount'))['total'] or Decimal('0.00')

        # à§©. à¦†à¦œ à¦•à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¶à§‡ à¦ªà¦°à¦¿à¦¶à§‹à¦§à¦¿à¦¤ à¦…à¦¨à§à¦®à§‹à¦¦à¦¿à¦¤ à¦–à¦°à¦š
        cash_expenses = CollegeExpense.objects.filter(
            expense_date=target_date,
            status=CollegeExpense.ExpenseStatus.APPROVED
        ).aggregate(total=Sum('amount'))['total'] or Decimal('0.00')

        # à§ª. à¦†à¦œ à¦•à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¶ à¦¡à§à¦°à§Ÿà¦¾à¦° à¦¥à§‡à¦•à§‡ à¦¬à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦‚à¦•à§‡ à¦œà¦®à¦¾ à¦¹à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾ à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦•à¦¾
        deposited_to_bank = BankDeposit.objects.filter(
            deposit_date=target_date
        ).aggregate(total=Sum('amount'))['total'] or Decimal('0.00')

        # à§«. à¦­à§‡à¦¨à§à¦¡à¦°à¦¦à§‡à¦° à¦•à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¶à§‡ à¦ªà¦°à¦¿à¦¶à§‹à¦§à¦¿à¦¤ à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦•à¦¾
        supplier_paid = SupplierPayment.objects.filter(
            payment_date=target_date,
            payment_method=PaymentTransaction.PaymentMethod.CASH
        ).aggregate(total=Sum('amount_paid'))['total'] or Decimal('0.00')

        # à¦¨à¦¿à¦Ÿ à¦•à§à¦²à§‹à¦œà¦¿à¦‚ à¦•à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¶ à¦¹à¦¿à¦¸à¦¾à¦¬ (à¦•à§‹à¦¨à§‹ à¦¡à¦¾à¦¬à¦² à¦•à¦¾à¦‰à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦‚ à¦¨à§‡à¦‡)
        total_inflow = counter_cash_collected + incharge_handover_cash
        total_outflow = cash_expenses + deposited_to_bank + supplier_paid
        net_cash_in_hand = total_inflow - total_outflow

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "report_title": "DAILY CASH CLOSING SHEET",
            "date": target_date.strftime('%Y-%m-%d'),
            "cash_summary": {
                "direct_counter_cash_collection": float(counter_cash_collected),
                "incharge_handed_over_cash": float(incharge_handover_cash),
                "total_cash_inflow": float(total_inflow),
                "total_expense_paid_cash": float(cash_expenses),
                "cash_deposited_to_bank": float(deposited_to_bank),
                "supplier_payments_cash": float(supplier_paid),
                "total_cash_outflow": float(total_outflow),
                "net_closing_cash_in_hand": float(net_cash_in_hand),
            }
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class MasterDefaulterListView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAccountantOrPrincipal]

    def get(self, request):
        min_due = Decimal(str(request.query_params.get('min_due', '1.00')))
        section_id = request.query_params.get('section')

        invoices = StudentInvoice.objects.filter(due_amount__gte=min_due).select_related('student', 'student__user', 'section')
        if section_id:
            invoices = invoices.filter(section_id=section_id)

        defaulters_map = {}
        for inv in invoices:
            stu_id = inv.student_id
            if stu_id not in defaulters_map:
                defaulters_map[stu_id] = {
                    "student_unique_id": inv.student.student_unique_id,
                    "student_name": inv.student.user.get_full_name(),
                    "roll_number": inv.student.roll_number,
                    "section": str(inv.section),
                    "guardian_phone": inv.student.guardian_phone,
                    "total_unpaid_months": 0,
                    "total_due_amount": Decimal('0.00'),
                }
            defaulters_map[stu_id]['total_unpaid_months'] += 1
            defaulters_map[stu_id]['total_due_amount'] += inv.due_amount

        result_list = list(defaulters_map.values())
        result_list.sort(key=lambda x: x['total_due_amount'], reverse=True)

        total_due_college = sum(item['total_due_amount'] for item in result_list)

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "total_defaulter_students": len(result_list),
            "total_outstanding_due": float(total_due_college),
            "defaulters": [
                {**item, "total_due_amount": float(item["total_due_amount"])}
                for item in result_list
            ]
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

from datetime import timedelta
from decimal import Decimal
from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError
from django.db import models
from django.utils import timezone
from apps.common.models import BaseModel
from apps.users_auth.models import StudentProfile, User

class BookCategory(BaseModel):
    name = models.CharField(max_length=100, unique=True)
    description = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Book Category'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Book Categories'

    def __str__(self):
        return self.name


class Book(BaseModel):
    """
    à¦²à¦¾à¦‡à¦¬à§à¦°à§‡à¦°à¦¿à¦° à¦¬à¦‡ à¦•à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦²à¦— à¦“ à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà¦• à¦®à¦¡à§‡à¦²à¥¤
    """
    title = models.CharField(max_length=255, db_index=True)
    author = models.CharField(max_length=200)
    isbn = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True, unique=True, null=True)
    category = models.ForeignKey(BookCategory, on_delete=models.PROTECT, related_name='books')
    shelf_location = models.CharField(max_length=50, help_text="à¦¯à§‡à¦®à¦¨: à¦†à¦²à¦®à¦¾à¦°à¦¿-à§©, à¦¤à¦¾à¦•-à¦¬à¦¿")
    
    total_copies = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=1)
    available_copies = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=1)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Book'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Books'
        ordering = ['title']

    def clean(self):
        if self.available_copies > self.total_copies:
            raise ValidationError("à¦…à¦¬à¦¶à¦¿à¦·à§à¦Ÿ à¦¬à¦‡à§Ÿà§‡à¦° à¦¸à¦‚à¦–à§à¦¯à¦¾ à¦®à§‹à¦Ÿ à¦•à¦ªà¦¿à¦° à¦šà§‡à§Ÿà§‡ à¦¬à§‡à¦¶à¦¿ à¦¹à¦¤à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à§‡ à¦¨à¦¾à¥¤")

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.title} - {self.author} ({self.available_copies}/{self.total_copies} Copies Available)"


class BookBorrowRequest(BaseModel):
    """
    à§ª-à¦§à¦¾à¦ªà§‡à¦° à¦²à¦¾à¦‡à¦¬à§à¦°à§‡à¦°à¦¿ à¦«à§à¦²à§‹:
    à§§. Student à¦°à¦¿à¦•à§‹à¦¯à¦¼à§‡à¦¸à§à¦Ÿ à¦•à¦°à¦¬à§‡ (REQUESTED)
    à§¨. à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦• à¦¤à¦¦à¦¾à¦°à¦•à¦¿ à¦•à¦°à§‡ à¦…à¦¨à§à¦®à§‹à¦¦à¦¨ à¦¦à§‡à¦¬à§‡à¦¨ (TEACHER_APPROVED)
    à§©. à¦²à¦¾à¦‡à¦¬à§à¦°à§‡à¦°à¦¿ à¦®à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¨à§‡à¦œà¦¾à¦° à¦¬à¦‡ à¦‡à¦¸à§à¦¯à§ à¦•à¦°à¦¬à§‡à¦¨ à¦“ à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà¦• à¦•à¦®à¦¬à§‡ (ISSUED)
    à§ª. à¦¬à¦‡ à¦«à§‡à¦°à¦¤ à¦¦à¦¿à¦²à§‡ à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà¦• à¦¬à¦¾à§œà¦¬à§‡ (RETURNED)
    """
    class BorrowStatus(models.TextChoices):
        REQUESTED = 'REQUESTED', 'à¦…à¦¨à§à¦°à§‹à¦§ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡ (Requested by Student)'
        TEACHER_APPROVED = 'TEACHER_APPROVED', 'à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦• à¦•à¦°à§à¦¤à§ƒà¦• à¦…à¦¨à§à¦®à§‹à¦¦à¦¿à¦¤ (Approved by Teacher)'
        TEACHER_REJECTED = 'TEACHER_REJECTED', 'à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦• à¦•à¦°à§à¦¤à§ƒà¦• à¦¬à¦¾à¦¤à¦¿à¦² (Rejected)'
        ISSUED = 'ISSUED', 'à¦¬à¦‡ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¦à¦¾à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡ (Issued from Library)'
        RETURNED = 'RETURNED', 'à¦«à§‡à¦°à¦¤ à¦¸à¦®à§à¦ªà¦¨à§à¦¨ (Returned to Library)'

    student = models.ForeignKey(StudentProfile, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='borrowed_books')
    book = models.ForeignKey(Book, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='borrow_records')
    reason = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True, help_text="à¦¬à¦‡ à¦¨à§‡à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾à¦° à¦‰à¦¦à§à¦¦à§‡à¦¶à§à¦¯ à¦¬à¦¾ à¦•à¦¾à¦°à¦£")
    status = models.CharField(max_length=25, choices=BorrowStatus.choices, default=BorrowStatus.REQUESTED, db_index=True)

    # à¦§à¦¾à¦ª à§¨: à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦• à¦¤à¦¦à¦¾à¦°à¦•à¦¿
    teacher_approver = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, related_name='approved_book_requests')
    teacher_approved_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    teacher_remarks = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True)

    # à¦§à¦¾à¦ª à§© à¦“ à§ª: à¦²à¦¾à¦‡à¦¬à§à¦°à§‡à¦°à¦¿ à¦®à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¨à§‡à¦œà¦¾à¦° à¦‡à¦¸à§à¦¯à§ à¦“ à¦°à¦¿à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦°à§à¦¨
    issued_by = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, related_name='issued_library_books')
    issued_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    due_date = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True)
    returned_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    late_fine = models.DecimalField(max_digits=8, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Book Borrow Request'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Book Borrow Requests'
        ordering = ['-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.student.roll_number} - {self.book.title} ({self.status})"

from rest_framework import serializers
from apps.library.models import Book, BookCategory, BookBorrowRequest

class BookCategorySerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    class Meta:
        model = BookCategory
        fields = '__all__'


class BookSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    category_name = serializers.CharField(source='category.name', read_only=True)

    class Meta:
        model = Book
        fields = ['id', 'title', 'author', 'isbn', 'category', 'category_name', 'shelf_location', 'total_copies', 'available_copies', 'created_at']


class BookBorrowRequestSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    student_name = serializers.CharField(source='student.user.get_full_name', read_only=True)
    student_unique_id = serializers.CharField(source='student.student_unique_id', read_only=True)
    roll_number = serializers.IntegerField(source='student.roll_number', read_only=True)
    book_title = serializers.CharField(source='book.title', read_only=True)
    status_display = serializers.CharField(source='get_status_display', read_only=True)
    teacher_name = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    issued_by_name = serializers.SerializerMethodField()

    class Meta:
        model = BookBorrowRequest
        fields = [
            'id', 'student', 'student_unique_id', 'roll_number', 'student_name', 
            'book', 'book_title', 'reason', 'status', 'status_display', 
            'teacher_approver', 'teacher_name', 'teacher_approved_at', 'teacher_remarks',
            'issued_by', 'issued_by_name', 'issued_at', 'due_date', 'returned_at', 'late_fine', 'created_at'
        ]
        read_only_fields = [
            'id', 'student', 'status', 'teacher_approver', 'teacher_approved_at', 
            'issued_by', 'issued_at', 'due_date', 'returned_at', 'late_fine', 'created_at'
        ]

    def get_teacher_name(self, obj) -> str | None:
        if obj.teacher_approver:
            return obj.teacher_approver.get_full_name() or obj.teacher_approver.username
        return None

    def get_issued_by_name(self, obj) -> str | None:
        if obj.issued_by:
            return obj.issued_by.get_full_name() or obj.issued_by.username
        return None
