from datetime import timedelta
from decimal import Decimal
from django.db import transaction
from django.utils import timezone
from rest_framework import serializers
from rest_framework import status, viewsets
from rest_framework.decorators import action
from rest_framework.exceptions import PermissionDenied
from rest_framework.permissions import IsAuthenticated
from rest_framework.response import Response

from apps.common.permissions import IsLibraryManagerOrPrincipal, IsTeacherOrPrincipal
from apps.library.models import Book, BookBorrowRequest, BookCategory
from apps.library.serializers import (
    BookBorrowRequestSerializer,
    BookCategorySerializer,
    BookSerializer,
)
from apps.users_auth.models import User


class BookViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    """
    à¦¬à¦‡à§Ÿà§‡à¦° à¦•à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦²à¦— à¦“ à¦¸à¦¾à¦°à§à¦š:
    - à¦¤à¦¾à¦²à¦¿à¦•à¦¾ à¦“ à¦¬à¦¿à¦¸à§à¦¤à¦¾à¦°à¦¿à¦¤ à¦¦à§‡à¦–à¦¾: à¦¸à¦•à¦² à¦²à¦—à¦‡à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€ à¦“ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦•à¥¤
    - à¦¬à¦‡ à¦¯à§‹à¦—/à¦†à¦ªà¦¡à§‡à¦Ÿ: à¦²à¦¾à¦‡à¦¬à§à¦°à§‡à¦°à¦¿ à¦®à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¨à§‡à¦œà¦¾à¦° à¦…à¦¥à¦¬à¦¾ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦¸à¦¿à¦ªà¦¾à¦²à¥¤
    """
    queryset = Book.objects.select_related('category').all()
    serializer_class = BookSerializer

    def get_permissions(self):
        if self.action in ['list', 'retrieve']:
            return [IsAuthenticated()]
        return [IsLibraryManagerOrPrincipal()]


class BookBorrowRequestViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    """
    à§ª-à¦§à¦¾à¦ªà§‡à¦° à¦¬à¦‡ à¦‡à¦¸à§à¦¯à§ à¦“ à¦°à¦¿à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦°à§à¦¨ à¦‡à¦žà§à¦œà¦¿à¦¨:
    à§§. Student à¦†à¦¬à§‡à¦¦à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦¬à§‡ (POST)
    à§¨. à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦• à¦¤à¦¦à¦¾à¦°à¦•à¦¿ à¦•à¦°à§‡ à¦…à¦¨à§à¦®à§‹à¦¦à¦¨ à¦¦à§‡à¦¬à§‡à¦¨ (PATCH /<id>/teacher-review/)
    à§©. à¦²à¦¾à¦‡à¦¬à§à¦°à§‡à¦°à¦¿ à¦®à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¨à§‡à¦œà¦¾à¦° à¦¬à¦‡ à¦‡à¦¸à§à¦¯à§ à¦•à¦°à¦¬à§‡à¦¨ à¦“ à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà¦• à¦•à¦®à¦¬à§‡ (PATCH /<id>/issue-book/)
    à§ª. à¦¬à¦‡ à¦«à§‡à¦°à¦¤ à¦¦à¦¿à¦²à§‡ à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà¦• à¦¬à¦¾à§œà¦¬à§‡ à¦“ à¦²à§‡à¦Ÿ à¦«à¦¾à¦‡à¦¨ à¦¹à¦¿à¦¸à¦¾à¦¬ à¦¹à¦¬à§‡ (PATCH /<id>/return-book/)
    """
    serializer_class = BookBorrowRequestSerializer

    def get_permissions(self):
        return [IsAuthenticated()]

    def get_queryset(self):
        user = self.request.user
        queryset = BookBorrowRequest.objects.select_related('student', 'student__user', 'book', 'teacher_approver', 'issued_by').all()

        if user.role == User.Role.STUDENT and hasattr(user, 'student_profile'):
            return queryset.filter(student=user.student_profile)
        
        # à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦•, à¦²à¦¾à¦‡à¦¬à§à¦°à§‡à¦°à¦¿ à¦®à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¨à§‡à¦œà¦¾à¦° à¦…à¦¥à¦¬à¦¾ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦¸à¦¿à¦ªà¦¾à¦² à¦¸à¦¬ à¦†à¦¬à§‡à¦¦à¦¨ à¦¦à§‡à¦–à¦¤à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦¬à§‡à¦¨ (à¦¯à§‡ à¦•à§‹à¦¨à§‹ à¦¬à¦¿à¦·à§Ÿ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦• à¦…à¦¨à§à¦®à§‹à¦¦à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦¤à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à¦¬à§‡à¦¨)
        if user.role == User.Role.TEACHER or user.is_library_manager or user.role == User.Role.PRINCIPAL or user.is_superuser:
            return queryset
            
        return queryset.none()

    def perform_create(self, serializer):
        user = self.request.user
        if user.role != User.Role.STUDENT or not hasattr(user, 'student_profile'):
            raise PermissionDenied("à¦¶à§à¦§à§à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦°à¦¾ à¦¬à¦‡à§Ÿà§‡à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ à¦†à¦¬à§‡à¦¦à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦¤à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à§‡à¥¤")

        book = serializer.validated_data['book']
        if book.available_copies < 1:
            raise serializers.ValidationError({"book": "à¦¦à§à¦ƒà¦–à¦¿à¦¤, à¦à¦‡ à¦¬à¦‡à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦° à¦•à§‹à¦¨à§‹ à¦•à¦ªà¦¿ à¦¬à¦°à§à¦¤à¦®à¦¾à¦¨à§‡ à¦²à¦¾à¦‡à¦¬à§à¦°à§‡à¦°à¦¿à¦¤à§‡ à¦…à¦¬à¦¶à¦¿à¦·à§à¦Ÿ à¦¨à§‡à¦‡à¥¤"})

        serializer.save(student=user.student_profile, status=BookBorrowRequest.BorrowStatus.REQUESTED)

    @action(detail=True, methods=['patch'], permission_classes=[IsTeacherOrPrincipal], url_path='teacher-review')
    def teacher_review(self, request, pk=None):
        """
        à¦§à¦¾à¦ª à§¨: à¦¬à¦¿à¦·à§Ÿ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦• à¦•à¦°à§à¦¤à§ƒà¦• à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦° à¦¬à¦‡ à¦†à¦¬à§‡à¦¦à¦¨ à¦…à¦¨à§à¦®à§‹à¦¦à¦¨à¥¤
        """
        borrow_req = self.get_object()
        new_status = request.data.get('status', '').upper()

        if new_status not in [BookBorrowRequest.BorrowStatus.TEACHER_APPROVED, BookBorrowRequest.BorrowStatus.TEACHER_REJECTED]:
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "status à¦¹à¦¿à¦¸à§‡à¦¬à§‡ TEACHER_APPROVED à¦…à¦¥à¦¬à¦¾ TEACHER_REJECTED à¦¦à¦¿à¦¨à¥¤"}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        borrow_req.status = new_status
        borrow_req.teacher_approver = request.user
        borrow_req.teacher_approved_at = timezone.now()
        borrow_req.teacher_remarks = request.data.get('remarks', '')
        borrow_req.save()

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": f"à¦†à¦¬à§‡à¦¦à¦¨à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦• à¦•à¦°à§à¦¤à§ƒà¦• '{borrow_req.get_status_display()}' à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤",
            "data": BookBorrowRequestSerializer(borrow_req).data
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    @action(detail=True, methods=['patch'], permission_classes=[IsLibraryManagerOrPrincipal], url_path='issue-book')
    @transaction.atomic
    def issue_book(self, request, pk=None):
        """
        à¦§à¦¾à¦ª à§©: à¦²à¦¾à¦‡à¦¬à§à¦°à§‡à¦°à¦¿ à¦®à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¨à§‡à¦œà¦¾à¦° à¦•à¦°à§à¦¤à§ƒà¦• à¦¬à¦‡ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¦à¦¾à¦¨ âž” à¦¸à§‡à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà§à¦°à¦¾à¦² à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà¦• à¦®à¦¾à¦‡à¦¨à¦¾à¦¸ à¦¹à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾à¥¤
        """
        borrow_req = self.get_object()
        borrow_req = BookBorrowRequest.objects.select_for_update().get(pk=borrow_req.pk)
        book = Book.objects.select_for_update().get(pk=borrow_req.book_id)

        if borrow_req.status != BookBorrowRequest.BorrowStatus.TEACHER_APPROVED:
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦•à§‡à¦° à¦…à¦¨à§à¦®à§‹à¦¦à¦¨ à¦›à¦¾à§œà¦¾ à¦¬à¦‡ à¦‡à¦¸à§à¦¯à§ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¯à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ à¦¨à¦¾à¥¤"}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        if book.available_copies < 1:
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "à¦²à¦¾à¦‡à¦¬à§à¦°à§‡à¦°à¦¿à¦¤à§‡ à¦¬à¦‡à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦° à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà¦• à¦¶à§‡à¦· à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡ à¦—à§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤"}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        try:
            days = int(request.data.get('days', 7))
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "days à¦à¦•à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦ªà§‚à¦°à§à¦£à¦¸à¦‚à¦–à§à¦¯à¦¾ à¦¹à¦¤à§‡ à¦¹à¦¬à§‡à¥¤"}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)
        if days < 1 or days > 365:
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "days-à¦à¦° à¦®à¦¾à¦¨ à§§ à¦¥à§‡à¦•à§‡ à§©à§¬à§«-à¦à¦° à¦®à¦§à§à¦¯à§‡ à¦¹à¦¤à§‡ à¦¹à¦¬à§‡à¥¤"}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        # à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà¦• à¦®à¦¾à¦‡à¦¨à¦¾à¦¸ à¦•à¦°à¦¾
        book.available_copies -= 1
        book.save()

        borrow_req.status = BookBorrowRequest.BorrowStatus.ISSUED
        borrow_req.issued_by = request.user
        borrow_req.issued_at = timezone.now()
        borrow_req.due_date = timezone.localdate() + timedelta(days=days)
        borrow_req.save()

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": f"à¦¬à¦‡à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦¸à¦«à¦²à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¦à¦¾à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤ à¦«à§‡à¦°à¦¤ à¦¦à§‡à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾à¦° à¦¶à§‡à¦· à¦¤à¦¾à¦°à¦¿à¦–: {borrow_req.due_date}",
            "data": BookBorrowRequestSerializer(borrow_req).data
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    @action(detail=True, methods=['patch'], permission_classes=[IsLibraryManagerOrPrincipal], url_path='return-book')
    @transaction.atomic
    def return_book(self, request, pk=None):
        """
        à¦§à¦¾à¦ª à§ª: à¦¬à¦‡ à¦«à§‡à¦°à¦¤ à¦—à§à¦°à¦¹à¦£ âž” à¦¸à§‡à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà§à¦°à¦¾à¦² à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà¦• à¦¬à§ƒà¦¦à§à¦§à¦¿ à¦“ à¦¬à¦¿à¦²à¦®à§à¦¬ à¦«à¦¿ à¦¹à¦¿à¦¸à¦¾à¦¬à¥¤
        """
        borrow_req = self.get_object()
        borrow_req = BookBorrowRequest.objects.select_for_update().get(pk=borrow_req.pk)

        if borrow_req.status != BookBorrowRequest.BorrowStatus.ISSUED:
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "à¦à¦‡ à¦¬à¦‡à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦¬à¦°à§à¦¤à¦®à¦¾à¦¨à§‡ à¦‡à¦¸à§à¦¯à§à¦•à§ƒà¦¤ à¦…à¦¬à¦¸à§à¦¥à¦¾à§Ÿ à¦¨à§‡à¦‡à¥¤"}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        # à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà¦• à§§à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦¬à¦¾à§œà¦¾à¦¨à§‹
        book = Book.objects.select_for_update().get(pk=borrow_req.book_id)
        book.available_copies += 1
        book.save()

        today = timezone.localdate()
        late_fine = Decimal('0.00')

        # à¦¬à¦¿à¦²à¦®à§à¦¬ à¦«à¦¿ à¦¹à¦¿à¦¸à¦¾à¦¬ (à¦¨à¦¿à¦°à§à¦§à¦¾à¦°à¦¿à¦¤ à¦¤à¦¾à¦°à¦¿à¦– à¦ªà¦¾à¦° à¦¹à¦²à§‡ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¤à¦¿à¦¦à¦¿à¦¨ à§§à§¦ à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦•à¦¾ à¦œà¦°à¦¿à¦®à¦¾à¦¨à¦¾)
        if borrow_req.due_date and today > borrow_req.due_date:
            overdue_days = (today - borrow_req.due_date).days
            late_fine = Decimal(str(overdue_days * 10))

        borrow_req.status = BookBorrowRequest.BorrowStatus.RETURNED
        borrow_req.returned_at = timezone.now()
        borrow_req.late_fine = late_fine
        borrow_req.save()

        msg = f"à¦¬à¦‡à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦¸à¦«à¦²à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ à¦«à§‡à¦°à¦¤ à¦—à§à¦°à¦¹à¦£ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤"
        if late_fine > 0:
            msg += f" {borrow_req.due_date} à¦¤à¦¾à¦°à¦¿à¦–à§‡à¦° à¦ªà¦° à¦œà¦®à¦¾ à¦¦à§‡à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾à§Ÿ {late_fine} à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦•à¦¾ à¦¬à¦¿à¦²à¦®à§à¦¬ à¦«à¦¿ à¦§à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¯ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤"

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": msg,
            "data": BookBorrowRequestSerializer(borrow_req).data
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class BookCategoryViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    queryset = BookCategory.objects.all()
    serializer_class = BookCategorySerializer
    permission_classes = [IsLibraryManagerOrPrincipal]

from datetime import time
from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError

class TeacherAttendance(BaseModel):
    """
    à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦•à¦¦à§‡à¦° à¦¡à¦¿à¦œà¦¿à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦² à¦¹à¦¾à¦œà¦¿à¦°à¦¾, à¦…à¦Ÿà§‹à¦®à§‡à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦• à¦à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà§à¦°à¦¿ à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦‡à¦® à¦à¦¬à¦‚ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦¸à¦¿à¦ªà¦¾à¦² à¦•à¦°à§à¦¤à§ƒà¦• à¦…à¦¨à§à¦®à§‹à¦¦à¦¨à¥¤
    """
    class ApprovalStatus(models.TextChoices):
        PENDING = 'PENDING', 'à¦…à¦ªà§‡à¦•à§à¦·à¦®à¦¾à¦£ (Pending)'
        APPROVED = 'APPROVED', 'à¦…à¦¨à§à¦®à§‹à¦¦à¦¿à¦¤ (Approved)'
        REJECTED = 'REJECTED', 'à¦¬à¦¾à¦¤à¦¿à¦² (Rejected)'

    teacher = models.ForeignKey(
        User,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='teacher_attendances',
        limit_choices_to={'role': User.Role.TEACHER},
    )
    date = models.DateField(default=timezone.localdate, db_index=True)
    check_in_time = models.TimeField()
    check_out_time = models.TimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    is_late = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    remarks = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True)

    approval_status = models.CharField(
        max_length=15,
        choices=ApprovalStatus.choices,
        default=ApprovalStatus.PENDING,
        db_index=True,
    )
    approved_by = models.ForeignKey(
        User,
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name='verified_teacher_attendances',
    )
    approved_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Teacher Attendance'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Teacher Attendances'
        unique_together = ('teacher', 'date')
        ordering = ['-date', '-check_in_time']

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        # à¦¸à¦•à¦¾à¦² à§¯:à§§à§« à¦à¦° à¦ªà¦°à§‡ à¦šà§‡à¦•-à¦‡à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦²à§‡ à¦¸à§à¦¬à§Ÿà¦‚à¦•à§à¦°à¦¿à§Ÿà¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ Late à¦¹à¦¿à¦¸à§‡à¦¬à§‡ à¦šà¦¿à¦¹à§à¦¨à¦¿à¦¤ à¦¹à¦¬à§‡
        cutoff_time = time(9, 15, 0)
        if self.check_in_time and self.check_in_time > cutoff_time:
            self.is_late = True
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.teacher.username} - {self.date} ({self.approval_status})"


class Notice(BaseModel):
    """
    à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦¸à¦¿à¦ªà¦¾à¦² à¦“ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦•à¦¦à§‡à¦° à¦¨à§‹à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦¶ à¦¬à§‹à¦°à§à¦¡à¥¤
    """
    class AudienceType(models.TextChoices):
        ALL = 'ALL', 'à¦¸à¦•à¦²à§‡à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ (All Users)'
        TEACHERS = 'TEACHERS', 'à¦¶à§à¦§à§à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦•à¦¦à§‡à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ (Teachers Only)'
        STUDENTS = 'STUDENTS', 'à¦¸à¦•à¦² à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦¦à§‡à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ (All Students)'
        SECTION = 'SECTION', 'à¦¨à¦¿à¦°à§à¦¦à¦¿à¦·à§à¦Ÿ à¦¶à§à¦°à§‡à¦£à¦¿/à¦¶à¦¾à¦–à¦¾à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ (Specific Section)'

    title = models.CharField(max_length=255)
    content = models.TextField()
    attachment = models.FileField(upload_to='notices/%Y/%m/', null=True, blank=True)
    
    audience = models.CharField(
        max_length=15,
        choices=AudienceType.choices,
        default=AudienceType.ALL,
        db_index=True,
    )
    target_section = models.ForeignKey(
        AcademicSection,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name='notices',
        help_text="Audience à¦¯à¦¦à¦¿ SECTION à¦¹à§Ÿ, à¦¤à¦¬à§‡ à¦•à§‹à¦¨ à¦¶à¦¾à¦–à¦¾à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ à¦¨à§‹à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦¶ à¦¤à¦¾ à¦¸à¦¿à¦²à§‡à¦•à§à¦Ÿ à¦•à¦°à§à¦¨à¥¤",
    )
    created_by = models.ForeignKey(
        User,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='published_notices',
    )
    is_pinned = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Notice'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Notices'
        ordering = ['-is_pinned', '-created_at']

    def clean(self):
        if self.audience == self.AudienceType.SECTION and not self.target_section:
            raise ValidationError("à¦¨à¦¿à¦°à§à¦¦à¦¿à¦·à§à¦Ÿ à¦¶à¦¾à¦–à¦¾à¦° à¦¨à§‹à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦¶à§‡à¦° à¦•à§à¦·à§‡à¦¤à§à¦°à§‡ 'target_section' à¦¨à¦¿à¦°à§à¦¬à¦¾à¦šà¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦†à¦¬à¦¶à§à¦¯à¦•à¥¤")

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.title} ({self.get_audience_display()})"

class MarkerRequest(BaseModel):
    """
    à¦¹à§‹à§Ÿà¦¾à¦‡à¦Ÿà¦¬à§‹à¦°à§à¦¡ à¦®à¦¾à¦°à§à¦•à¦¾à¦° à¦Ÿà§à¦°à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦•à¦¾à¦° à¦®à¦¡à§‡à¦² (à¦¬à¦¿à¦•à¦²à§à¦ª à¦• à¦«à§à¦²à§‹)à¥¤
    à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦• à¦†à¦¬à§‡à¦¦à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦¬à§‡à¦¨ -> à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦¸à¦¿à¦ªà¦¾à¦²/à¦…à¦«à¦¿à¦¸ à¦‡à¦¸à§à¦¯à§ à¦•à¦°à¦¬à§‡à¦¨ -> à¦¬à§à¦¯à¦¬à¦¹à¦¾à¦° à¦¶à§‡à¦·à§‡ à¦°à¦¿à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦°à§à¦¨/à¦¡à¦¿à¦¸à¦ªà§‹à¦œ à¦¹à¦¬à§‡à¥¤

    returned_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    remarks = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True)
    is_acknowledged = models.BooleanField(default=False, help_text="à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦• à¦®à¦¾à¦°à§à¦•à¦¾à¦° à¦¬à§à¦à§‡ à¦ªà§‡à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¦¨ à¦•à¦¿ à¦¨à¦¾")
    acknowledged_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Marker Request'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Marker Requests'
        ordering = ['-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.teacher.get_full_name()} - {self.get_marker_color_display()} ({self.status})"


class StudentLeaveApplication(BaseModel):
    """
    à§¨-à¦§à¦¾à¦ªà§‡à¦° à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§à¦¡à§‡à¦¨à§à¦Ÿ à¦›à§à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦° à¦†à¦¬à§‡à¦¦à¦¨ à¦®à¦¡à§‡à¦²:
    Student à¦†à¦¬à§‡à¦¦à¦¨ âž” à¦¶à§à¦°à§‡à¦£à¦¿ à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œ Recommendation âž” à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦¸à¦¿à¦ªà¦¾à¦² Final Approval
    """
    class InChargeStatus(models.TextChoices):
        PENDING = 'PENDING', 'à¦ªà¦°à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦²à§‹à¦šà¦¨à¦¾à§Ÿ à¦†à¦›à§‡ (Pending)'
        RECOMMENDED = 'RECOMMENDED', 'à¦¸à§à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à¦¿à¦¶à¦•à§ƒà¦¤ (Recommended)'
        REJECTED = 'REJECTED', 'à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œ à¦•à¦°à§à¦¤à§ƒà¦• à¦¬à¦¾à¦¤à¦¿à¦² (Rejected)'

    class PrincipalStatus(models.TextChoices):
        PENDING = 'PENDING', 'à¦…à¦ªà§‡à¦•à§à¦·à¦®à¦¾à¦£ (Pending)'
        APPROVED = 'APPROVED', 'à¦šà§‚à§œà¦¾à¦¨à§à¦¤ à¦…à¦¨à§à¦®à§‹à¦¦à¦¿à¦¤ (Approved)'
        REJECTED = 'REJECTED', 'à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦¸à¦¿à¦ªà¦¾à¦² à¦•à¦°à§à¦¤à§ƒà¦• à¦¬à¦¾à¦¤à¦¿à¦² (Rejected)'

    student = models.ForeignKey(
        StudentProfile,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='leave_applications'
    )
    section = models.ForeignKey(
        AcademicSection,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='student_leaves'
    )
    start_date = models.DateField(db_index=True)
    end_date = models.DateField(db_index=True)
    reason = models.TextField()
    attachment = models.FileField(upload_to='leaves/%Y/%m/', null=True, blank=True)

    # à¦§à¦¾à¦ª à§§: à¦¶à§à¦°à§‡à¦£à¦¿ à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œà§‡à¦° à¦ªà¦°à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦²à§‹à¦šà¦¨à¦¾
    incharge_status = models.CharField(
        max_length=20,
        choices=InChargeStatus.choices,
        default=InChargeStatus.PENDING,
        db_index=True
    )
    incharge_remarks = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True)
    reviewed_by_incharge = models.ForeignKey(
        User,
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name='reviewed_student_leaves'
    )
    incharge_reviewed_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)

    # à¦§à¦¾à¦ª à§¨: à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦¸à¦¿à¦ªà¦¾à¦²à§‡à¦° à¦šà§‚à§œà¦¾à¦¨à§à¦¤ à¦¸à¦¿à¦¦à§à¦§à¦¾à¦¨à§à¦¤
    principal_status = models.CharField(
        max_length=20,
        choices=PrincipalStatus.choices,
        default=PrincipalStatus.PENDING,
        db_index=True
    )
    principal_remarks = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True)
    approved_by_principal = models.ForeignKey(
        User,
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name='approved_student_leaves'
    )
    principal_reviewed_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Student Leave Application'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Student Leave Applications'
        ordering = ['-created_at']

    def clean(self):
        if self.start_date and self.end_date and self.start_date > self.end_date:
            raise ValidationError("à¦›à§à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦¶à§à¦°à§à¦° à¦¤à¦¾à¦°à¦¿à¦– à¦¶à§‡à¦· à¦¤à¦¾à¦°à¦¿à¦–à§‡à¦° à¦šà§‡à§Ÿà§‡ à¦†à¦—à§‡à¦° à¦¹à¦¤à§‡ à¦¹à¦¬à§‡à¥¤")

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.student.student_unique_id} ({self.start_date} to {self.end_date}) - [InCharge: {self.incharge_status}, Principal: {self.principal_status}]"


class MarkerStock(BaseModel):
    """
    à¦•à§‡à¦¨à§à¦¦à§à¦°à§€à¦¯à¦¼ à¦®à¦¾à¦°à§à¦•à¦¾à¦°à§‡à¦° à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà¦• à¦°à§‡à¦œà¦¿à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦°à¥¤

class CollegeAsset(BaseModel):
    """
    à¦•à¦²à§‡à¦œà§‡à¦° à¦®à§‹à¦Ÿ à¦¬à§‡à¦žà§à¦š, à¦¹à§‹à¦¯à¦¼à¦¾à¦‡à¦Ÿà¦¬à§‹à¦°à§à¦¡, à¦¡à§‡à¦¸à§à¦•, à¦«à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¨ à¦“ à¦²à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¬ à¦¸à¦°à¦žà§à¦œà¦¾à¦®à§‡à¦° à¦‡à¦¨à¦­à§‡à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà¦°à¦¿ à¦Ÿà§à¦°à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦•à¦¾à¦°à¥¤
    """
    class Condition(models.TextChoices):
        GOOD = 'GOOD', 'à¦­à¦¾à¦²à§‹/à¦¸à¦šà¦² (Good/Usable)'
        DAMAGED = 'DAMAGED', 'à¦¨à¦·à§à¦Ÿ/à¦­à¦¾à¦™à¦¾ (Damaged)'
        UNDER_REPAIR = 'UNDER_REPAIR', 'à¦®à§‡à¦°à¦¾à¦®à¦¤à¦¾à¦§à§€à¦¨ (Under Repair)'
        DISPOSED = 'DISPOSED', 'à¦ªà¦°à¦¿à¦¤à§à¦¯à¦•à§à¦¤ (Disposed)'

    name = models.CharField(max_length=150, help_text="à¦¯à§‡à¦®à¦¨: à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§à¦¡à§‡à¦¨à§à¦Ÿ à¦¹à¦¾à¦‡-à¦¬à§‡à¦žà§à¦š, à¦¹à§‹à¦¯à¦¼à¦¾à¦‡à¦Ÿà¦¬à§‹à¦°à§à¦¡ à§¬xà§ª")
    asset_code = models.CharField(max_length=50, unique=True, db_index=True, help_text="à¦¯à§‡à¦®à¦¨: AST-BNCH-001")
    category = models.ForeignKey(AssetCategory, on_delete=models.PROTECT, related_name='assets')
    quantity = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=1)
    room_or_location = models.CharField(max_length=100, help_text="à¦¯à§‡à¦®à¦¨: Room-302, Chemistry Lab, Principal Room")
    condition = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=Condition.choices, default=Condition.GOOD, db_index=True)
    purchase_date = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True)
    unit_cost = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    remarks = models.TextField(blank=True)
    recorded_by = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'College Asset'
        verbose_name_plural = 'College Assets'
        ordering = ['room_or_location', 'name']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"[{self.asset_code}] {self.name} (Qty: {self.quantity}) - {self.room_or_location}"


class CertificateRequest(BaseModel):
    """
    à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦¦à§‡à¦° à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¤à§à¦¯à§Ÿà¦¨à¦ªà¦¤à§à¦°, à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¶à¦‚à¦¸à¦¾à¦ªà¦¤à§à¦° à¦“ à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦¸à¦¿ à¦†à¦¬à§‡à¦¦à¦¨ à¦‡à¦žà§à¦œà¦¿à¦¨à¥¤
    """
    class CertType(models.TextChoices):
        STUDENTSHIP = 'STUDENTSHIP', 'à¦…à¦§à§à¦¯à§Ÿà¦¨à¦°à¦¤ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¤à§à¦¯à§Ÿà¦¨à¦ªà¦¤à§à¦° (Studentship Certificate)'
        TESTIMONIAL = 'TESTIMONIAL', 'à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¶à¦‚à¦¸à¦¾à¦ªà¦¤à§à¦° (Testimonial)'
        TRANSFER = 'TC', 'à¦›à¦¾à§œà¦ªà¦¤à§à¦° (Transfer Certificate - TC)'

    class RequestStatus(models.TextChoices):
        PENDING = 'PENDING', 'à¦…à¦ªà§‡à¦•à§à¦·à¦®à¦¾à¦£ (Pending)'
        APPROVED = 'APPROVED', 'à¦…à¦¨à§à¦®à§‹à¦¦à¦¿à¦¤ (Approved)'
        REJECTED = 'REJECTED', 'à¦¬à¦¾à¦¤à¦¿à¦² (Rejected)'
        ISSUED = 'ISSUED', 'à¦¬à¦¿à¦¤à¦°à¦£à¦•à§ƒà¦¤ (Issued)'

    student = models.ForeignKey(StudentProfile, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='certificate_requests')
    certificate_type = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=CertType.choices, default=CertType.STUDENTSHIP)
    fee_amount = models.DecimalField(max_digits=8, decimal_places=2, default=100.00, help_text="à¦†à¦¬à§‡à¦¦à¦¨ à¦«à¦¿ (BDT)")
    is_fee_paid = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    
    reason = models.TextField(help_text="à¦†à¦¬à§‡à¦¦à¦¨à§‡à¦° à¦‰à¦¦à§à¦¦à§‡à¦¶à§à¦¯ (à¦¯à§‡à¦®à¦¨: à¦ªà¦¾à¦¸à¦ªà§‹à¦°à§à¦Ÿ à¦¬à¦¾ à¦¬à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦‚à¦• à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦•à¦¾à¦‰à¦¨à§à¦Ÿ à¦–à§‹à¦²à¦¾)")
    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=RequestStatus.choices, default=RequestStatus.PENDING, db_index=True)
    
    certificate_number = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True, unique=True, null=True, db_index=True)
    approved_by = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, related_name='approved_certificates')
    approved_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    rejection_reason = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Certificate Request'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Certificate Requests'
        ordering = ['-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.student.student_unique_id} - {self.get_certificate_type_display()} ({self.status})"

    """
    à¦¶à§à¦°à§‡à¦£à¦¿ à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œà§‡à¦° à¦…à¦­à¦¿à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à¦• à¦•à¦²-à¦¡à¦¾à§Ÿà§‡à¦°à¦¿:

class TeacherLeave(BaseModel):
    """
    à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦•à¦¦à§‡à¦° à¦›à§à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦° à¦†à¦¬à§‡à¦¦à¦¨ à¦“ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦•à§à¦¸à¦¿ à¦•à§à¦²à¦¾à¦¸ à¦‡à¦žà§à¦œà¦¿à¦¨:
    à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦• à¦†à¦¬à§‡à¦¦à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦¬à§‡à¦¨ -> à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦¸à¦¿à¦ªà¦¾à¦² à¦…à¦¨à§à¦®à§‹à¦¦à¦¨ à¦¦à¦¿à¦²à§‡ à¦®à¦¨à§‹à¦¨à§€à¦¤ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦•à§à¦¸à¦¿ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦•à§‡à¦° à¦•à¦¾à¦›à§‡ à¦¨à§‹à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦«à¦¿à¦•à§‡à¦¶à¦¨ à¦¯à¦¾à¦¬à§‡à¥¤
    """
    class LeaveType(models.TextChoices):
        CASUAL = 'CASUAL', 'à¦¨à§ˆà¦®à¦¿à¦¤à§à¦¤à¦¿à¦• à¦›à§à¦Ÿà¦¿ (Casual Leave - CL)'
        MEDICAL = 'MEDICAL', 'à¦šà¦¿à¦•à¦¿à§Žà¦¸à¦¾ à¦›à§à¦Ÿà¦¿ (Medical Leave - ML)'
        DUTY = 'DUTY', 'à¦¦à¦¾à¦ªà§à¦¤à¦°à¦¿à¦•/à¦ªà¦°à§€à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦° à¦¦à¦¾à§Ÿà¦¿à¦¤à§à¦¬ (Duty Leave)'
        MATERNITY = 'MATERNITY', 'à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§ƒà¦¤à§à¦¬à¦•à¦¾à¦²à§€à¦¨ à¦›à§à¦Ÿà¦¿ (Maternity Leave)'
        EMERGENCY = 'EMERGENCY', 'à¦œà¦°à§à¦°à¦¿ à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à¦¿à¦¬à¦¾à¦°à¦¿à¦• à¦›à§à¦Ÿà¦¿ (Emergency Leave)'

    class LeaveStatus(models.TextChoices):
        PENDING = 'PENDING', 'à¦ªà¦°à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦²à§‹à¦šà¦¨à¦¾à§Ÿ à¦†à¦›à§‡ (Pending)'
        APPROVED = 'APPROVED', 'à¦…à¦¨à§à¦®à§‹à¦¦à¦¿à¦¤ (Approved)'
        REJECTED = 'REJECTED', 'à¦¬à¦¾à¦¤à¦¿à¦² (Rejected)'

    teacher = models.ForeignKey(
        User,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='leaves_taken',
        limit_choices_to={'role': User.Role.TEACHER}
    )
    leave_type = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=LeaveType.choices, default=LeaveType.CASUAL)
    start_date = models.DateField(db_index=True)
    end_date = models.DateField(db_index=True)
    total_days = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=1)
    reason = models.TextField(help_text="à¦›à§à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦° à¦¸à§à¦¨à¦¿à¦°à§à¦¦à¦¿à¦·à§à¦Ÿ à¦•à¦¾à¦°à¦£")

    # à¦›à§à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦° à¦¸à¦®à§Ÿ à¦•à§à¦²à¦¾à¦¸à§‡à¦° à¦¦à¦¾à§Ÿà¦¿à¦¤à§à¦¬à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¾à¦ªà§à¦¤ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦•à§à¦¸à¦¿ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦•
    proxy_teacher = models.ForeignKey(
        User,
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name='proxy_assigned_classes',
        limit_choices_to={'role': User.Role.TEACHER},
        help_text="à¦›à§à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦•à¦¾à¦²à§€à¦¨ à¦¸à¦®à§Ÿà§‡ à¦¯à¦¿à¦¨à¦¿ à¦•à§à¦²à¦¾à¦¸ à¦ªà¦°à¦¿à¦šà¦¾à¦²à¦¨à¦¾ à¦•à¦°à¦¬à§‡à¦¨"
    )

    status = models.CharField(max_length=15, choices=LeaveStatus.choices, default=LeaveStatus.PENDING, db_index=True)
    reviewed_by = models.ForeignKey(
        User,
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name='reviewed_teacher_leaves'
    )
    reviewed_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    principal_remarks = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Teacher Leave & Proxy'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Teacher Leaves & Proxies'
        ordering = ['-start_date']

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        if self.start_date and self.end_date:
            self.total_days = max(1, (self.end_date - self.start_date).days + 1)
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.teacher.get_full_name()} ({self.get_leave_type_display()}): {self.start_date} to {self.end_date} - {self.status}"

    """
    à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦°à§à¦—à§‡à¦Ÿà§‡à¦¡ à¦¸à§à¦®à¦¾à¦°à§à¦Ÿ à¦¬à¦¾à¦²à§à¦• SMS à¦ªà§à¦°à§‡à¦°à¦£à§‡à¦° à¦²à¦— à¦“ à¦¹à¦¿à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§à¦°à¦¿à¥¤
    """
    class TargetGroup(models.TextChoices):
        ABSENT_TODAY = 'ABSENT_TODAY', 'à¦†à¦œà¦•à§‡à¦° à¦…à¦¨à§à¦ªà¦¸à§à¦¥à¦¿à¦¤ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦¦à§‡à¦° à¦…à¦­à¦¿à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à¦•'
        FEE_DEFAULTERS = 'FEE_DEFAULTERS', 'à¦¬à¦•à§‡à§Ÿà¦¾ à¦¥à¦¾à¦•à¦¾ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦¦à§‡à¦° à¦…à¦­à¦¿à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à¦•'
        ALL_TEACHERS = 'ALL_TEACHERS', 'à¦¸à¦•à¦² à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦•'
        SECTION_STUDENTS = 'SECTION_STUDENTS', 'à¦¨à¦¿à¦°à§à¦¦à¦¿à¦·à§à¦Ÿ à¦¶à¦¾à¦–à¦¾à¦° à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€'

    sender = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True)
    target_group = models.CharField(max_length=30, choices=TargetGroup.choices, db_index=True)
    message_body = models.TextField(help_text="à¦ªà§à¦°à§‡à¦°à¦¿à¦¤ à¦¬à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¤à¦¾à¦° à¦¬à¦¿à¦·à§Ÿà¦¬à¦¸à§à¦¤à§")

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'SMS Broadcast Log'
        verbose_name_plural = 'SMS Broadcast Logs'
        ordering = ['-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"[{self.target_group}] {self.recipients_count} SMS Sent at {self.created_at.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M')}"

    """
    à¦•à¦²à§‡à¦œà§‡à¦° à¦¸à¦¹-à¦ªà¦¾à¦ à§à¦¯à¦•à§à¦°à¦®à¦¿à¦• à¦•à§à¦²à¦¾à¦¬ (à¦¸à¦¾à§Ÿà§‡à¦¨à§à¦¸ à¦•à§à¦²à¦¾à¦¬, à¦¡à¦¿à¦¬à§‡à¦Ÿ à¦•à§à¦²à¦¾à¦¬ à¦‡à¦¤à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¦à¦¿)à¥¤

    """
    name = models.CharField(max_length=150, unique=True, help_text="à¦¯à§‡à¦®à¦¨: à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà¦ªà¦²à¦¾à¦‡à¦Ÿ à¦¸à¦¾à¦¯à¦¼à§‡à¦¨à§à¦¸ à¦•à§à¦²à¦¾à¦¬, à¦¡à¦¿à¦¬à§‡à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦‚ à¦¸à§‹à¦¸à¦¾à¦‡à¦Ÿà¦¿")
    description = models.TextField(blank=True)
    
    # à¦¦à¦¾à§Ÿà¦¿à¦¤à§à¦¬à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¾à¦ªà§à¦¤ à¦•à§à¦²à¦¾à¦¬ à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦•

        User,

        blank=True,

        limit_choices_to={'role': User.Role.TEACHER},
        help_text="à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦¸à¦¿à¦ªà¦¾à¦² à¦•à¦°à§à¦¤à§ƒà¦• à¦®à¦¨à§‹à¦¨à§€à¦¤ à¦•à§à¦²à¦¾à¦¬ à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦•"

        return f"{self.name} (Moderator: {moderator})"

    """
    à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦¦à§‡à¦° à¦•à§à¦²à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ à¦¸à¦¦à¦¸à§à¦¯ à¦¹à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾à¦° à¦†à¦¬à§‡à¦¦à¦¨ à¦®à¦¡à§‡à¦²à¥¤

        PENDING = 'PENDING', 'à¦…à¦ªà§‡à¦•à§à¦·à¦®à¦¾à¦£ (Pending)'
        APPROVED = 'APPROVED', 'à¦…à¦¨à§à¦®à§‹à¦¦à¦¿à¦¤ à¦¸à¦¦à¦¸à§à¦¯ (Approved)'
        REJECTED = 'REJECTED', 'à¦¬à¦¾à¦¤à¦¿à¦² (Rejected)'

    student = models.ForeignKey(StudentProfile, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='club_memberships')
    reason = models.TextField(blank=True, help_text="à¦•à§à¦²à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ à¦¯à§‹à¦—à¦¦à¦¾à¦¨à§‡à¦° à¦†à¦—à§à¦°à¦¹ à¦¬à¦¾ à¦¦à¦•à§à¦·à¦¤à¦¾")

    reviewed_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)

        verbose_name = 'Club Membership'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Club Memberships'
        unique_together = ('club', 'student')
        ordering = ['-created_at']
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=['club', 'status']),
        ]

        return f"{self.student.roll_number} - {self.club.name} ({self.status})"

    """
    à¦‡à¦­à§‡à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà§‡ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦¦à§‡à¦° à¦¨à¦¾à¦® à¦°à§‡à¦œà¦¿à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§à¦°à§‡à¦¶à¦¨à¥¤

    student = models.ForeignKey(StudentProfile, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='event_registrations')

    attended = models.BooleanField(default=False)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Event Registration'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Event Registrations'
        unique_together = ('event', 'student')
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=['event', 'student']),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.student.student_unique_id} - {self.event.title}"

    """
    à¦¹à§‹à§Ÿà¦¾à¦Ÿà¦¸à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦ª à¦®à§‡à¦¸à§‡à¦œà§‡à¦° à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¿-à¦¡à¦¿à¦«à¦¾à¦‡à¦¨à§à¦¡ à¦Ÿà§‡à¦®à¦ªà§à¦²à§‡à¦Ÿ:
    à¦‰à¦ªà¦¸à§à¦¥à¦¿à¦¤à¦¿, à¦¬à§‡à¦¤à¦¨ à¦¬à¦•à§‡à§Ÿà¦¾, à¦°à§à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦¨ à¦¬à¦¾ à¦œà¦°à§à¦°à¦¿ à¦¨à§‹à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦¶à§‡à¦° à¦°à§‡à¦¡à¦¿à¦®à§‡à¦¡ à¦«à¦°à¦®à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦Ÿà¥¤

        TRANSPORT_ALERT = 'TRANSPORT', 'à¦ªà¦°à¦¿à¦¬à¦¹à¦¨ à¦“ à¦—à¦¾à§œà¦¿ à¦¬à¦¿à¦•à¦² à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦²à¦¾à¦°à§à¦Ÿ (Transport Breakdown)'
        EXAM_NOTICE = 'EXAM', 'à¦ªà¦°à§€à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾ à¦¸à¦‚à¦•à§à¦°à¦¾à¦¨à§à¦¤ à¦¨à§‹à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦¶ (Exam Notice)'

class WhatsAppMessageLog(BaseModel):
    """
    à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦• à¦“ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦¸à¦¿à¦ªà¦¾à¦² à¦•à¦°à§à¦¤à§ƒà¦• à¦ªà§à¦°à§‡à¦°à¦¿à¦¤ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¤à¦¿à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦¹à§‹à§Ÿà¦¾à¦Ÿà¦¸à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦ª à¦¬à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¤à¦¾à¦° à¦…à¦¡à¦¿à¦Ÿ à¦¹à¦¿à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§à¦°à¦¿à¥¤
    """
    class SendStatus(models.TextChoices):
        SENT = 'SENT', 'à¦¸à¦«à¦²à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ à¦ªà§à¦°à§‡à¦°à¦¿à¦¤ (Sent)'
        FAILED = 'FAILED', 'à¦¬à§à¦¯à¦°à§à¦¥ (Failed)'
        QUEUED = 'QUEUED', 'à¦…à¦ªà§‡à¦•à§à¦·à¦®à¦¾à¦£ (Queued)'

    sender = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True)
    student = models.ForeignKey(StudentProfile, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, related_name='whatsapp_messages')
    recipient_phone = models.CharField(max_length=20, db_index=True)
    recipient_name = models.CharField(max_length=150)
    
    template = models.ForeignKey(WhatsAppTemplate, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True)
    message_content = models.TextField()
    status = models.CharField(max_length=15, choices=SendStatus.choices, default=SendStatus.SENT, db_index=True)
    error_message = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'WhatsApp Message Log'
        verbose_name_plural = 'WhatsApp Message Logs'
        ordering = ['-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"To: {self.recipient_name} ({self.recipient_phone}) - {self.status}"


class InAppNotification(BaseModel):
    """
    à¦‡à¦¨-à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦ª à¦¨à§‹à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦«à¦¿à¦•à§‡à¦¶à¦¨ à¦®à¦¡à§‡à¦² (à¦‡à¦‰à¦œà¦¾à¦°à§‡à¦° à¦¡à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¶à¦¬à§‹à¦°à§à¦¡à§‡à¦° à¦¬à§‡à¦² à¦†à¦‡à¦•à¦¨à§‡ à¦°à¦¿à§Ÿà§‡à¦²-à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦‡à¦®à§‡ à¦¶à§‹ à¦•à¦°à¦¬à§‡)à¥¤
    """
    class NotificationType(models.TextChoices):
        LEAVE = 'LEAVE', 'à¦›à§à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦¸à¦‚à¦•à§à¦°à¦¾à¦¨à§à¦¤ (Leave Alert)'
        ATTENDANCE = 'ATTENDANCE', 'à¦‰à¦ªà¦¸à§à¦¥à¦¿à¦¤à¦¿ à¦¸à¦‚à¦•à§à¦°à¦¾à¦¨à§à¦¤ (Attendance)'
        EXAM = 'EXAM', 'à¦ªà¦°à§€à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾ à¦“ à¦°à§‡à¦œà¦¾à¦²à§à¦Ÿ (Exam & Result)'
        FEE = 'FEE', 'à¦¬à§‡à¦¤à¦¨ à¦“ à¦œà¦°à¦¿à¦®à¦¾à¦¨à¦¾ (Fees & Dues)'
        ROUTINE = 'ROUTINE', 'à¦•à§à¦²à¦¾à¦¸ à¦°à¦¿à¦®à¦¾à¦‡à¦¨à§à¦¡à¦¾à¦° (Class Reminder)'
        TRANSPORT = 'TRANSPORT', 'à¦ªà¦°à¦¿à¦¬à¦¹à¦¨ à¦“ à¦œà¦°à§à¦°à¦¿ à¦¨à§‹à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦¶ (Transport Emergency)'
        GENERAL = 'GENERAL', 'à¦¸à¦¾à¦§à¦¾à¦°à¦£ à¦¨à§‹à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦¶ (General Notice)'

    recipient = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='notifications')
    title = models.CharField(max_length=200)
    body = models.TextField()
    notification_type = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=NotificationType.choices, default=NotificationType.GENERAL, db_index=True)
    action_url = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True, help_text="à¦•à§à¦²à¦¿à¦• à¦•à¦°à¦²à§‡ à¦«à§à¦°à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà¦à¦¨à§à¦¡à§‡à¦° à¦•à§‹à¦¨ à¦ªà§‡à¦œà§‡ à¦¨à¦¿à§Ÿà§‡ à¦¯à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ (à¦¯à§‡à¦®à¦¨: /leaves/ à¦¬à¦¾ /admit-card/)")
    
    is_read = models.BooleanField(default=False, db_index=True)
    read_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'In-App Notification'
        verbose_name_plural = 'In-App Notifications'
        ordering = ['-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"[{self.notification_type}] To: {self.recipient.username} - {self.title}"


class DevicePushSubscription(BaseModel):
    """
    à¦‡à¦‰à¦œà¦¾à¦°à§‡à¦° à¦¡à¦¿à¦­à¦¾à¦‡à¦¸à§‡à¦° à¦ªà§à¦¶ à¦Ÿà§‹à¦•à§‡à¦¨ à¦“ à¦¬à§à¦°à¦¾à¦‰à¦œà¦¾à¦° à¦¸à¦¾à¦¬à¦¸à§à¦•à§à¦°à¦¿à¦ªà¦¶à¦¨ à¦•à¦¿ (Offline Web Push Delivery à¦à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯)à¥¤
    à¦‡à¦‰à¦œà¦¾à¦° à¦…à¦«à¦²à¦¾à¦‡à¦¨à§‡ à¦¥à¦¾à¦•à¦²à§‡à¦“ à¦¬à§à¦°à¦¾à¦‰à¦œà¦¾à¦°/à¦«à§‹à¦¨à§‡à¦° à¦“à¦à¦¸à§‡ à¦ªà§à¦¶ à¦¨à§‹à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦«à¦¿à¦•à§‡à¦¶à¦¨ à¦ªà¦¾à¦ à¦¾à¦¤à§‡ à¦à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦¬à§à¦¯à¦¬à¦¹à§ƒà¦¤ à¦¹à§Ÿà¥¤
    """
    class DeviceType(models.TextChoices):
        WEB = 'WEB', 'à¦“à¦¯à¦¼à§‡à¦¬ à¦¬à§à¦°à¦¾à¦‰à¦œà¦¾à¦° (Chrome/Firefox/Edge)'
        ANDROID = 'ANDROID', 'à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¨à§à¦¡à§à¦°à¦¯à¦¼à§‡à¦¡ à¦«à§‹à¦¨ (Mobile App)'
        IOS = 'IOS', 'à¦†à¦‡à¦“à¦à¦¸ (iOS)'

    user = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='push_subscriptions')
    device_type = models.CharField(max_length=15, choices=DeviceType.choices, default=DeviceType.WEB)
    
    # à¦¬à§à¦°à¦¾à¦‰à¦œà¦¾à¦° Web Push API à¦¬à¦¾ Firebase (FCM) à¦Ÿà§‹à¦•à§‡à¦¨ à¦¡à§‡à¦Ÿà¦¾
    endpoint = models.TextField(help_text="à¦¬à§à¦°à¦¾à¦‰à¦œà¦¾à¦°à§‡à¦° à¦ªà§à¦¶ à¦¸à¦¾à¦°à§à¦­à¦¿à¦¸ à¦à¦¨à§à¦¡à¦ªà¦¯à¦¼à§‡à¦¨à§à¦Ÿ URL")
    p256dh_key = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True, help_text="Web Push à¦à¦¨à¦•à§à¦°à¦¿à¦ªà¦¶à¦¨ à¦ªà¦¾à¦¬à¦²à¦¿à¦• à¦•à¦¿")
    auth_key = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True, help_text="Web Push à¦…à¦¥à§‡à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦•à§‡à¦¶à¦¨ à¦¸à¦¿à¦•à§à¦°à§‡à¦Ÿ")
    fcm_token = models.TextField(blank=True, help_text="Firebase Cloud Messaging à¦Ÿà§‹à¦•à§‡à¦¨ (à¦¯à¦¦à¦¿ à¦®à§‹à¦¬à¦¾à¦‡à¦² à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦ªà§‡ à¦¬à§à¦¯à¦¬à¦¹à§ƒà¦¤ à¦¹à§Ÿ)")
    
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Device Push Subscription'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Device Push Subscriptions'
        unique_together = ('user', 'endpoint')

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.user.username} ({self.device_type}) - Active: {self.is_active}"

class TeacherAttendanceSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    teacher_name = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    employee_id = serializers.CharField(source='teacher.teacher_profile.employee_id', read_only=True, default=None)
    department = serializers.CharField(source='teacher.teacher_profile.department', read_only=True, default=None)
    approval_status_display = serializers.CharField(source='get_approval_status_display', read_only=True)
    approved_by_name = serializers.SerializerMethodField()

    class Meta:
        model = TeacherAttendance
        fields = [
            'id',
            'teacher',
            'teacher_name',
            'employee_id',
            'department',
            'date',
            'check_in_time',
            'check_out_time',
            'is_late',
            'remarks',
            'approval_status',
            'approval_status_display',
            'approved_by',
            'approved_by_name',
            'approved_at',
            'created_at',
        ]
        read_only_fields = [
            'id', 'teacher', 'date', 'check_in_time', 'check_out_time',
            'is_late', 'approval_status', 'approved_by', 'approved_at', 'created_at'
        ]

    def get_teacher_name(self, obj) -> str:
        return obj.teacher.get_full_name() or obj.teacher.username

    def get_approved_by_name(self, obj) -> str | None:
        if obj.approved_by:
            return obj.approved_by.get_full_name() or obj.approved_by.username
        return None


class NoticeSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    created_by_name = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    created_by_role = serializers.CharField(source='created_by.get_role_display', read_only=True)
    audience_display = serializers.CharField(source='get_audience_display', read_only=True)
    target_section_display = serializers.CharField(source='target_section.__str__', read_only=True, default=None)

    class Meta:
        model = Notice
        fields = [
            'id',
            'title',
            'content',
            'attachment',
            'audience',
            'audience_display',
            'target_section',
            'target_section_display',
            'is_pinned',
            'is_active',
            'created_by',
            'created_by_name',
            'created_by_role',
            'created_at',
        ]
        read_only_fields = ['id', 'created_by', 'created_at']

    def get_created_by_name(self, obj) -> str:
        return obj.created_by.get_full_name() or obj.created_by.username

    def validate(self, attrs):
        audience = attrs.get('audience', getattr(self.instance, 'audience', Notice.AudienceType.ALL))
        target_section = attrs.get('target_section', getattr(self.instance, 'target_section', None))

        if audience == Notice.AudienceType.SECTION and not target_section:
            raise serializers.ValidationError({
                "target_section": "à¦¨à¦¿à¦°à§à¦¦à¦¿à¦·à§à¦Ÿ à¦¶à§à¦°à§‡à¦£à¦¿ à¦¬à¦¾ à¦¶à¦¾à¦–à¦¾à¦° à¦¨à§‹à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦¶ à¦¦à§‡à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ 'target_section' à¦†à¦‡à¦¡à¦¿ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¦à¦¾à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦†à¦¬à¦¶à§à¦¯à¦•à¥¤"
            })
        if audience != Notice.AudienceType.SECTION:
            attrs['target_section'] = None

        return attrs

class StudentComplaintSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    student_details = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    category_display = serializers.CharField(source='get_category_display', read_only=True)
    status_display = serializers.CharField(source='get_status_display', read_only=True)

    class Meta:
        model = StudentComplaint
        fields = [
            'id', 'category', 'category_display', 'title', 'description', 
            'attachment', 'status', 'status_display', 'principal_remarks', 
            'action_taken_at', 'student_details', 'created_at'
        ]
        read_only_fields = ['id', 'status', 'principal_remarks', 'action_taken_at', 'created_at']

    def get_student_details(self, obj) -> dict | None:
        request = self.context.get('request')
        if request and (request.user.role == User.Role.PRINCIPAL or request.user.is_superuser):
            return {
                "student_name": obj.student.user.get_full_name(),
                "student_unique_id": obj.student.student_unique_id,
                "roll_number": obj.student.roll_number,
                "section": str(obj.student.section),
                "student_phone": obj.student.user.phone,
                "guardian_phone": obj.student.guardian_phone,
            }
        return None

class ClubSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    moderator_name = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    total_members = serializers.SerializerMethodField()

    class Meta:
        model = Club
        fields = ['id', 'name', 'description', 'moderator_teacher', 'moderator_name', 'total_members', 'is_active', 'created_at']

    def get_moderator_name(self, obj) -> str:
        if obj.moderator_teacher:
            return obj.moderator_teacher.get_full_name() or obj.moderator_teacher.username
        return "à¦®à¦¨à§‹à¦¨à§€à¦¤ à¦¹à§Ÿà¦¨à¦¿"

    def get_total_members(self, obj) -> int:
        if hasattr(obj, 'approved_member_count'):
            return obj.approved_member_count
        return obj.memberships.filter(
            status=ClubMembership.MembershipStatus.APPROVED
        ).count()

        fields = ['id', 'title', 'template_type', 'template_type_display', 'body_template', 'is_active', 'created_at']


class WhatsAppMessageLogSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    sender_name = serializers.CharField(source='sender.get_full_name', read_only=True)
    status_display = serializers.CharField(source='get_status_display', read_only=True)

    class Meta:
        model = WhatsAppMessageLog
        fields = [
            'id', 'sender', 'sender_name', 'student', 'recipient_name', 
            'recipient_phone', 'message_content', 'status', 'status_display', 
            'created_at'
        ]
        read_only_fields = '__all__'


class InAppNotificationSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    type_display = serializers.CharField(source='get_notification_type_display', read_only=True)

    class Meta:
        model = InAppNotification
        fields = [
            'id', 'recipient', 'title', 'body', 'notification_type', 
            'type_display', 'action_url', 'is_read', 'read_at', 'created_at'
        ]
        read_only_fields = ['id', 'recipient', 'title', 'body', 'notification_type', 'action_url', 'created_at']


class DevicePushSubscriptionSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    class Meta:
        model = DevicePushSubscription
        fields = ['id', 'device_type', 'endpoint', 'p256dh_key', 'auth_key', 'fcm_token']

import re
from django.utils import timezone
from apps.operations.models import WhatsAppMessageLog, WhatsAppTemplate
import json
import logging
from apps.operations.models import DevicePushSubscription, InAppNotification

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

class WhatsAppNotificationService:
    """
    à¦¹à§‹à§Ÿà¦¾à¦Ÿà¦¸à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦ª à¦®à§‡à¦¸à§‡à¦œ à¦«à¦°à¦®à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦‚, à¦¡à¦¾à§Ÿà¦¨à¦¾à¦®à¦¿à¦• à¦Ÿà§‡à¦®à¦ªà§à¦²à§‡à¦Ÿ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¸à§‡à¦¸à¦¿à¦‚ à¦“ à¦¸à§‡à¦¨à§à¦¡à¦¾à¦° à¦¸à¦¾à¦°à§à¦­à¦¿à¦¸à¥¤
    """
    COLLEGE_NAME = "à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦°à¦²à¦¾à¦‡à¦Ÿ à¦•à¦²à§‡à¦œ, à¦¸à¦¿à¦²à§‡à¦Ÿ"

    @staticmethod
    def format_phone_number(phone: str) -> str:
        """
        à¦¬à¦¾à¦‚à¦²à¦¾à¦¦à§‡à¦¶à¦¿ à¦«à§‹à¦¨ à¦¨à¦®à§à¦¬à¦°à¦•à§‡ à¦†à¦¨à§à¦¤à¦°à§à¦œà¦¾à¦¤à¦¿à¦• à¦¹à§‹à§Ÿà¦¾à¦Ÿà¦¸à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦ª à¦«à¦°à¦®à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦Ÿà§‡ à¦°à§‚à¦ªà¦¾à¦¨à§à¦¤à¦° à¦•à¦°à§‡à¥¤
        à¦¯à§‡à¦®à¦¨: 01711223344 -> +8801711223344
        """
        cleaned = re.sub(r'\D', '', str(phone).strip())
        if cleaned.startswith('880'):
            return f"+{cleaned}"
        if cleaned.startswith('0'):
            return f"+88{cleaned}"
        return f"+880{cleaned}"

    @classmethod
    def render_template(cls, template_body: str, context: dict) -> str:
        """
        à¦Ÿà§‡à¦®à¦ªà§à¦²à§‡à¦Ÿà§‡à¦° à¦ªà§à¦²à§‡à¦¸à¦¹à§‹à¦²à§à¦¡à¦¾à¦°à¦—à§à¦²à§‹ à¦†à¦¸à¦² à¦¤à¦¥à§à¦¯ à¦¦à¦¿à§Ÿà§‡ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¤à¦¿à¦¸à§à¦¥à¦¾à¦ªà¦¨ à¦•à¦°à§‡à¥¤
        """
        context.setdefault('college_name', cls.COLLEGE_NAME)
        context.setdefault('date', timezone.localdate().strftime('%d-%m-%Y'))
        
        rendered = template_body
        for key, value in context.items():
            rendered = rendered.replace(f"{{{key}}}", str(value))
        return rendered

    @classmethod
    def send_whatsapp(cls, sender_user, recipient_phone, recipient_name, message_text, student=None, template=None) -> WhatsAppMessageLog:
        """
        à¦®à§‡à¦¸à§‡à¦œ à¦²à¦— à¦¸à¦‚à¦°à¦•à§à¦·à¦£ à¦“ à¦¡à¦¿à¦¸à¦ªà§à¦¯à¦¾à¦š à¦•à¦°à§‡ (à¦°à¦¿à§Ÿà§‡à¦² API à¦¬à¦¾ à¦ªà§à¦²à¦¾à¦—à¦¿à¦¨ à¦¯à§à¦•à§à¦¤ à¦•à¦°à¦¾à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ à¦‰à¦¨à§à¦®à§à¦•à§à¦¤)à¥¤
        """
        formatted_phone = cls.format_phone_number(recipient_phone)

        # à¦à¦–à¦¾à¦¨à§‡ à¦¯à§‡à¦•à§‹à¦¨à§‹ à¦¥à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à§à¦Ÿà¦¿ API (à¦¯à§‡à¦®à¦¨: Twilio / UltraMsg / Meta Cloud API) à¦•à¦² à¦¹à¦¬à§‡
        # à¦¡à§‡à¦­à§‡à¦²à¦ªà¦®à§‡à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà§‡ à¦¶à¦¤à¦­à¦¾à¦— à¦°à¦¿à§Ÿà§‡à¦²-à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾à¦°à§à¦²à§à¦¡ à¦…à¦¡à¦¿à¦Ÿ à¦¨à¦¿à¦¶à§à¦šà¦¿à¦¤ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à¦²à§‹
        log = WhatsAppMessageLog.objects.create(
            sender=sender_user,
            student=student,
            recipient_phone=formatted_phone,
            recipient_name=recipient_name,
            template=template,
            message_content=message_text,
            status=WhatsAppMessageLog.SendStatus.SENT
        )
        return log

class NotificationDispatcher:
    """
    à¦°à¦¿à¦¯à¦¼à§‡à¦²-à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦‡à¦® à¦‡à¦¨-à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦ª à¦“ à¦…à¦«à¦²à¦¾à¦‡à¦¨ à¦ªà§à¦¶ à¦¨à§‹à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦«à¦¿à¦•à§‡à¦¶à¦¨ à¦¡à¦¿à¦¸à¦ªà§à¦¯à¦¾à¦šà¦¾à¦° à¦¸à¦¾à¦°à§à¦­à¦¿à¦¸à¥¤
    """
    @classmethod
    def send_to_user(cls, user, title: str, body: str, notification_type: str = 'GENERAL', action_url: str = '') -> InAppNotification:
        """
        à§§. à¦‡à¦¨-à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦ª à¦¨à§‹à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦«à¦¿à¦•à§‡à¦¶à¦¨ à¦¡à¦¾à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦¬à§‡à¦¸à§‡ à¦¤à§ˆà¦°à¦¿ à¦•à¦°à§‡ (à¦²à¦—à¦‡à¦¨ à¦¥à¦¾à¦•à¦¾ à¦…à¦¬à¦¸à§à¦¥à¦¾à§Ÿ à¦¦à§‡à¦–à¦¾à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯)à¥¤
        à§¨. à¦‡à¦‰à¦œà¦¾à¦°à§‡à¦° à¦°à§‡à¦œà¦¿à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦°à§à¦¡ à¦¡à¦¿à¦­à¦¾à¦‡à¦¸à§‡ à¦…à¦«à¦²à¦¾à¦‡à¦¨ à¦ªà§à¦¶ à¦¨à§‹à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦«à¦¿à¦•à§‡à¦¶à¦¨ à¦ªà¦¾à¦ à¦¾à§Ÿ (à¦¬à§à¦°à¦¾à¦‰à¦œà¦¾à¦°/à¦«à§‹à¦¨ à¦¬à¦¨à§à¦§ à¦¥à¦¾à¦•à¦²à§‡à¦“)à¥¤
        """
        # à§§. à¦‡à¦¨-à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦ª à¦¨à§‹à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦«à¦¿à¦•à§‡à¦¶à¦¨ à¦¸à§‡à¦­
        notification = InAppNotification.objects.create(
            recipient=user,
            title=title,
            body=body,
            notification_type=notification_type,
            action_url=action_url
        )

        # à§¨. à¦…à¦«à¦²à¦¾à¦‡à¦¨ à¦ªà§à¦¶ à¦¡à§‡à¦²à¦¿à¦­à¦¾à¦°à¦¿ (à¦¸à¦•à¦² à¦¸à¦šà¦² à¦¡à¦¿à¦­à¦¾à¦‡à¦¸ à¦¬à§à¦°à¦¾à¦‰à¦œà¦¾à¦°à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦ à¦¾à¦¨à§‹)
        active_subscriptions = DevicePushSubscription.objects.filter(user=user, is_active=True)
        
        push_payload = {
            "title": title,
            "body": body,
            "icon": "/logo.png",
            "badge": "/badge.png",
            "url": action_url,
            "notification_id": str(notification.id),
        }

        for sub in active_subscriptions:
            try:
                # à¦à¦–à¦¾à¦¨à§‡ pywebpush à¦¬à¦¾ FCM à¦•à¦² à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡ à¦…à¦«à¦²à¦¾à¦‡à¦¨ à¦ªà§à¦¶ à¦šà¦²à§‡ à¦¯à¦¾à§Ÿ
                # (à¦‰à¦¨à§à¦¨à§Ÿà¦¨ à¦“ à¦Ÿà§‡à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§‡à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ à¦¸à¦«à¦² à¦²à¦— à¦¨à¦¿à¦¶à§à¦šà¦¿à¦¤ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à¦²à§‹)
                logger.info(f"Offline Web Push dispatched to {user.username} ({sub.device_type}): {title}")
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Failed to send push to device {sub.id}: {str(e)}")

        return notification

    @classmethod
    def send_bulk_notification(cls, users_qs, title: str, body: str, notification_type: str = 'GENERAL', action_url: str = ''):
        """
        à¦à¦•à¦¾à¦§à¦¿à¦• à¦‡à¦‰à¦œà¦¾à¦°à¦•à§‡ (à¦¯à§‡à¦®à¦¨ à¦ªà§à¦°à§‹ à¦•à§à¦²à¦¾à¦¸à§‡à¦° à¦¸à¦¬ à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§à¦¡à§‡à¦¨à§à¦Ÿ à¦¬à¦¾ à¦¸à¦•à¦² à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦•) à¦à¦•à¦¸à¦¾à¦¥à§‡ à¦¨à§‹à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦«à¦¿à¦•à§‡à¦¶à¦¨ à¦ªà¦¾à¦ à¦¾à¦¨à§‹à¥¤
        """
        created_notifications = []
        for user in users_qs:
            notif = cls.send_to_user(
                user=user,
                title=title,
                body=body,
                notification_type=notification_type,
                action_url=action_url
            )
            created_notifications.append(notif)
        return created_notifications

class PrincipalManagedContentViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    permission_classes = [AllowAny]

    def get_permissions(self):
        if self.request.method in ('GET', 'HEAD', 'OPTIONS'):
            return [AllowAny()]
        return [IsPrincipal()]


class HomepageHeroViewSet(PrincipalManagedContentViewSet):
    queryset = HomepageHero.objects.filter(is_active=True)
    serializer_class = HomepageHeroSerializer


class HomepageServiceViewSet(PrincipalManagedContentViewSet):
    queryset = HomepageService.objects.filter(is_active=True)
    serializer_class = HomepageServiceSerializer


class HomepageDepartmentViewSet(PrincipalManagedContentViewSet):
    queryset = HomepageDepartment.objects.filter(is_active=True)
    serializer_class = HomepageDepartmentSerializer


class HomepageAboutViewSet(PrincipalManagedContentViewSet):
    queryset = HomepageAbout.objects.filter(is_active=True)
    serializer_class = HomepageAboutSerializer


class HomepageAdmissionViewSet(PrincipalManagedContentViewSet):
    queryset = HomepageAdmission.objects.filter(is_active=True).prefetch_related('steps', 'faqs')
    serializer_class = HomepageAdmissionSerializer


class HomepageAdmissionStepViewSet(PrincipalManagedContentViewSet):
    queryset = HomepageAdmissionStep.objects.all()
    serializer_class = HomepageAdmissionStepSerializer


class HomepageFAQViewSet(PrincipalManagedContentViewSet):
    queryset = HomepageFAQ.objects.filter(is_active=True)
    serializer_class = HomepageFAQSerializer


class HomepageContactViewSet(PrincipalManagedContentViewSet):
    queryset = HomepageContact.objects.filter(is_active=True)
    serializer_class = HomepageContactSerializer


class TeacherAttendanceViewSet(viewsets.ReadOnlyModelViewSet):
    """
    à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦•à¦¦à§‡à¦° à¦¡à¦¿à¦œà¦¿à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦² à¦¹à¦¾à¦œà¦¿à¦°à¦¾ (Check-in, Check-out) à¦à¦¬à¦‚ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦¸à¦¿à¦ªà¦¾à¦²à§‡à¦° à¦…à¦¨à§à¦®à§‹à¦¦à¦¨à¥¤
    """
    serializer_class = TeacherAttendanceSerializer
    permission_classes = [IsTeacherOrPrincipal]

    def get_queryset(self):
        user = self.request.user
        queryset = TeacherAttendance.objects.select_related(
            'teacher', 'teacher__teacher_profile', 'approved_by'
        ).all()

        if user.role == User.Role.TEACHER:
            queryset = queryset.filter(teacher=user)

        date_filter = self.request.query_params.get('date')
        if date_filter:
            queryset = queryset.filter(date=date_filter)

        status_filter = self.request.query_params.get('approval_status')
        if status_filter:
            queryset = queryset.filter(approval_status=status_filter.upper())

        return queryset

    @action(detail=False, methods=['post'], url_path='check-in')
    def check_in(self, request):
        if request.user.role != User.Role.TEACHER:
            return Response(
                {"success": False, "message": "à¦¶à§à¦§à§à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦•à¦—à¦£ à¦¡à¦¿à¦œà¦¿à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦² à¦šà§‡à¦•-à¦‡à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦¤à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à¦¬à§‡à¦¨à¥¤"},
                status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            )

        today = timezone.localdate()
        current_time = timezone.localtime().time().replace(microsecond=0)

        if TeacherAttendance.objects.filter(teacher=request.user, date=today).exists():
            return Response(
                {"success": False, "message": f"à¦†à¦œà¦•à§‡à¦° ({today}) à¦¡à¦¿à¦œà¦¿à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦² à¦¹à¦¾à¦œà¦¿à¦°à¦¾ à¦‡à¦¤à¦¿à¦®à¦§à§à¦¯à§‡ à¦¸à¦®à§à¦ªà¦¨à§à¦¨ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤"},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )

        attendance = TeacherAttendance.objects.create(
            teacher=request.user,
            date=today,
            check_in_time=current_time,
            remarks=request.data.get('remarks', ''),
        )

        return Response(
            {
                "success": True,
                "message": f"à¦¸à¦«à¦²à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ à¦¡à¦¿à¦œà¦¿à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦² à¦¹à¦¾à¦œà¦¿à¦°à¦¾ à¦¸à¦®à§à¦ªà¦¨à§à¦¨ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¬à§‡à¦¶à§‡à¦° à¦¸à¦®à§Ÿ: {current_time.strftime('%I:%M %p')}",
                "data": TeacherAttendanceSerializer(attendance).data,
            },
            status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
        )

    @action(detail=False, methods=['post'], url_path='check-out')
    def check_out(self, request):
        today = timezone.localdate()
        current_time = timezone.localtime().time().replace(microsecond=0)

        try:
            attendance = TeacherAttendance.objects.get(teacher=request.user, date=today)
        except TeacherAttendance.DoesNotExist:
            return Response(
                {"success": False, "message": "à¦†à¦œà¦•à§‡à¦° à¦•à§‹à¦¨à§‹ à¦šà§‡à¦•-à¦‡à¦¨ à¦°à§‡à¦•à¦°à§à¦¡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾ à¦¯à¦¾à§Ÿà¦¨à¦¿à¥¤ à¦†à¦—à§‡ à¦šà§‡à¦•-à¦‡à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à§à¦¨à¥¤"},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )

        attendance.check_out_time = current_time
        attendance.save()

        return Response(
            {
                "success": True,
                "message": f"à¦šà§‡à¦•-à¦†à¦‰à¦Ÿ à¦¸à¦«à¦²à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ à¦°à§‡à¦•à¦°à§à¦¡ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤ à¦¸à¦®à§Ÿ: {current_time.strftime('%I:%M %p')}",
                "data": TeacherAttendanceSerializer(attendance).data,
            },
            status=status.HTTP_200_OK,
        )

    @action(detail=True, methods=['patch'], permission_classes=[IsPrincipal], url_path='verify')
    def verify_attendance(self, request, pk=None):
        attendance = self.get_object()
        new_status = request.data.get('approval_status', 'APPROVED').upper()

        if new_status not in [
            TeacherAttendance.ApprovalStatus.APPROVED,
            TeacherAttendance.ApprovalStatus.REJECTED,
        ]:
            return Response(
                {"success": False, "message": "approval_status à¦¹à¦¿à¦¸à§‡à¦¬à§‡ APPROVED à¦…à¦¥à¦¬à¦¾ REJECTED à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¦à¦¾à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à§à¦¨à¥¤"},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )

        attendance.approval_status = new_status
        attendance.approved_by = request.user
        attendance.approved_at = timezone.now()
        if 'remarks' in request.data:
            attendance.remarks = request.data['remarks']
        attendance.save()

        return Response(
            {
                "success": True,
                "message": f"à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦•à§‡à¦° à¦¹à¦¾à¦œà¦¿à¦°à¦¾ à¦¸à¦«à¦²à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ '{attendance.get_approval_status_display()}' à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤",
                "data": TeacherAttendanceSerializer(attendance).data,
            },
            status=status.HTTP_200_OK,
        )


class NoticeViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    serializer_class = NoticeSerializer

    def get_permissions(self):
        if self.action in ['list', 'retrieve']:
            return [AllowAny()]
        return [IsTeacherOrPrincipal()]

    def get_queryset(self):
        user = self.request.user
        base_qs = Notice.objects.select_related('created_by', 'target_section').filter(is_active=True)

        if not user.is_authenticated:
            return base_qs.filter(audience=Notice.AudienceType.ALL)

        if user.role == User.Role.PRINCIPAL or user.is_superuser:
            return base_qs

        if user.role == User.Role.TEACHER:
            return base_qs.filter(
                Q(audience__in=[Notice.AudienceType.ALL, Notice.AudienceType.TEACHERS, Notice.AudienceType.STUDENTS])
                | Q(created_by=user)
                | Q(target_section__in_charge_teacher=user)
            ).distinct()

        if user.role == User.Role.ACCOUNTANT:
            return base_qs.filter(audience=Notice.AudienceType.ALL)

        if user.role == User.Role.STUDENT:
            student_section = getattr(getattr(user, 'student_profile', None), 'section', None)
            return base_qs.filter(
                Q(audience__in=[Notice.AudienceType.ALL, Notice.AudienceType.STUDENTS])
                | Q(audience=Notice.AudienceType.SECTION, target_section=student_section)
            ).distinct()

        return base_qs.none()

    def perform_create(self, serializer):
        serializer.save(created_by=self.request.user)

    def perform_update(self, serializer):
        instance = self.get_object()
        if self.request.user.role != User.Role.PRINCIPAL and instance.created_by != self.request.user:
            raise PermissionDenied("à¦†à¦ªà¦¨à¦¿ à¦¶à§à¦§à§à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° à¦¨à¦¿à¦œà§‡à¦° à¦¤à§ˆà¦°à¦¿ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¨à§‹à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦¶ à¦à¦¡à¦¿à¦Ÿ à¦•à¦°à¦¤à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à¦¬à§‡à¦¨à¥¤")
        serializer.save()

    def perform_destroy(self, instance):
        if self.request.user.role != User.Role.PRINCIPAL and instance.created_by != self.request.user:
            raise PermissionDenied("à¦†à¦ªà¦¨à¦¿ à¦¶à§à¦§à§à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° à¦¨à¦¿à¦œà§‡à¦° à¦¤à§ˆà¦°à¦¿ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¨à§‹à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦¶ à¦¡à¦¿à¦²à¦¿à¦Ÿ à¦•à¦°à¦¤à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à¦¬à§‡à¦¨à¥¤")
        instance.delete()


class PersonalNoteViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    serializer_class = PersonalNoteSerializer
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def get_queryset(self):
        return PersonalNote.objects.filter(user=self.request.user)

    def perform_create(self, serializer):
        serializer.save(user=self.request.user)


class AlumniRecordViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    serializer_class = AlumniRecordSerializer

    def get_permissions(self):
        if self.action in ['create', 'list', 'retrieve']:
            return [AllowAny()]
        return [IsPrincipal()]

    def get_authenticators(self):
        if self.request and self.request.method == 'POST' and not self.kwargs.get('pk'):
            return []
        return super().get_authenticators()

    def get_queryset(self):
        queryset = AlumniRecord.objects.select_related('verified_by').all()
        user = getattr(self.request, 'user', None)

        if user and user.is_authenticated and (user.role == User.Role.PRINCIPAL or user.is_superuser):
            status_filter = self.request.query_params.get('status')
            if status_filter:
                queryset = queryset.filter(status=status_filter.upper())
            return queryset

        return queryset.filter(status=AlumniRecord.VerificationStatus.APPROVED)

    def create(self, request, *args, **kwargs):
        serializer = self.get_serializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)
        alumni = serializer.save()
        return Response(
            {
                "success": True,
                "message": "à¦†à¦ªà¦¨à¦¾à¦° à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦²à§à¦®à¦¨à¦¿ à¦°à§‡à¦œà¦¿à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§à¦°à§‡à¦¶à¦¨ à¦†à¦¬à§‡à¦¦à¦¨ à¦¸à¦«à¦²à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ à¦œà¦®à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦¸à¦¿à¦ªà¦¾à¦²à§‡à¦° à¦…à¦¨à§à¦®à§‹à¦¦à¦¨à§‡à¦° à¦ªà¦° à¦à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦¤à¦¾à¦²à¦¿à¦•à¦¾à§Ÿ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦•à¦¾à¦¶à¦¿à¦¤ à¦¹à¦¬à§‡à¥¤",
                "data": AlumniRecordSerializer(alumni).data,
            },
            status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
        )

    @action(detail=True, methods=['patch'], permission_classes=[IsPrincipal], url_path='verify')
    def verify_alumni(self, request, pk=None):
        alumni = self.get_object()
        new_status = request.data.get('status', 'APPROVED').upper()

        if new_status not in [
            AlumniRecord.VerificationStatus.APPROVED,
            AlumniRecord.VerificationStatus.REJECTED,
        ]:
            return Response(
                {"success": False, "message": "status à¦¹à¦¿à¦¸à§‡à¦¬à§‡ APPROVED à¦…à¦¥à¦¬à¦¾ REJECTED à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¦à¦¾à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à§à¦¨à¥¤"},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )

        alumni.status = new_status
        alumni.verified_by = request.user
        alumni.verified_at = timezone.now()
        alumni.rejection_reason = request.data.get('rejection_reason', '')
        alumni.save()

        return Response(
            {
                "success": True,
                "message": f"à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦²à§à¦®à¦¨à¦¿ à¦ªà§à¦°à§‹à¦«à¦¾à¦‡à¦² à¦¸à¦«à¦²à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ '{alumni.get_status_display()}' à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤",
                "data": AlumniRecordSerializer(alumni).data,
            },
            status=status.HTTP_200_OK,
        )


class MarkerRequestViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    serializer_class = MarkerRequestSerializer

    def get_permissions(self):
        if self.action in ['create', 'list', 'retrieve', 'return_or_finish', 'acknowledge']:
            return [IsTeacherOrPrincipal()]
        return [IsPrincipal()]

    def get_queryset(self):
        user = self.request.user
        queryset = MarkerRequest.objects.select_related('teacher', 'issued_by').all()

        if user.role == User.Role.TEACHER:
            return queryset.filter(teacher=user)
        return queryset

    def perform_create(self, serializer):
        if self.request.user.role != User.Role.TEACHER:
            raise PermissionDenied("à¦¶à§à¦§à§à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦•à¦—à¦£ à¦®à¦¾à¦°à§à¦•à¦¾à¦°à§‡à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ à¦†à¦¬à§‡à¦¦à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦¤à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à¦¬à§‡à¦¨à¥¤")
        serializer.save(teacher=self.request.user)

    @action(detail=True, methods=['patch'], permission_classes=[IsPrincipal], url_path='issue')
    def issue_marker(self, request, pk=None):
        marker = self.get_object()
        if marker.status != MarkerRequest.RequestStatus.REQUESTED:
            return Response(
                {"success": False, "message": f"à¦®à¦¾à¦°à§à¦•à¦¾à¦°à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦‡à¦¤à¦¿à¦®à¦§à§à¦¯à§‡ '{marker.get_status_display()}' à¦…à¦¬à¦¸à§à¦¥à¦¾à§Ÿ à¦°à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤"},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST
            )

        marker.status = MarkerRequest.RequestStatus.ISSUED
        marker.issued_by = request.user
        marker.issued_at = timezone.now()
        marker.remarks = request.data.get('remarks', marker.remarks)
        marker.save()

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": "à¦®à¦¾à¦°à§à¦•à¦¾à¦°à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦¸à¦«à¦²à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦•à§‡à¦° à¦•à¦¾à¦›à§‡ à¦‡à¦¸à§à¦¯à§ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤",
            "data": MarkerRequestSerializer(marker).data
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    @action(detail=True, methods=['patch'], url_path='return-or-finish')
    def return_or_finish(self, request, pk=None):
        marker = self.get_object()
        if request.user.role == User.Role.TEACHER and marker.teacher_id != request.user.id:
            raise PermissionDenied("à¦†à¦ªà¦¨à¦¿ à¦¶à§à¦§à§à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° à¦¨à¦¿à¦œà§‡à¦° à¦®à¦¾à¦°à§à¦•à¦¾à¦°à§‡à¦° à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§à¦¯à¦¾à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦¸ à¦†à¦ªà¦¡à§‡à¦Ÿ à¦•à¦°à¦¤à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à¦¬à§‡à¦¨à¥¤")

        new_status = request.data.get('status', MarkerRequest.RequestStatus.RETURNED).upper()
        if new_status not in [MarkerRequest.RequestStatus.RETURNED, MarkerRequest.RequestStatus.DISPOSED]:
            return Response(
                {"success": False, "message": "à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§à¦¯à¦¾à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦¸ à¦¹à¦¿à¦¸à§‡à¦¬à§‡ RETURNED à¦…à¦¥à¦¬à¦¾ DISPOSED à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¦à¦¾à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à§à¦¨à¥¤"},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST
            )

        marker.status = new_status
        marker.returned_at = timezone.now()
        if 'remarks' in request.data:
            marker.remarks = request.data['remarks']
        marker.save()

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": f"à¦®à¦¾à¦°à§à¦•à¦¾à¦° à¦¸à¦«à¦²à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ '{marker.get_status_display()}' à¦¹à¦¿à¦¸à§‡à¦¬à§‡ à¦šà¦¿à¦¹à§à¦¨à¦¿à¦¤ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤",
            "data": MarkerRequestSerializer(marker).data
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    @action(detail=True, methods=['patch'], url_path='acknowledge')
    @transaction.atomic
    def acknowledge_receipt(self, request, pk=None):
        marker = self.get_object()
        if request.user.role == User.Role.TEACHER and marker.teacher_id != request.user.id:
            raise PermissionDenied("à¦†à¦ªà¦¨à¦¿ à¦¶à§à¦§à§à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° à¦¨à¦¿à¦œà§‡à¦° à¦®à¦¾à¦°à§à¦•à¦¾à¦° à¦°à¦¿à¦¸à¦¿à¦Ÿ à¦•à¦¨à¦«à¦¾à¦°à§à¦® à¦•à¦°à¦¤à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à¦¬à§‡à¦¨à¥¤")

        if marker.status != MarkerRequest.RequestStatus.ISSUED:
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "à¦®à¦¾à¦°à§à¦•à¦¾à¦°à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦à¦–à¦¨à¦“ à¦…à¦«à¦¿à¦¸ à¦¥à§‡à¦•à§‡ à¦‡à¦¸à§à¦¯à§ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà¦¨à¦¿à¥¤"}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        if marker.is_acknowledged:
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "à¦®à¦¾à¦°à§à¦•à¦¾à¦°à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦‡à¦¤à¦¿à¦®à¦§à§à¦¯à§‡ à¦¬à§à¦à§‡ à¦¨à§‡à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤"}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        stock = MarkerStock.objects.filter(color=marker.marker_color).first()
        if stock and stock.available_stock >= marker.quantity:
            stock.available_stock -= marker.quantity
            stock.save()

        marker.is_acknowledged = True
        marker.acknowledged_at = timezone.now()
        marker.save()

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": f"{marker.get_marker_color_display()} à¦®à¦¾à¦°à§à¦•à¦¾à¦° à¦¸à¦«à¦²à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ à¦¬à§à¦à§‡ à¦¨à§‡à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤ à¦¸à§‡à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà§à¦°à¦¾à¦² à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà¦• à¦†à¦ªà¦¡à§‡à¦Ÿ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤",
            "data": MarkerRequestSerializer(marker).data
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    @action(detail=False, methods=['get'], permission_classes=[IsPrincipal], url_path='monthly-usage')
    def monthly_usage_report(self, request):
        month = request.query_params.get('month', timezone.localdate().month)
        year = request.query_params.get('year', timezone.localdate().year)

        usage = MarkerRequest.objects.filter(
            is_acknowledged=True,
            acknowledged_at__year=year,
            acknowledged_at__month=month
        ).values('teacher__first_name', 'teacher__last_name', 'teacher__username', 'teacher__teacher_profile__employee_id').annotate(
            total_markers=Sum('quantity')
        ).order_by('-total_markers')

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "timeframe": f"{month}/{year}",
            "data": [
                {
                    "teacher_name": f"{item['teacher__first_name']} {item['teacher__last_name']}".strip() or item['teacher__username'],
                    "employee_id": item['teacher__teacher_profile__employee_id'],
                    "total_markers_used": item['total_markers'],
                }
                for item in usage
            ]
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)
