class StudentLeaveApplicationViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    serializer_class = StudentLeaveApplicationSerializer

    def get_permissions(self):
        return [IsAuthenticated()]

    def get_queryset(self):
        user = self.request.user
        queryset = StudentLeaveApplication.objects.select_related(
            'student', 'student__user', 'section', 'reviewed_by_incharge', 'approved_by_principal'
        ).all()

        if user.role == User.Role.PRINCIPAL or user.is_superuser:
            return queryset
        if user.role == User.Role.TEACHER:
            return queryset.filter(section__in_charge_teacher=user)
        if user.role == User.Role.STUDENT and hasattr(user, 'student_profile'):
            return queryset.filter(student=user.student_profile)
        return queryset.none()

    def perform_create(self, serializer):
        user = self.request.user
        if user.role != User.Role.STUDENT or not hasattr(user, 'student_profile'):
            raise PermissionDenied("à¦¶à§à¦§à§à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦°à¦¾ à¦›à§à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ à¦†à¦¬à§‡à¦¦à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦¤à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à¦¬à§‡à¥¤")
        
        student = user.student_profile
        serializer.save(student=student, section=student.section)

    @action(detail=True, methods=['patch'], url_path='incharge-review')
    def incharge_review(self, request, pk=None):
        leave_app = self.get_object()
        user = request.user

        if user.role != User.Role.PRINCIPAL and leave_app.section.in_charge_teacher_id != user.id:
            raise PermissionDenied(f"à¦†à¦ªà¦¨à¦¿ '{leave_app.section}' à¦¶à¦¾à¦–à¦¾à¦° à¦¶à§à¦°à§‡à¦£à¦¿ à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œ à¦¨à¦¨à¥¤")

        new_status = request.data.get('status', '').upper()
        if new_status not in [
            StudentLeaveApplication.InChargeStatus.RECOMMENDED,
            StudentLeaveApplication.InChargeStatus.REJECTED
        ]:
            return Response(
                {"success": False, "message": "status à¦¹à¦¿à¦¸à§‡à¦¬à§‡ RECOMMENDED à¦…à¦¥à¦¬à¦¾ REJECTED à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¦à¦¾à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à§à¦¨à¥¤"},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST
            )

        leave_app.incharge_status = new_status
        leave_app.incharge_remarks = request.data.get('remarks', '')
        leave_app.reviewed_by_incharge = user
        leave_app.incharge_reviewed_at = timezone.now()
        leave_app.save()

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": f"à¦†à¦¬à§‡à¦¦à¦¨à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦¸à¦«à¦²à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œ à¦•à¦°à§à¦¤à§ƒà¦• '{leave_app.get_incharge_status_display()}' à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤",
            "data": StudentLeaveApplicationSerializer(leave_app).data
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    @action(detail=True, methods=['patch'], permission_classes=[IsPrincipal], url_path='principal-review')
    @transaction.atomic
    def principal_review(self, request, pk=None):
        leave_app = self.get_object()

        if leave_app.incharge_status != StudentLeaveApplication.InChargeStatus.RECOMMENDED:
            force_override = request.data.get('force_approve', False)
            if not force_override:
                return Response(
                    {
                        "success": False,
                        "message": f"à¦à¦‡ à¦†à¦¬à§‡à¦¦à¦¨à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦à¦–à¦¨à§‹ à¦¶à§à¦°à§‡à¦£à¦¿ à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œ à¦•à¦°à§à¦¤à§ƒà¦• à¦¸à§à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à¦¿à¦¶ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà¦¨à¦¿à¥¤ à¦¤à¦¬à§à¦“ à¦…à¦¨à§à¦®à§‹à¦¦à¦¨ à¦¦à¦¿à¦¤à§‡ à¦šà¦¾à¦‡à¦²à§‡ 'force_approve': true à¦ªà¦¾à¦ à¦¾à¦¨à¥¤"
                    },
                    status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST
                )

        new_status = request.data.get('status', '').upper()
        if new_status not in [
            StudentLeaveApplication.PrincipalStatus.APPROVED,
            StudentLeaveApplication.PrincipalStatus.REJECTED
        ]:
            return Response(
                {"success": False, "message": "status à¦¹à¦¿à¦¸à§‡à¦¬à§‡ APPROVED à¦…à¦¥à¦¬à¦¾ REJECTED à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¦à¦¾à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à§à¦¨à¥¤"},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST
            )

        leave_app.principal_status = new_status
        leave_app.principal_remarks = request.data.get('remarks', '')
        leave_app.approved_by_principal = request.user
        leave_app.principal_reviewed_at = timezone.now()
        leave_app.save()

        if new_status == StudentLeaveApplication.PrincipalStatus.APPROVED:
            curr_date = leave_app.start_date
            while curr_date <= leave_app.end_date:
                StudentAttendance.objects.update_or_create(
                    student=leave_app.student,
                    date=curr_date,
                    defaults={
                        'section': leave_app.section,
                        'status': StudentAttendance.AttendanceStatus.LEAVE,
                        'remarks': f"à¦›à§à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦…à¦¨à§à¦®à§‹à¦¦à¦¿à¦¤ (à¦†à¦¬à§‡à¦¦à¦¨ à¦†à¦‡à¦¡à¦¿: {leave_app.id})",
                        'recorded_by': request.user
                    }
                )
                curr_date += timedelta(days=1)

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": f"à¦†à¦¬à§‡à¦¦à¦¨à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦¸à¦«à¦²à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ '{leave_app.get_principal_status_display()}' à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡ à¦à¦¬à¦‚ à¦¹à¦¾à¦œà¦¿à¦°à¦¾à§Ÿ à¦¸à§à¦¬à§Ÿà¦‚à¦•à§à¦°à¦¿à§Ÿà¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ à¦›à§à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦° à¦°à§‡à¦•à¦°à§à¦¡ à¦†à¦ªà¦¡à§‡à¦Ÿ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤",
            "data": StudentLeaveApplicationSerializer(leave_app).data
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class CertificateRequestViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    serializer_class = CertificateRequestSerializer

    def get_permissions(self):
        return [IsAuthenticated()]

    def get_queryset(self):
        user = self.request.user
        queryset = CertificateRequest.objects.select_related('student', 'student__user').all()

        if user.role == User.Role.STUDENT and hasattr(user, 'student_profile'):
            return queryset.filter(student=user.student_profile)
        if user.role in [User.Role.PRINCIPAL, User.Role.ACCOUNTANT] or user.is_superuser:
            return queryset
        return queryset.none()

    def perform_create(self, serializer):
        user = self.request.user
        if user.role != User.Role.STUDENT or not hasattr(user, 'student_profile'):
            raise PermissionDenied("à¦¶à§à¦§à§à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦°à¦¾ à¦¸à¦¾à¦°à§à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦«à¦¿à¦•à§‡à¦Ÿà§‡à¦° à¦†à¦¬à§‡à¦¦à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦¤à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à§‡à¥¤")
        
        cert_type = serializer.validated_data.get('certificate_type')
        fee = Decimal('200.00') if cert_type in [CertificateRequest.CertType.TESTIMONIAL, CertificateRequest.CertType.TRANSFER] else Decimal('100.00')
        serializer.save(student=user.student_profile, fee_amount=fee)

    @action(detail=True, methods=['patch'], permission_classes=[IsAccountantOrPrincipal], url_path='mark-fee-paid')
    def mark_fee_paid(self, request, pk=None):
        req_obj = self.get_object()
        req_obj.is_fee_paid = True
        req_obj.save()
        return Response({"success": True, "message": "à¦¸à¦¾à¦°à§à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦«à¦¿à¦•à§‡à¦Ÿ à¦«à¦¿ à¦ªà¦°à¦¿à¦¶à§‹à¦§à¦¿à¦¤ à¦¹à¦¿à¦¸à§‡à¦¬à§‡ à¦šà¦¿à¦¹à§à¦¨à¦¿à¦¤ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤", "data": CertificateRequestSerializer(req_obj).data})

    @action(detail=True, methods=['patch'], permission_classes=[IsPrincipal], url_path='principal-approve')
    def principal_approve(self, request, pk=None):
        req_obj = self.get_object()
        status_input = request.data.get('status', 'APPROVED').upper()

        if status_input == 'APPROVED':
            if not req_obj.is_fee_paid:
                return Response({"success": False, "message": "à¦†à¦¬à§‡à¦¦à¦¨ à¦«à¦¿ à¦ªà¦°à¦¿à¦¶à§‹à¦§ à¦¨à¦¾ à¦¹à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾ à¦ªà¦°à§à¦¯à¦¨à§à¦¤ à¦…à¦¨à§à¦®à§‹à¦¦à¦¨ à¦¦à§‡à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾ à¦¸à¦®à§à¦­à¦¬ à¦¨à§Ÿà¥¤"}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

            req_obj.status = CertificateRequest.RequestStatus.APPROVED
            req_obj.approved_by = request.user
            req_obj.approved_at = timezone.now()
            if not req_obj.certificate_number:
                req_obj.certificate_number = f"CERT-{req_obj.certificate_type}-{timezone.now().year}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6].upper()}"
            req_obj.save()

            message = f"à¦†à¦ªà¦¨à¦¾à¦° {req_obj.get_certificate_type_display()} à¦¸à¦«à¦²à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ à¦…à¦¨à§à¦®à§‹à¦¦à¦¿à¦¤ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤ à¦¸à¦¨à¦¦ à¦¨à¦®à§à¦¬à¦°: {req_obj.certificate_number}à¥¤"
        else:
            req_obj.status = CertificateRequest.RequestStatus.REJECTED
            req_obj.rejection_reason = request.data.get('rejection_reason', 'à¦…à¦§à§à¦¯à¦•à§à¦· à¦•à¦°à§à¦¤à§ƒà¦• à¦¬à¦¾à¦¤à¦¿à¦²à¦•à§ƒà¦¤')
            req_obj.save()
            message = "à¦†à¦¬à§‡à¦¦à¦¨à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦¬à¦¾à¦¤à¦¿à¦² à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤"

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": message,
            "data": CertificateRequestSerializer(req_obj).data
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class StudentComplaintViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    serializer_class = StudentComplaintSerializer

    def get_permissions(self):
        return [IsAuthenticated()]

    def get_queryset(self):
        user = self.request.user
        queryset = StudentComplaint.objects.select_related('student', 'student__user').all()

        if user.role == User.Role.PRINCIPAL or user.is_superuser:
            return queryset
        if user.role == User.Role.STUDENT and hasattr(user, 'student_profile'):
            return queryset.filter(student=user.student_profile)
        return queryset.none()

    def perform_create(self, serializer):
        user = self.request.user
        if user.role != User.Role.STUDENT or not hasattr(user, 'student_profile'):
            raise PermissionDenied("à¦¶à§à¦§à§à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦°à¦¾ à¦…à¦­à¦¿à¦¯à§‹à¦— à¦¬à¦¾ à¦ªà¦°à¦¾à¦®à¦°à§à¦¶ à¦œà¦®à¦¾ à¦¦à¦¿à¦¤à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à¦¬à§‡à¥¤")
        serializer.save(student=user.student_profile)

    @action(detail=True, methods=['patch'], permission_classes=[IsPrincipal], url_path='resolve')
    def resolve_complaint(self, request, pk=None):
        complaint = self.get_object()
        complaint.status = request.data.get('status', StudentComplaint.ComplaintStatus.RESOLVED).upper()
        complaint.principal_remarks = request.data.get('remarks', '')
        complaint.action_taken_at = timezone.now()
        complaint.save()

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": f"à¦…à¦­à¦¿à¦¯à§‹à¦—à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦¸à¦«à¦²à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ '{complaint.get_status_display()}' à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤",
            "data": StudentComplaintSerializer(complaint, context={'request': request}).data
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class ParentCommunicationLogViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    serializer_class = ParentCommunicationLogSerializer

    def get_permissions(self):
        return [IsTeacherOrPrincipal()]

    def get_queryset(self):
        user = self.request.user
        queryset = ParentCommunicationLog.objects.select_related('student', 'student__user', 'in_charge').all()

        if user.role == User.Role.PRINCIPAL or user.is_superuser:
            return queryset
        if user.role == User.Role.TEACHER:
            return queryset.filter(in_charge=user)
        return queryset.none()

    def perform_create(self, serializer):
        user = self.request.user
        student = serializer.validated_data['student']

        if user.role == User.Role.TEACHER and student.section.in_charge_teacher_id != user.id:
            raise PermissionDenied("à¦†à¦ªà¦¨à¦¿ à¦à¦‡ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦° à¦¶à§à¦°à§‡à¦£à¦¿ à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œ à¦¨à¦¨à¥¤")

        phone = serializer.validated_data.get('guardian_phone_called') or student.guardian_phone
        serializer.save(in_charge=user, guardian_phone_called=phone)


class TeacherLeaveViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    serializer_class = TeacherLeaveSerializer

    def get_permissions(self):
        return [IsTeacherOrPrincipal()]

    def get_queryset(self):
        user = self.request.user
        queryset = TeacherLeave.objects.select_related('teacher', 'proxy_teacher', 'reviewed_by').all()

        if user.role == User.Role.PRINCIPAL or user.is_superuser:
            return queryset
        if user.role == User.Role.TEACHER:
            return queryset.filter(teacher=user)
        return queryset.none()

    def perform_create(self, serializer):
        if self.request.user.role != User.Role.TEACHER:
            raise PermissionDenied("à¦¶à§à¦§à§à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦•à¦—à¦£ à¦à¦‡ à¦›à§à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ à¦†à¦¬à§‡à¦¦à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦¤à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à¦¬à§‡à¦¨à¥¤")
        serializer.save(teacher=self.request.user)

    @action(detail=False, methods=['get'], url_path='my-proxy-classes')
    def my_proxy_classes(self, request):
        if request.user.role != User.Role.TEACHER:
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "à¦¶à§à¦§à§à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦•à¦¦à§‡à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¯à§‹à¦œà§à¦¯à¥¤"}, status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)

        proxies = TeacherLeave.objects.filter(
            proxy_teacher=request.user,
            status=TeacherLeave.LeaveStatus.APPROVED,
            end_date__gte=timezone.localdate()
        ).select_related('teacher')

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "active_proxy_duties_count": proxies.count(),
            "data": TeacherLeaveSerializer(proxies, many=True).data
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    @action(detail=True, methods=['patch'], permission_classes=[IsPrincipal], url_path='review-leave')
    def review_leave(self, request, pk=None):
        leave_obj = self.get_object()
        new_status = request.data.get('status', 'APPROVED').upper()

        if new_status not in [TeacherLeave.LeaveStatus.APPROVED, TeacherLeave.LeaveStatus.REJECTED]:
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "status à¦¹à¦¿à¦¸à§‡à¦¬à§‡ APPROVED à¦…à¦¥à¦¬à¦¾ REJECTED à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¦à¦¾à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à§à¦¨à¥¤"}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        leave_obj.status = new_status
        leave_obj.reviewed_by = request.user
        leave_obj.reviewed_at = timezone.now()
        leave_obj.principal_remarks = request.data.get('remarks', '')
        leave_obj.save()

        msg = f"à¦›à§à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦° à¦†à¦¬à§‡à¦¦à¦¨à¦Ÿà¦¿ '{leave_obj.get_status_display()}' à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤"
        if new_status == TeacherLeave.LeaveStatus.APPROVED and leave_obj.proxy_teacher:
            msg += f" à¦ªà§à¦°à¦•à§à¦¸à¦¿ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦• {leave_obj.proxy_teacher.get_full_name()} à¦à¦° à¦•à¦¾à¦›à§‡ à¦¨à§‹à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦«à¦¿à¦•à§‡à¦¶à¦¨ à¦ªà¦¾à¦ à¦¾à¦¨à§‹ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤"

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": msg,
            "data": TeacherLeaveSerializer(leave_obj).data
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class SMSBroadcastViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    queryset = SMSBroadcastLog.objects.all()
    serializer_class = SMSBroadcastLogSerializer
    permission_classes = [IsPrincipal]

    @action(detail=False, methods=['post'], url_path='dispatch')
    @transaction.atomic
    def dispatch_sms(self, request):
        target = request.data.get('target_group')
        message_body = request.data.get('message_body', '')

        if not target or not message_body:
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "target_group à¦à¦¬à¦‚ message_body à¦†à¦¬à¦¶à§à¦¯à¦•à¥¤"}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        recipient_phones = []

        if target == SMSBroadcastLog.TargetGroup.ABSENT_TODAY:
            today = timezone.localdate()
            absent_attendances = StudentAttendance.objects.filter(
                date=today,
                status=StudentAttendance.AttendanceStatus.ABSENT
            ).select_related('student')
            recipient_phones = [att.student.guardian_phone for att in absent_attendances if att.student.guardian_phone]

        elif target == SMSBroadcastLog.TargetGroup.FEE_DEFAULTERS:
            defaulters = StudentInvoice.objects.filter(due_amount__gt=Decimal('0.00')).select_related('student')
            recipient_phones = list(set([inv.student.guardian_phone for inv in defaulters if inv.student.guardian_phone]))

        elif target == SMSBroadcastLog.TargetGroup.ALL_TEACHERS:
            teachers = User.objects.filter(role=User.Role.TEACHER, is_active=True)
            recipient_phones = [t.phone for t in teachers if t.phone]

        elif target == SMSBroadcastLog.TargetGroup.SECTION_STUDENTS:
            sec_id = request.data.get('section_id')
            students = StudentProfile.objects.filter(section_id=sec_id, user__is_active=True)
            recipient_phones = [s.guardian_phone for s in students if s.guardian_phone]

        log = SMSBroadcastLog.objects.create(
            sender=request.user,
            target_group=target,
            message_body=message_body,
            recipients_count=len(recipient_phones),
            recipients_sample=", ".join(recipient_phones[:10])
        )

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": f"à¦®à§‹à¦Ÿ {len(recipient_phones)} à¦œà¦¨ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¾à¦ªà¦•à§‡à¦° à¦•à¦¾à¦›à§‡ à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦°à§à¦—à§‡à¦Ÿà§‡à¦¡ SMS à¦¸à¦«à¦²à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ à¦ªà§à¦°à§‡à¦°à¦¿à¦¤ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤",
            "log": SMSBroadcastLogSerializer(log).data,
            "total_recipients": len(recipient_phones),
        }, status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)


class ClubViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    queryset = Club.objects.select_related('moderator_teacher').annotate(
        approved_member_count=Count(
            'memberships',
            filter=Q(memberships__status=ClubMembership.MembershipStatus.APPROVED),
        )
    ).all()
    serializer_class = ClubSerializer

    def get_permissions(self):
        if self.action in ['list', 'retrieve']:
            return [IsAuthenticated()]
        return [IsPrincipal()]

    @action(detail=True, methods=['post'], url_path='apply')
    def apply_membership(self, request, pk=None):
        if request.user.role != User.Role.STUDENT or not hasattr(request.user, 'student_profile'):
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "à¦¶à§à¦§à§à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦°à¦¾ à¦•à§à¦²à¦¾à¦¬à§‡à¦° à¦¸à¦¦à¦¸à§à¦¯ à¦¹à¦¤à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à§‡à¥¤"}, status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)

        club = self.get_object()
        student = request.user.student_profile

        if ClubMembership.objects.filter(club=club, student=student).exists():
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "à¦†à¦ªà¦¨à¦¿ à¦‡à¦¤à¦¿à¦®à¦§à§à¦¯à§‡ à¦à¦‡ à¦•à§à¦²à¦¾à¦¬à§‡à¦° à¦¸à¦¦à¦¸à§à¦¯à¦¤à¦¾à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ à¦†à¦¬à§‡à¦¦à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à§‡à¦›à§‡à¦¨à¥¤"}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        membership = ClubMembership.objects.create(
            club=club,
            student=student,
            reason=request.data.get('reason', '')
        )

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": f"{club.name}-à¦ à¦†à¦ªà¦¨à¦¾à¦° à¦¸à¦¦à¦¸à§à¦¯à¦¤à¦¾à¦° à¦†à¦¬à§‡à¦¦à¦¨ à¦œà¦®à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤ à¦•à§à¦²à¦¾à¦¬ à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦• à¦¯à¦¾à¦šà¦¾à¦‡ à¦•à¦°à§‡ à¦…à¦¨à§à¦®à§‹à¦¦à¦¨ à¦¦à§‡à¦¬à§‡à¦¨à¥¤",
            "data": ClubMembershipSerializer(membership).data
        }, status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)


class ClubMembershipViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    serializer_class = ClubMembershipSerializer

    def get_permissions(self):
        return [IsAuthenticated()]

    def get_queryset(self):
        user = self.request.user
        queryset = ClubMembership.objects.select_related('club', 'student', 'student__user').all()

        if user.role == User.Role.PRINCIPAL or user.is_superuser:
            return queryset
        if user.role == User.Role.TEACHER:
            return queryset.filter(club__moderator_teacher=user)
        if user.role == User.Role.STUDENT and hasattr(user, 'student_profile'):
            return queryset.filter(student=user.student_profile)
        return queryset.none()

    @action(detail=True, methods=['patch'], url_path='review')
    def review_membership(self, request, pk=None):
        membership = self.get_object()
        user = request.user

        if user.role != User.Role.PRINCIPAL and membership.club.moderator_teacher_id != user.id:
            raise PermissionDenied("à¦†à¦ªà¦¨à¦¿ à¦à¦‡ à¦•à§à¦²à¦¾à¦¬à§‡à¦° à¦¦à¦¾à§Ÿà¦¿à¦¤à§à¦¬à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¾à¦ªà§à¦¤ à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦• à¦¨à¦¨à¥¤")

        new_status = request.data.get('status', 'APPROVED').upper()
        if new_status not in [
            ClubMembership.MembershipStatus.APPROVED,
            ClubMembership.MembershipStatus.REJECTED,
        ]:
            return Response(
                {"success": False, "message": "status à¦¹à¦¿à¦¸à§‡à¦¬à§‡ APPROVED à¦…à¦¥à¦¬à¦¾ REJECTED à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¦à¦¾à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à§à¦¨à¥¤"},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )
        membership.status = new_status
        membership.reviewed_by = user
        membership.reviewed_at = timezone.now()
        membership.save()

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": f"à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦° à¦¸à¦¦à¦¸à§à¦¯à¦ªà¦¦ à¦¸à¦«à¦²à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ '{membership.get_status_display()}' à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤",
            "data": ClubMembershipSerializer(membership).data
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class ClubEventViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    queryset = ClubEvent.objects.select_related('club').annotate(
        participant_count=Count('registrations')
    ).all()
    serializer_class = ClubEventSerializer

    def get_permissions(self):
        if self.action in ['list', 'retrieve', 'register_event']:
            return [IsAuthenticated()]
        return [IsTeacherOrPrincipal()]

    def perform_create(self, serializer):
        user = self.request.user
        club = serializer.validated_data['club']
        if user.role != User.Role.PRINCIPAL and club.moderator_teacher_id != user.id:
            raise PermissionDenied("à¦†à¦ªà¦¨à¦¿ à¦¶à§à¦§à§à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° à¦¨à¦¿à¦œà§‡à¦° à¦•à§à¦²à¦¾à¦¬à§‡à¦° à¦‡à¦­à§‡à¦¨à§à¦Ÿ à¦¤à§ˆà¦°à¦¿ à¦•à¦°à¦¤à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à¦¬à§‡à¦¨à¥¤")
        serializer.save()

    @action(detail=True, methods=['post'], url_path='register')
    def register_event(self, request, pk=None):
        if request.user.role != User.Role.STUDENT or not hasattr(request.user, 'student_profile'):
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "à¦¶à§à¦§à§à¦®à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦°à¦¾ à¦‡à¦­à§‡à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà§‡ à¦°à§‡à¦œà¦¿à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§à¦°à§‡à¦¶à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦¤à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦°à§‡à¥¤"}, status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)

        event = self.get_object()
        student = request.user.student_profile

        if event.registration_deadline < timezone.localdate():
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "à¦¦à§à¦ƒà¦–à¦¿à¦¤, à¦à¦‡ à¦‡à¦­à§‡à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà§‡à¦° à¦°à§‡à¦œà¦¿à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§à¦°à§‡à¦¶à¦¨à§‡à¦° à¦¸à¦®à§Ÿà¦¸à§€à¦®à¦¾ à¦¶à§‡à¦· à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡ à¦—à§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤"}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        if EventRegistration.objects.filter(event=event, student=student).exists():
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "à¦†à¦ªà¦¨à¦¿ à¦‡à¦¤à¦¿à¦®à¦§à§à¦¯à§‡ à¦à¦‡ à¦‡à¦­à§‡à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà§‡ à¦°à§‡à¦œà¦¿à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§à¦°à§‡à¦¶à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à§‡à¦›à§‡à¦¨à¥¤"}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        reg = EventRegistration.objects.create(event=event, student=student)
        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": f"'{event.title}' à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¤à¦¿à¦¯à§‹à¦—à¦¿à¦¤à¦¾à§Ÿ à¦†à¦ªà¦¨à¦¾à¦° à¦¨à¦¾à¦® à¦¸à¦«à¦²à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ à¦°à§‡à¦œà¦¿à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§à¦°à§‡à¦¶à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤",
            "data": EventRegistrationSerializer(reg).data
        }, status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)


class WhatsAppNotificationViewSet(viewsets.GenericViewSet):
    permission_classes = [IsTeacherOrPrincipal]

    @action(detail=False, methods=['get'], url_path='templates')
    def get_templates(self, request):
        templates = WhatsAppTemplate.objects.filter(is_active=True)
        return Response({"success": True, "data": WhatsAppTemplateSerializer(templates, many=True).data})

    @action(detail=False, methods=['post'], url_path='send-absent-alert')
    @transaction.atomic
    def send_absent_alert(self, request):
        user = request.user
        today = timezone.localdate()
        section_id = request.data.get('section_id')

        template = WhatsAppTemplate.objects.filter(template_type=WhatsAppTemplate.TemplateType.ABSENT_ALERT, is_active=True).first()
        template_body = template.body_template if template else "à¦†à¦¸à¦¸à¦¾à¦²à¦¾à¦®à§ à¦†à¦²à¦¾à¦‡à¦•à§à¦®à¥¤ à¦†à¦ªà¦¨à¦¾à¦° à¦¸à¦¨à§à¦¤à¦¾à¦¨ {student_name} (à¦°à§‹à¦²: {roll}) à¦†à¦œ {date} à¦¤à¦¾à¦°à¦¿à¦–à§‡ à¦•à¦²à§‡à¦œà§‡ à¦…à¦¨à§à¦ªà¦¸à§à¦¥à¦¿à¦¤ à¦°à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤ - {college_name}"

        absent_qs = StudentAttendance.objects.filter(date=today, status=StudentAttendance.AttendanceStatus.ABSENT).select_related('student', 'student__user', 'section')

        if user.role == User.Role.TEACHER:
            absent_qs = absent_qs.filter(section__in_charge_teacher=user)
        elif section_id:
            absent_qs = absent_qs.filter(section_id=section_id)

        sent_count = 0
        for att in absent_qs:
            s = att.student
            if not s.guardian_phone:
                continue

            msg = WhatsAppNotificationService.render_template(template_body, {
                "student_name": s.user.get_full_name(),
                "roll": s.roll_number,
                "section_name": str(s.section),
                "date": today.strftime('%d-%m-%Y'),
            })

            WhatsAppNotificationService.send_whatsapp(
                sender_user=user,
                recipient_phone=s.guardian_phone,
                recipient_name=s.father_name or s.user.get_full_name(),
                message_text=msg,
                student=s,
                template=template
            )
            sent_count += 1

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": f"à¦®à§‹à¦Ÿ {sent_count} à¦œà¦¨ à¦…à¦­à¦¿à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à¦•à§‡à¦° à¦¹à§‹à§Ÿà¦¾à¦Ÿà¦¸à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦ªà§‡ à¦…à¦¨à§à¦ªà¦¸à§à¦¥à¦¿à¦¤à¦¿ à¦¬à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¤à¦¾ à¦ªà¦¾à¦ à¦¾à¦¨à§‹ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤",
            "total_sent": sent_count
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    @action(detail=False, methods=['post'], url_path='send-fee-reminder')
    @transaction.atomic
    def send_fee_reminder(self, request):
        user = request.user
        section_id = request.data.get('section_id')

        template = WhatsAppTemplate.objects.filter(template_type=WhatsAppTemplate.TemplateType.FEE_REMINDER, is_active=True).first()
        template_body = template.body_template if template else "à¦†à¦¸à¦¸à¦¾à¦²à¦¾à¦®à§ à¦†à¦²à¦¾à¦‡à¦•à§à¦®à¥¤ {student_name} (à¦°à§‹à¦²: {roll}) à¦à¦° {month} à¦®à¦¾à¦¸à§‡à¦° à¦¬à§‡à¦¤à¦¨ à¦¬à¦¾à¦¬à¦¦ à¦®à§‹à¦Ÿ {due_amount} à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦•à¦¾ à¦¬à¦•à§‡à§Ÿà¦¾ à¦°à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤ à¦¨à¦¿à¦°à§à¦§à¦¾à¦°à¦¿à¦¤ à¦¸à¦®à§Ÿà§‡à¦° à¦®à¦§à§à¦¯à§‡ à¦ªà¦°à¦¿à¦¶à§‹à¦§à§‡à¦° à¦…à¦¨à§à¦°à§‹à¦§ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à¦²à§‹à¥¤ - {college_name}"

        invoices = StudentInvoice.objects.filter(due_amount__gt=Decimal('0.00')).select_related('student', 'student__user', 'section')

        if user.role == User.Role.TEACHER:
            invoices = invoices.filter(section__in_charge_teacher=user)
        elif section_id:
            invoices = invoices.filter(section_id=section_id)

        sent_count = 0
        for inv in invoices:
            s = inv.student
            if not s.guardian_phone:
                continue

            msg = WhatsAppNotificationService.render_template(template_body, {
                "student_name": s.user.get_full_name(),
                "roll": s.roll_number,
                "month": f"{inv.month}/{inv.year}",
                "due_amount": float(inv.due_amount),
            })

            WhatsAppNotificationService.send_whatsapp(
                sender_user=user,
                recipient_phone=s.guardian_phone,
                recipient_name=s.father_name or s.user.get_full_name(),
                message_text=msg,
                student=s,
                template=template
            )
            sent_count += 1

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": f"à¦®à§‹à¦Ÿ {sent_count} à¦œà¦¨ à¦…à¦­à¦¿à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à¦•à§‡à¦° à¦¹à§‹à§Ÿà¦¾à¦Ÿà¦¸à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦ªà§‡ à¦¬à¦•à§‡à§Ÿà¦¾ à¦¤à¦¾à¦—à¦¾à¦¦à¦¾ à¦ªà¦¾à¦ à¦¾à¦¨à§‹ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤",
            "total_sent": sent_count
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    @action(detail=False, methods=['post'], url_path='send-custom')
    def send_custom(self, request):
        user = request.user
        student_id = request.data.get('student_id')
        recipient_type = request.data.get('recipient_type', 'GUARDIAN')
        custom_message = request.data.get('custom_message', '').strip()

        if not student_id or not custom_message:
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "student_id à¦à¦¬à¦‚ custom_message à¦†à¦¬à¦¶à§à¦¯à¦•à¥¤"}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        student = StudentProfile.objects.select_related('user', 'section').get(id=student_id)

        if user.role == User.Role.TEACHER and student.section.in_charge_teacher_id != user.id:
            raise PermissionDenied("à¦†à¦ªà¦¨à¦¿ à¦à¦‡ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦° à¦¶à§à¦°à§‡à¦£à¦¿ à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œ à¦¨à¦¨à¥¤")

        phone = student.guardian_phone if recipient_type == 'GUARDIAN' else student.user.phone
        name = student.father_name if recipient_type == 'GUARDIAN' else student.user.get_full_name()

        if not phone:
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¦à¦¤à§à¦¤ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦° à¦•à§‹à¦¨à§‹ à¦«à§‹à¦¨ à¦¨à¦®à§à¦¬à¦° à¦ªà¦¾à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾ à¦¯à¦¾à§Ÿà¦¨à¦¿à¥¤"}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        log = WhatsAppNotificationService.send_whatsapp(
            sender_user=user,
            recipient_phone=phone,
            recipient_name=name or student.user.username,
            message_text=custom_message,
            student=student
        )

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": f"{name} à¦à¦° à¦¹à§‹à§Ÿà¦¾à¦Ÿà¦¸à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦ªà§‡ à¦¬à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¤à¦¾à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦¸à¦«à¦²à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦ à¦¾à¦¨à§‹ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤",
            "log": WhatsAppMessageLogSerializer(log).data
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    @action(detail=False, methods=['get'], url_path='history')
    def message_history(self, request):
        user = request.user
        queryset = WhatsAppMessageLog.objects.select_related('sender', 'student').all()

        if user.role == User.Role.TEACHER:
            queryset = queryset.filter(sender=user)

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "total_messages": queryset.count(),
            "history": WhatsAppMessageLogSerializer(queryset[:50], many=True).data
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class NotificationViewSet(viewsets.GenericViewSet):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def get_queryset(self):
        return InAppNotification.objects.filter(recipient=self.request.user)

    def list(self, request):
        queryset = self.get_queryset()[:30]
        serializer = InAppNotificationSerializer(queryset, many=True)
        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "unread_count": self.get_queryset().filter(is_read=False).count(),
            "notifications": serializer.data
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    @action(detail=False, methods=['get'], url_path='unread-count')
    def unread_count(self, request):
        count = self.get_queryset().filter(is_read=False).count()
        return Response({"success": True, "unread_count": count}, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    @action(detail=True, methods=['patch'], url_path='mark-read')
    def mark_read(self, request, pk=None):
        notif = self.get_queryset().filter(id=pk).first()
        if not notif:
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "à¦¨à§‹à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦«à¦¿à¦•à§‡à¦¶à¦¨ à¦ªà¦¾à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾ à¦¯à¦¾à§Ÿà¦¨à¦¿à¥¤"}, status=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND)

        notif.is_read = True
        notif.read_at = timezone.now()
        notif.save()
        return Response({"success": True, "message": "à¦ªà§œà¦¾ à¦¸à¦®à§à¦ªà¦¨à§à¦¨ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤", "data": InAppNotificationSerializer(notif).data})

    @action(detail=False, methods=['post'], url_path='mark-all-read')
    def mark_all_read(self, request):
        updated = self.get_queryset().filter(is_read=False).update(is_read=True, read_at=timezone.now())
        return Response({"success": True, "message": f"{updated}à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦¨à§‹à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦«à¦¿à¦•à§‡à¦¶à¦¨ à¦ªà¦ à¦¿à¦¤ à¦¹à¦¿à¦¸à§‡à¦¬à§‡ à¦šà¦¿à¦¹à§à¦¨à¦¿à¦¤ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤"}, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    @action(detail=False, methods=['post'], url_path='subscribe-device')
    def subscribe_device(self, request):
        serializer = DevicePushSubscriptionSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        sub, created = DevicePushSubscription.objects.update_or_create(
            user=request.user,
            endpoint=serializer.validated_data['endpoint'],
            defaults={
                'device_type': serializer.validated_data.get('device_type', DevicePushSubscription.DeviceType.WEB),
                'p256dh_key': serializer.validated_data.get('p256dh_key', ''),
                'auth_key': serializer.validated_data.get('auth_key', ''),
                'fcm_token': serializer.validated_data.get('fcm_token', ''),
                'is_active': True,
            }
        )

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": "à¦¡à¦¿à¦­à¦¾à¦‡à¦¸à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦…à¦«à¦²à¦¾à¦‡à¦¨ à¦ªà§à¦¶ à¦¨à§‹à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦«à¦¿à¦•à§‡à¦¶à¦¨à§‡à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ à¦¸à¦«à¦²à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ à¦¨à¦¿à¦¬à¦¨à§à¦§à¦¿à¦¤ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤",
            "subscription_id": str(sub.id)
        }, status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED if created else status.HTTP_200_OK)

from django.contrib import admin
from django.contrib.auth.admin import UserAdmin as BaseUserAdmin
from apps.users_auth.models import StudentProfile, TeacherProfile, User

@admin.register(User)
class UserAdmin(BaseUserAdmin):
    list_display = ('username', 'get_full_name', 'role', 'phone', 'is_library_manager', 'is_active', 'created_at')
    list_filter = ('role', 'is_library_manager', 'is_active')
    search_fields = ('username', 'first_name', 'last_name', 'phone', 'email')
    ordering = ('-created_at',)

    fieldsets = BaseUserAdmin.fieldsets + (
        ('College ERP Role & Info', {
            'fields': ('role', 'phone', 'profile_photo', 'is_library_manager'),
        }),
    )

    add_fieldsets = BaseUserAdmin.add_fieldsets + (
        ('College ERP Role & Info', {
            'fields': ('first_name', 'last_name', 'email', 'role', 'phone', 'is_library_manager'),
        }),
    )

@admin.register(TeacherProfile)
class TeacherProfileAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ('employee_id', 'user', 'designation', 'department', 'base_salary', 'joining_date')
    search_fields = ('employee_id', 'user__first_name', 'user__last_name', 'department')
    list_filter = ('department', 'designation')

@admin.register(StudentProfile)
class StudentProfileAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ('student_unique_id', 'roll_number', 'user', 'gender', 'section', 'assigned_vehicle', 'uses_transport')
    search_fields = ('student_unique_id', 'user__first_name', 'user__last_name', 'roll_number')
    list_filter = ('gender', 'section', 'uses_transport', 'assigned_vehicle')

import uuid
from django.core import signing
from django.contrib.auth.models import AbstractUser
from django.db import models
from apps.common.models import BaseModel


class User(AbstractUser):
    class Role(models.TextChoices):
        PRINCIPAL = 'PRINCIPAL', 'Principal (Super Admin)'
        TEACHER = 'TEACHER', 'Teacher'
        ACCOUNTANT = 'ACCOUNTANT', 'Accountant'
        STUDENT = 'STUDENT', 'Student'

    id = models.UUIDField(primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4, editable=False)
    role = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=Role.choices, db_index=True)
    phone = models.CharField(max_length=20, unique=True, null=True, blank=True, db_index=True)
    profile_photo = models.ImageField(upload_to='profiles/%Y/%m/', null=True, blank=True)
    
    # à¦¸à¦¾à¦®à§Ÿà¦¿à¦•à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ à¦²à¦¾à¦‡à¦¬à§à¦°à§‡à¦°à¦¿à¦° à¦¦à¦¾à§Ÿà¦¿à¦¤à§à¦¬à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¾à¦ªà§à¦¤ à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦«à§‡à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ à¦«à§à¦²à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦—
    is_library_manager = models.BooleanField(default=False)

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    REQUIRED_FIELDS = ['role']

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'User'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Users'
        ordering = ['-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.username} [{self.get_role_display()}]"

    @property
    def is_principal(self):
        return self.role == self.Role.PRINCIPAL

    @property
    def is_teacher(self):
        return self.role == self.Role.TEACHER

    @property
    def is_accountant(self):
        return self.role == self.Role.ACCOUNTANT

    @property
    def is_student(self):
        return self.role == self.Role.STUDENT


class TeacherProfile(BaseModel):
    """
    à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦•à¦¦à§‡à¦° à¦¬à¦¿à¦¸à§à¦¤à¦¾à¦°à¦¿à¦¤ à¦ªà§à¦°à§‹à¦«à¦¾à¦‡à¦², à¦®à§‚à¦² à¦¬à§‡à¦¤à¦¨ à¦“ à¦ªà¦¾à¦¬à¦²à¦¿à¦• à¦“à§Ÿà§‡à¦¬à¦¸à¦¾à¦‡à¦Ÿà§‡à¦° à¦ªà¦°à¦¿à¦šà¦¿à¦¤à¦¿à¥¤
    """
    user = models.OneToOneField(User, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='teacher_profile')
    employee_id = models.CharField(max_length=30, unique=True, db_index=True)
    designation = models.CharField(max_length=100, help_text="à¦¯à§‡à¦®à¦¨: à¦ªà§à¦°à¦­à¦¾à¦·à¦•, à¦¸à¦¹à¦•à¦¾à¦°à§€ à¦…à¦§à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦ªà¦•")
    department = models.CharField(max_length=100, help_text="à¦¯à§‡à¦®à¦¨: à¦°à¦¸à¦¾à§Ÿà¦¨, à¦ªà¦¦à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à¦¬à¦¿à¦œà§à¦žà¦¾à¦¨, à¦¬à¦¾à¦‚à¦²à¦¾")
    qualification = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True)
    joining_date = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True)
    
    # à¦¸à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦²à¦¾à¦°à¦¿ à¦°à¦¿à¦•à§‹à§Ÿà§‡à¦¸à§à¦Ÿ à¦«à§à¦²à§‹-à¦à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦•à§‡à¦° à¦¨à¦¿à¦°à§à¦§à¦¾à¦°à¦¿à¦¤ à¦®à¦¾à¦¸à¦¿à¦• à¦¬à§‡à¦¤à¦¨
    base_salary = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    
    # à¦ªà¦¾à¦¬à¦²à¦¿à¦• à¦“à§Ÿà§‡à¦¬à¦¸à¦¾à¦‡à¦Ÿà§‡à¦° à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦šà¦¾à¦° à¦ªà§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¨à§‡à¦² à¦ªà§‡à¦œà§‡ à¦¦à§‡à¦–à¦¾à¦¨à§‹à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯
    bio = models.TextField(blank=True)
    show_on_website = models.BooleanField(default=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Teacher Profile'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Teacher Profiles'
        ordering = ['department', 'user__first_name']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.user.get_full_name() or self.user.username} ({self.department})"


class StudentProfile(BaseModel):
    """
    à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦° à¦à¦•à¦¾à¦¡à§‡à¦®à¦¿à¦• à¦ªà§à¦°à§‹à¦«à¦¾à¦‡à¦², à¦‡à¦‰à¦¨à¦¿à¦• à¦†à¦‡à¦¡à¦¿, à¦°à§‹à¦², à¦¸à§‡à¦¶à¦¨ à¦à¦¬à¦‚ à¦®à¦¾à¦¸à¦¿à¦• à¦«à¦¿ à¦¸à§‡à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦‚à¦¸à¥¤
    
    """
    class GenderChoices(models.TextChoices):
        MALE = 'MALE', 'à¦›à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦° (Male)'
        FEMALE = 'FEMALE', 'à¦›à¦¾à¦¤à§à¦°à§€ (Female)'
    
    user = models.OneToOneField(User, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='student_profile')
    student_unique_id = models.CharField(max_length=35, unique=True, editable=False, db_index=True)
    gender = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=GenderChoices.choices, default=GenderChoices.MALE, db_index=True)
    section = models.ForeignKey('academics.AcademicSection', on_delete=models.PROTECT, related_name='students')
    roll_number = models.PositiveIntegerField(db_index=True)
    session = models.CharField(max_length=20, db_index=True, help_text="à¦¯à§‡à¦®à¦¨: 2025-2026")

    
    # à¦¬à§‹à¦°à§à¦¡ à¦°à§‡à¦œà¦¿à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§à¦°à§‡à¦¶à¦¨ à¦¤à¦¥à§à¦¯ (à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦²à§à¦®à¦¨à¦¿ à¦“ à¦ªà¦°à§€à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯)
    ssc_roll = models.CharField(max_length=30, blank=True, null=True)
    passing_year = models.CharField(max_length=4, blank=True, default='', db_index=True)
    temporary_pin_token = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True, default='')
    hsc_registration_no = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True, null=True, db_index=True)
    
    # à¦…à¦­à¦¿à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à¦•à§‡à¦° à¦¤à¦¥à§à¦¯ (SMS à¦¨à§‹à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦«à¦¿à¦•à§‡à¦¶à¦¨à§‡à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯)
    father_name = models.CharField(max_length=150)
    mother_name = models.CharField(max_length=150)
    guardian_phone = models.CharField(max_length=20, db_index=True)
    guardian_whatsapp_number = models.CharField(
        max_length=20,
        blank=True,
        default='',
        help_text="à¦…à¦­à¦¿à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à¦•à§‡à¦° WhatsApp à¦¨à¦®à§à¦¬à¦°",
    )
    student_whatsapp_number = models.CharField(
        max_length=20,
        blank=True,
        default='',
        help_text="à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€à¦° WhatsApp à¦¨à¦®à§à¦¬à¦°",
    )
    present_address = models.TextField(blank=True)
    
    # à¦«à¦¿ à¦“ à¦ªà¦°à¦¿à¦¬à¦¹à¦¨ à¦•à¦¨à¦«à¦¿à¦—à¦¾à¦°à§‡à¦¶à¦¨ (à¦¶à§à¦°à§‡à¦£à¦¿ à¦‡à¦¨à¦šà¦¾à¦°à§à¦œ à¦¯à¦¾ à¦¹à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¨à§à¦¡à§‡à¦² à¦•à¦°à¦¬à§‡à¦¨)
    monthly_tuition_fee = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    uses_transport = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    assigned_vehicle = models.ForeignKey(
        'finance.TransportVehicle',
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name='passengers',
        help_text="à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€ à¦¯à§‡ à¦¨à¦¿à¦°à§à¦¦à¦¿à¦·à§à¦Ÿ à¦—à¦¾à§œà¦¿à¦¤à§‡ à¦¯à¦¾à¦¤à¦¾à§Ÿà¦¾à¦¤ à¦•à¦°à§‡"
    )
    
    monthly_transport_fee = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    
    created_by = models.ForeignKey(
        User,
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name='enrolled_students'
    )

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Student Profile'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Student Profiles'
        unique_together = ('section', 'roll_number')
        constraints = [
            models.UniqueConstraint(
                fields=['ssc_roll', 'passing_year'],
                name='unique_student_ssc_roll_passing_year',
            ),
        ]
        ordering = ['section', 'roll_number']
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=['section', 'roll_number']),
            models.Index(fields=['section', 'session']),
        ]

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        # à¦¸à§‡à¦¶à¦¨ à¦¯à¦¦à¦¿ à¦–à¦¾à¦²à¦¿ à¦¥à¦¾à¦•à§‡ à¦¤à¦¬à§‡ à¦¸à§‡à¦•à¦¶à¦¨à§‡à¦° à¦¸à§‡à¦¶à¦¨ à¦…à¦Ÿà§‹ à¦¬à¦¸à§‡ à¦¯à¦¾à¦¬à§‡
        if not self.session and self.section:
            self.session = self.section.session

        # à¦…à¦Ÿà§‹à¦®à§‡à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦• à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§à¦¡à§‡à¦¨à§à¦Ÿ à¦‡à¦‰à¦¨à¦¿à¦• à¦†à¦‡à¦¡à¦¿ à¦œà§‡à¦¨à¦¾à¦°à§‡à¦¶à¦¨: [Year]-[Class]-[Group]-[Section]-[Roll]
        if not self.student_unique_id and self.section:
            year_prefix = self.session.split('-')[0].strip()
            self.student_unique_id = (
                f"{year_prefix}-{self.section.class_level}-"
                f"{self.section.group}-{self.section.section_name}-"
                f"{int(self.roll_number):04d}"
            ).upper()
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def set_temporary_pin(self, pin):
        self.temporary_pin_token = signing.dumps({'pin': pin}, compress=True)

    def get_temporary_pin(self):
        if not self.temporary_pin_token:
            return ''
        try:
            return signing.loads(self.temporary_pin_token)['pin']
        except signing.BadSignature:
            return ''

    def __str__(self):
        return f"[{self.student_unique_id}] Roll: {self.roll_number} - {self.user.get_full_name()}"

from django.db import transaction
from rest_framework import serializers
from rest_framework_simplejwt.serializers import TokenObtainPairSerializer
from apps.users_auth.models import StudentProfile, TeacherProfile, User
from apps.common.validators import validate_upload


class TeacherProfileSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    class Meta:
        model = TeacherProfile
        fields = [
            'employee_id',
            'designation',
            'department',
            'qualification',
            'joining_date',
            'base_salary',
            'bio',
            'show_on_website',
        ]


class StudentProfileMinimalSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    section_details = serializers.SerializerMethodField()

    class Meta:
        model = StudentProfile
        fields = [
            'student_unique_id',
            'roll_number',
            'session',
            'section',
            'section_details',
            'ssc_roll',
            'passing_year',
            'hsc_registration_no',
            'father_name',
            'mother_name',
            'guardian_phone',
            'guardian_whatsapp_number',
            'student_whatsapp_number',
            'present_address',
            'monthly_tuition_fee',
            'uses_transport',
            'monthly_transport_fee',
        ]

    def get_section_details(self, obj) -> dict:
        return {
            "id": str(obj.section.id),
            "class_level": obj.section.class_level,
            "class_level_display": obj.section.get_class_level_display(),
            "group": obj.section.group,
            "group_display": obj.section.get_group_display(),
            "section_name": obj.section.section_name,
            "session": obj.section.session,
        }


class UserMinimalSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    """
    à¦‡à¦‰à¦œà¦¾à¦°à§‡à¦° à¦ªà§à¦°à§‹à¦«à¦¾à¦‡à¦², à¦°à§‹à¦², à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦• à¦ªà§à¦°à§‹à¦«à¦¾à¦‡à¦² à¦à¦¬à¦‚ à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§à¦¡à§‡à¦¨à§à¦Ÿ à¦ªà§à¦°à§‹à¦«à¦¾à¦‡à¦² à¦¤à¦¥à§à¦¯à¥¤
    """
    full_name = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    role_display = serializers.CharField(source='get_role_display', read_only=True)
    is_class_incharge = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    incharge_sections = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    teacher_profile = TeacherProfileSerializer(read_only=True)
    student_profile = StudentProfileMinimalSerializer(read_only=True)

    class Meta:
        model = User
        fields = [
            'id',
            'username',
            'first_name',
            'last_name',
            'full_name',
            'email',
            'phone',
            'role',
            'role_display',
            'profile_photo',
            'is_library_manager',
            'is_class_incharge',
            'incharge_sections',
            'teacher_profile',
            'student_profile',
            'is_active',
        ]
        read_only_fields = ['id', 'username', 'role', 'is_library_manager', 'is_active']

    def get_full_name(self, obj) -> str:
        full_name = f"{obj.first_name} {obj.last_name}".strip()
        return full_name if full_name else obj.username

    def validate_profile_photo(self, value):
        return validate_upload(value, {'.png', '.jpg', '.jpeg', '.webp'}, max_size_mb=5)

    def get_is_class_incharge(self, obj) -> bool:
        if obj.role == User.Role.TEACHER:
            return obj.incharge_sections.filter(is_active=True).exists()
        return False

    def get_incharge_sections(self, obj) -> list:
        if obj.role == User.Role.TEACHER:
            sections = obj.incharge_sections.filter(is_active=True)
            return [
                {
                    "id": str(sec.id),
                    "class_level": sec.class_level,
                    "class_level_display": sec.get_class_level_display(),
                    "group": sec.group,
                    "group_display": sec.get_group_display(),
                    "section_name": sec.section_name,
                    "session": sec.session,
                    "total_students": sec.students.filter(user__is_active=True).count(),
                }
                for sec in sections
            ]
        return []


class CustomTokenObtainPairSerializer(TokenObtainPairSerializer):
    username = serializers.CharField(required=False, allow_blank=True)
    password = serializers.CharField(required=False, allow_blank=True, write_only=True)
    ssc_roll = serializers.CharField(required=False, allow_blank=True)
    passing_year = serializers.CharField(required=False, allow_blank=True)
    pin = serializers.CharField(required=False, allow_blank=True, write_only=True)

    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
        self.fields[self.username_field].required = False
        self.fields['password'].required = False

    @classmethod
    def get_token(cls, user):
        token = super().get_token(user)
        token['username'] = user.username
        token['role'] = user.role
        token['is_library_manager'] = user.is_library_manager
        return token

    def validate(self, attrs):
        student_login = any(attrs.get(field) for field in ('ssc_roll', 'passing_year', 'pin'))
        if student_login:
            required_fields = ('ssc_roll', 'passing_year', 'pin')
            missing_fields = [field for field in required_fields if not attrs.get(field)]
            if missing_fields:
                raise serializers.ValidationError({
                    field: "à¦à¦‡ à¦¤à¦¥à§à¦¯à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¦à¦¾à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦†à¦¬à¦¶à§à¦¯à¦•à¥¤" for field in missing_fields
                })

            profile = StudentProfile.objects.select_related('user').filter(
                ssc_roll=attrs['ssc_roll'].strip(),
                passing_year=attrs['passing_year'].strip(),
            ).first()
            if not profile or not profile.user.check_password(attrs['pin']):
                raise serializers.ValidationError("SSC Roll, Passing Year à¦…à¦¥à¦¬à¦¾ PIN à¦¸à¦ à¦¿à¦• à¦¨à§Ÿà¥¤")
            self.user = profile.user
            data = {}
            refresh = self.get_token(self.user)
            data['refresh'] = str(refresh)
            data['access'] = str(refresh.access_token)
        else:
            if not attrs.get('username') or not attrs.get('password'):
                raise serializers.ValidationError({
                    'detail': "Username à¦à¦¬à¦‚ Password à¦…à¦¥à¦¬à¦¾ SSC Roll, Passing Year à¦à¦¬à¦‚ PIN à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¦à¦¾à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à§à¦¨à¥¤"
                })
            data = super().validate({
                'username': attrs['username'],
                'password': attrs['password'],
            })

        if not self.user.is_active:
            raise serializers.ValidationError("à¦†à¦ªà¦¨à¦¾à¦° à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦•à¦¾à¦‰à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦¬à¦°à§à¦¤à¦®à¦¾à¦¨à§‡ à¦¨à¦¿à¦·à§à¦•à§à¦°à¦¿à§Ÿ (Deactivated) à¦°à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤")
        data['user'] = UserMinimalSerializer(self.user, context=self.context).data
        return data


class StaffCreateSerializer(serializers.Serializer):
    """
    à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦¸à¦¿à¦ªà¦¾à¦² à¦•à¦°à§à¦¤à§ƒà¦• à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦• (TEACHER) à¦…à¦¥à¦¬à¦¾ à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦•à¦¾à¦‰à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¨à§à¦Ÿ (ACCOUNTANT) à¦¤à§ˆà¦°à¦¿à¦° à¦¸à¦¿à¦°à¦¿à§Ÿà¦¾à¦²à¦¾à¦‡à¦œà¦¾à¦°à¥¤
    """
    username = serializers.CharField(max_length=150)
    password = serializers.CharField(write_only=True, min_length=6)
    first_name = serializers.CharField(max_length=150)
    last_name = serializers.CharField(max_length=150, required=False, allow_blank=True)
    email = serializers.EmailField(required=False, allow_blank=True)
    phone = serializers.CharField(max_length=20)
    role = serializers.ChoiceField(choices=[
        (User.Role.TEACHER, 'Teacher'),
        (User.Role.ACCOUNTANT, 'Accountant'),
    ])
    is_library_manager = serializers.BooleanField(default=False)

    employee_id = serializers.CharField(max_length=30, required=False)
    designation = serializers.CharField(max_length=100, required=False)
    department = serializers.CharField(max_length=100, required=False)
    qualification = serializers.CharField(max_length=255, required=False, allow_blank=True)
    joining_date = serializers.DateField(required=False, allow_null=True)
    base_salary = serializers.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    bio = serializers.CharField(required=False, allow_blank=True)

    def validate_username(self, value):
        if User.objects.filter(username=value).exists():
            raise serializers.ValidationError("à¦à¦‡ à¦‡à¦‰à¦œà¦¾à¦°à¦¨à§‡à¦® à¦¦à¦¿à§Ÿà§‡ à¦‡à¦¤à¦¿à¦®à¦§à§à¦¯à§‡ à¦à¦•à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦•à¦¾à¦‰à¦¨à§à¦Ÿ à¦°à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤")
        return value

    def validate_phone(self, value):
        if User.objects.filter(phone=value).exists():
            raise serializers.ValidationError("à¦à¦‡ à¦«à§‹à¦¨ à¦¨à¦®à§à¦¬à¦°à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦‡à¦¤à¦¿à¦®à¦§à§à¦¯à§‡ à¦¬à§à¦¯à¦¬à¦¹à§ƒà¦¤ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤")
        return value

    def validate(self, attrs):
        if attrs.get('role') == User.Role.TEACHER:
            if not attrs.get('employee_id'):
                raise serializers.ValidationError({"employee_id": "à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦•à§‡à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ Employee ID à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¦à¦¾à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦†à¦¬à¦¶à§à¦¯à¦•à¥¤"})
            if TeacherProfile.objects.filter(employee_id=attrs['employee_id']).exists():
                raise serializers.ValidationError({"employee_id": "à¦à¦‡ Employee ID à¦‡à¦¤à¦¿à¦®à¦§à§à¦¯à§‡ à¦…à¦¨à§à¦¯ à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦•à§‡à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ à¦¬à§à¦¯à¦¬à¦¹à§ƒà¦¤ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤"})
            if not attrs.get('designation') or not attrs.get('department'):
                raise serializers.ValidationError("à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦•à§‡à¦° à¦ªà¦¦à¦¬à¦¿ (designation) à¦à¦¬à¦‚ à¦¬à¦¿à¦­à¦¾à¦— (department) à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¦à¦¾à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦†à¦¬à¦¶à§à¦¯à¦•à¥¤")
        return attrs

    @transaction.atomic
    def create(self, validated_data):
        role = validated_data['role']
        user = User.objects.create_user(
            username=validated_data['username'],
            password=validated_data['password'],
            first_name=validated_data['first_name'],
            last_name=validated_data.get('last_name', ''),
            email=validated_data.get('email', ''),
            phone=validated_data['phone'],
            role=role,
            is_library_manager=validated_data.get('is_library_manager', False),
        )

        if role == User.Role.TEACHER:
            TeacherProfile.objects.create(
                user=user,
                employee_id=validated_data['employee_id'],
                designation=validated_data['designation'],
                department=validated_data['department'],
                qualification=validated_data.get('qualification', ''),
                joining_date=validated_data.get('joining_date'),
                base_salary=validated_data.get('base_salary', 0.00),
                bio=validated_data.get('bio', ''),
            )
        return user

from django.urls import path
from rest_framework_simplejwt.views import TokenRefreshView
from apps.users_auth.views import (
    ChangePasswordView,
    CurrentUserProfileView,
    CustomLoginView,
    LogoutView,
    StaffDetailView,
    StaffListCreateView,
)

app_name = 'users_auth'

urlpatterns = [
    path('login/', CustomLoginView.as_view(), name='login'),
    path('token/refresh/', TokenRefreshView.as_view(), name='token_refresh'),
    path('me/', CurrentUserProfileView.as_view(), name='current_user'),
    path('change-password/', ChangePasswordView.as_view(), name='change_password'),
    path('logout/', LogoutView.as_view(), name='logout'),
    
    # Principal creates, lists, views & updates Teachers/Accountants
    path('staff/', StaffListCreateView.as_view(), name='staff_list_create'),
    path('staff/<uuid:pk>/', StaffDetailView.as_view(), name='staff_detail'),
]

from rest_framework import generics, status
from rest_framework.permissions import AllowAny, IsAuthenticated
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.views import APIView
from rest_framework_simplejwt.tokens import RefreshToken
from rest_framework_simplejwt.views import TokenObtainPairView

from apps.common.permissions import IsPrincipal
from apps.users_auth.models import User
from apps.users_auth.serializers import (
    CustomTokenObtainPairSerializer,
    StaffCreateSerializer,
    UserMinimalSerializer,
)


class CustomLoginView(TokenObtainPairView):
    """
    POST /api/v1/auth/login/
    à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¥à§€ (SSC Roll, Passing Year, PIN) à¦…à¦¥à¦¬à¦¾ à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦« (Username, Password) à¦‰à¦­à§Ÿà§‡à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯ à¦•à§‡à¦¨à§à¦¦à§à¦°à§€à§Ÿ à¦²à¦—à¦‡à¦¨à¥¤
    """
    authentication_classes = []
    permission_classes = [AllowAny]
    serializer_class = CustomTokenObtainPairSerializer


class CurrentUserProfileView(APIView):
    """
    GET / PATCH: /api/v1/auth/me/
    à¦²à¦—à¦‡à¦¨ à¦¥à¦¾à¦•à¦¾ à¦‡à¦‰à¦œà¦¾à¦°à§‡à¦° à¦ªà§à¦°à§‹à¦«à¦¾à¦‡à¦² à¦¤à¦¥à§à¦¯ à¦¦à§‡à¦–à¦¾ à¦à¦¬à¦‚ à¦¨à¦¿à¦œà¦¸à§à¦¬ à¦ªà§à¦°à§‹à¦«à¦¾à¦‡à¦² (à¦¨à¦¾à¦®, à¦«à§‹à¦¨, à¦‡à¦®à§‡à¦‡à¦², à¦›à¦¬à¦¿) à¦†à¦ªà¦¡à§‡à¦Ÿ à¦•à¦°à¦¾à¥¤
    """
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def get(self, request):
        serializer = UserMinimalSerializer(request.user, context={'request': request})
        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "data": serializer.data
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    def patch(self, request):
        serializer = UserMinimalSerializer(
            request.user, 
            data=request.data, 
            partial=True, 
            context={'request': request}
        )
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)
        serializer.save()
        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": "à¦ªà§à¦°à§‹à¦«à¦¾à¦‡à¦² à¦¤à¦¥à§à¦¯ à¦¸à¦«à¦²à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ à¦†à¦ªà¦¡à§‡à¦Ÿ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤",
            "data": serializer.data
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class ChangePasswordView(APIView):
    """
    POST /api/v1/auth/change-password/
    à¦²à¦—à¦‡à¦¨ à¦¥à¦¾à¦•à¦¾ à¦¯à§‡à¦•à§‹à¦¨à§‹ à¦‡à¦‰à¦œà¦¾à¦°à§‡à¦° à¦¨à¦¿à¦œà¦¸à§à¦¬ à¦ªà¦¾à¦¸à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾à¦°à§à¦¡ à¦ªà¦°à¦¿à¦¬à¦°à§à¦¤à¦¨à¥¤
    """
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def post(self, request):
        old_password = request.data.get('old_password')
        new_password = request.data.get('new_password')

        if not old_password or not new_password:
            return Response(
                {"success": False, "message": "à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¾à¦¤à¦¨ à¦“ à¦¨à¦¤à§à¦¨ à¦ªà¦¾à¦¸à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾à¦°à§à¦¡ à¦‰à¦­à§Ÿà¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¦à¦¾à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦†à¦¬à¦¶à§à¦¯à¦•à¥¤"},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST
            )

        if not request.user.check_password(old_password):
            return Response(
                {"success": False, "message": "à¦¬à¦°à§à¦¤à¦®à¦¾à¦¨ à¦ªà¦¾à¦¸à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾à¦°à§à¦¡à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦¸à¦ à¦¿à¦• à¦¨à§Ÿà¥¤"},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST
            )

        if len(new_password) < 6:
            return Response(
                {"success": False, "message": "à¦¨à¦¤à§à¦¨ à¦ªà¦¾à¦¸à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾à¦°à§à¦¡ à¦¨à§à¦¯à§‚à¦¨à¦¤à¦® à§¬ à¦…à¦•à§à¦·à¦°à§‡à¦° à¦¹à¦¤à§‡ à¦¹à¦¬à§‡à¥¤"},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST
            )

        request.user.set_password(new_password)
        request.user.save()
        return Response(
            {"success": True, "message": "à¦ªà¦¾à¦¸à¦“à§Ÿà¦¾à¦°à§à¦¡ à¦¸à¦«à¦²à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ à¦ªà¦°à¦¿à¦¬à¦°à§à¦¤à¦¿à¦¤ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤"},
            status=status.HTTP_200_OK
        )


class LogoutView(APIView):
    """
    POST /api/v1/auth/logout/
    à¦°à¦¿à¦«à§à¦°à§‡à¦¶ à¦Ÿà§‹à¦•à§‡à¦¨ à¦¬à§à¦²à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦•à¦²à¦¿à¦¸à§à¦Ÿ à¦•à¦°à§‡ à¦¨à¦¿à¦°à¦¾à¦ªà¦¦ à¦²à¦—à¦†à¦‰à¦Ÿ à¦¸à¦®à§à¦ªà¦¨à§à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à§‡à¥¤
    """
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def post(self, request):
        refresh_token = request.data.get("refresh")
        if not refresh_token:
            return Response(
                {"success": False, "message": "Refresh token à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¦à¦¾à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦†à¦¬à¦¶à§à¦¯à¦•à¥¤"},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST
            )
        try:
            token = RefreshToken(refresh_token)
            token.blacklist()
            return Response(
                {"success": True, "message": "à¦¸à¦«à¦²à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ à¦²à¦—à¦†à¦‰à¦Ÿ à¦¸à¦®à§à¦ªà¦¨à§à¦¨ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤"},
                status=status.HTTP_200_OK
            )
        except Exception:
            return Response(
                {"success": False, "message": "à¦Ÿà§‹à¦•à§‡à¦¨à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦¸à¦ à¦¿à¦• à¦¨à§Ÿ à¦…à¦¥à¦¬à¦¾ à¦®à§‡à§Ÿà¦¾à¦¦ à¦¶à§‡à¦· à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡ à¦—à§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤"},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST
            )


class StaffListCreateView(generics.ListCreateAPIView):
    """
    GET / POST: /api/v1/auth/staff/
    à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¿à¦¨à§à¦¸à¦¿à¦ªà¦¾à¦² à¦•à¦°à§à¦¤à§ƒà¦• à¦¶à¦¿à¦•à§à¦·à¦• à¦“ à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦•à¦¾à¦‰à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¨à§à¦Ÿ à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦•à¦¾à¦‰à¦¨à§à¦Ÿ à¦¤à§ˆà¦°à¦¿ à¦à¦¬à¦‚ à¦¤à¦¾à¦²à¦¿à¦•à¦¾ à¦¦à§‡à¦–à¦¾à¦° à¦à¦¨à§à¦¡à¦ªà§Ÿà§‡à¦¨à§à¦Ÿà¥¤
    """
    permission_classes = [IsPrincipal]

    def get_queryset(self):
        queryset = User.objects.filter(
            role__in=[User.Role.TEACHER, User.Role.ACCOUNTANT]
        ).select_related('teacher_profile').prefetch_related('incharge_sections')
        
        role_filter = self.request.query_params.get('role')
        if role_filter:
            queryset = queryset.filter(role=role_filter.upper())
        return queryset

    def get_serializer_class(self):
        if self.request.method == 'POST':
            return StaffCreateSerializer
        return UserMinimalSerializer

    def create(self, request, *args, **kwargs):
        serializer = self.get_serializer(data=request.data, context={'request': request})
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)
        user = serializer.save()
        return Response(
            {
                "success": True,
                "message": f"{user.get_role_display()} à¦…à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦•à¦¾à¦‰à¦¨à§à¦Ÿ à¦¸à¦«à¦²à¦­à¦¾à¦¬à§‡ à¦¤à§ˆà¦°à¦¿ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡à¥¤",
                "data": UserMinimalSerializer(user, context={'request': request}).data,
            },
            status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
        )


class StaffDetailView(generics.RetrieveUpdateDestroyAPIView):
    """
    GET / PUT / PATCH / DELETE: /api/v1/auth/staff/<uuid:pk>/
    à¦…à¦§à§à¦¯à¦•à§à¦· à¦•à¦°à§à¦¤à§ƒà¦• à¦¨à¦¿à¦°à§à¦¦à¦¿à¦·à§à¦Ÿ à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦«à§‡à¦° à¦ªà§à¦°à§‹à¦«à¦¾à¦‡à¦² à¦¦à§‡à¦–à¦¾, à¦¤à¦¥à§à¦¯ à¦†à¦ªà¦¡à§‡à¦Ÿ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦à¦¬à¦‚ à¦¨à¦¿à¦·à§à¦•à§à¦°à¦¿à§Ÿ (Deactivate) à¦•à¦°à¦¾à¥¤
    """
    permission_classes = [IsPrincipal]
    queryset = User.objects.filter(
        role__in=[User.Role.TEACHER, User.Role.ACCOUNTANT]
    ).select_related('teacher_profile').prefetch_related('incharge_sections')
    serializer_class = UserMinimalSerializer

    def perform_destroy(self, instance):
        instance.is_active = False
        instance.save(update_fields=['is_active'])

import multiprocessing
import os


bind = os.getenv('GUNICORN_BIND', '0.0.0.0:8000')
workers = int(os.getenv('GUNICORN_WORKERS', max(2, multiprocessing.cpu_count())))
threads = int(os.getenv('GUNICORN_THREADS', '2'))
timeout = int(os.getenv('GUNICORN_TIMEOUT', '120'))
graceful_timeout = int(os.getenv('GUNICORN_GRACEFUL_TIMEOUT', '30'))
keepalive = int(os.getenv('GUNICORN_KEEPALIVE', '5'))
accesslog = '-'
errorlog = '-'
capture_output = True
preload_app = True

#!/usr/bin/env python
"""Django's command-line utility for administrative tasks."""
import os
import sys


def main():
    """Run administrative tasks."""
    os.environ.setdefault('DJANGO_SETTINGS_MODULE', 'config.settings')
    try:
        from django.core.management import execute_from_command_line
    except ImportError as exc:
        raise ImportError(
            "Couldn't import Django. Are you sure it's installed and "
            "available on your PYTHONPATH environment variable? Did you "
            "forget to activate a virtual environment?"
        ) from exc
    execute_from_command_line(sys.argv)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
